单元 1 · 力学Unit 1 · Mechanics

AS 核心 · 物理里最吃数学的一块，学完它后面都轻松AS core · the most maths-heavy topic; the rest gets easier after this

本单元包括内容What this unit covers 今天要走 7 站7 stops today

  1. 运动学图像：s-t 图与 v-t 图Kinematics graphs: s-t and v-t

点标题可以直接跳到那一站。每站 = 一张图 + 一道例题 + 一道练习题（答案点开才看）。Click a title to jump there. Each stop = one diagram + one worked example + one practice question.

1 运动学图像：s-t 图与 v-t 图Kinematics graphs: s-t and v-t Kinematics运动学
学完你会：看懂一张运动图，能读出速度、加速度和位移。By the end: you can read velocity, acceleration and displacement straight off a motion graph.
说人话：两张图，看什么取决于你看的是斜率还是面积。s-t 图越陡表示走得越快（斜率就是速度）；v-t 图越陡表示加速越猛（斜率就是加速度），而它下面盖住的那块面积就是你走过的位移。这两个"斜率=什么、面积=什么"，是运动学的命根子。 In plain words: two graphs, and everything comes down to gradient or area. On an s-t graph, steeper means faster (the gradient is the velocity). On a v-t graph, steeper means harder acceleration (the gradient is the acceleration), and the area underneath is how far you travelled. "Gradient = what, area = what" is the whole game here.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • s-t 图：斜率 = 速度。水平线 = 站着不动。s-t graph: gradient = velocity. A flat line means standing still.
  • v-t 图：斜率 = 加速度；面积 = 位移。v-t graph: gradient = acceleration; area = displacement.
  • v-t 图是水平线 → 匀速；是斜直线 → 匀加速。A flat v-t line means constant velocity; a sloping straight line means constant acceleration.
  • 曲线下面积不会算？就当梯形算：½(上底+下底)×高。Not sure about the area? Treat it as a trapezium: ½(a + b) × height.

例题Example

一个物体从静止开始做匀加速直线运动，5 s 后速度达到 10 m/s。求：(a) 加速度；(b) 这 5 s 内走的位移。An object starts from rest and accelerates uniformly, reaching 10 m/s after 5 s. Find (a) the acceleration and (b) the displacement in those 5 s.
(a) v-t 图是一条从 0 升到 10、横着走 5 格的直线。斜率 = 加速度：(a) The v-t line goes from 0 up to 10 across 5 seconds. Gradient = acceleration: a = (10 − 0) / 5 = 2 m/s² (b) 位移 = v-t 图下的面积，这里是个三角形：(b) Displacement = area under the v-t graph, which here is a triangle: s = ½ × 5 × 10 = 25 m （梯形算也一样：½(0 + 10) × 5 = 25 m）(The trapezium rule gives the same: ½(0 + 10) × 5 = 25 m.)

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：初速度不是 0，所以面积是梯形不是三角形。Hint: the initial velocity is not 0, so the area is a trapezium, not a triangle.

一辆车以 4 m/s 的初速度匀加速行驶，8 s 后速度变为 16 m/s。求加速度和这 8 s 内的位移。A car starts at 4 m/s and accelerates uniformly to 16 m/s in 8 s. Find the acceleration and the displacement.
点开看答案Show answer
加速度 a = (16 − 4) / 8 = 12 / 8 = 1.5 m/s²Acceleration a = (16 − 4) / 8 = 12 / 8 = 1.5 m/s² 位移 = 梯形面积 = ½(4 + 16) × 8 = ½ × 20 × 8 = 80 mDisplacement = trapezium area = ½(4 + 16) × 8 = 80 m
— 第 1 站完成 —— Stop 1 done —
2 匀加速公式（SUVAT）The SUVAT equations SUVAT匀加速公式
学完你会：看到题目先列 u、v、a、s、t，选对公式一步算出来。By the end: you list u, v, a, s, t first, then pick the equation with only one unknown.
说人话：这五个字母，题目通常会给你三个，你要求的剩下一个。解题第一步永远是"列清单"：把已知的写下来，未知的打个问号，然后找一条公式，里面只有一个问号——就是它了。不用背四个公式谁是谁，看清单选就行。 In plain words: of these five letters, a question usually gives you three and asks for one more. Step one is always "write the list": jot down what you know, put a question mark by what you don't, then find the equation with only one question mark in it. No need to memorise which equation is which — let the list choose.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • v = u + at  （不含 s）(no s)
  • s = ut + ½at²  （不含 v）(no v)
  • v² = u² + 2as  （不含 t，最常被忘）(no t — the one people forget)
  • s = ½(u + v)t  （不含 a）(no a)
  • 方向要统一：先定一个正方向，反方向的量加负号。Fix one direction as positive first, and give anything pointing the other way a minus sign.

例题Example

一辆车以 10 m/s 行驶，以 2 m/s² 的加速度加速 6 s。求末速度和这段位移。A car travelling at 10 m/s accelerates at 2 m/s² for 6 s. Find its final velocity and the displacement.
列清单：u = 10，a = 2，t = 6，v = ?，s = ?List: u = 10, a = 2, t = 6, v = ?, s = ? 求 v：用 v = u + at（这条不含 s，正好）Find v using v = u + at (no s in it — perfect) v = 10 + 2 × 6 = 22 m/s 求 s：用 s = ut + ½at²Find s using s = ut + ½at² s = 10 × 6 + ½ × 2 × 36 = 60 + 36 = 96 m

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：没给时间 t，想想哪条公式里没有 t。Hint: no time is given — which equation does not contain t?

一颗子弹以 300 m/s 射入木板，受到 450000 m/s² 的减速度，穿入多深才停下？A bullet enters a block of wood at 300 m/s and decelerates at 450 000 m/s². How far does it travel before stopping?
点开看答案Show answer
清单：u = 300，v = 0（停下），a = −450000（减速要加负号），s = ?List: u = 300, v = 0 (it stops), a = −450 000 (deceleration needs a minus sign), s = ? 没给 t → 用 v² = u² + 2asNo t given → use v² = u² + 2as 0 = 300² + 2 × (−450000) × s 0 = 90000 − 900000 s s = 90000 / 900000 = 0.10 m （10 cm）(10 cm)
— 第 2 站完成 —— Stop 2 done —
3 自由落体与抛体运动Free fall and projectiles Projectile抛体
学完你会：把斜着扔的运动拆成"水平匀速 + 竖直匀加速"两半来算。By the end: you split any thrown motion into "constant horizontal + accelerating vertical".
说人话：扔出去的东西，水平方向没人管它（忽略空气阻力），所以一直匀速；竖直方向被重力拽着，所以是 a = g 的匀加速。两个方向互不干扰，唯一把它们连起来的东西是时间——水平飞多远和竖直掉多深，花的是同一段时间。所以先算竖直方向求时间，再去算水平位移。 In plain words: for a thrown object, nothing acts horizontally (ignore air resistance), so it keeps a constant horizontal velocity. Gravity pulls vertically, so vertically it accelerates at g. The two never interfere, and the only thing linking them is time — the horizontal distance and the vertical drop happen over the same seconds. So: work out the time from the vertical motion, then use it for the horizontal.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • 自由落体：u = 0，a = g = 9.8 m/s²，所有物体落地时间一样（不看质量）。Free fall: u = 0, a = g = 9.8 m/s². All objects take the same time regardless of mass.
  • 抛体：水平速度永远不变，竖直速度按 v = u + gt 变。Projectile: horizontal velocity never changes; vertical follows v = u + gt.
  • 往上抛到最高点：那一刻竖直速度为 0，但水平速度还在。At the top of the flight the vertical velocity is 0 — but the horizontal velocity is still there.
  • 落地时间只由高度决定，和水平扔多远无关。Time of flight depends only on the height, not on how hard you throw it sideways.

例题Example

从 45 m 高的楼顶水平扔出一块石头，初速度 20 m/s（取 g = 10 m/s²）。求落地时间和水平飞行距离。A stone is thrown horizontally at 20 m/s from a 45 m high building (take g = 10 m/s²). Find the time of flight and the horizontal distance.
先算竖直：uy = 0，s = 45，a = 10，求 tVertical first: uy = 0, s = 45, a = 10, find t 用 s = ut + ½at² → 45 = 0 + ½ × 10 × t² = 5t²Use s = ut + ½at² → 45 = 0 + ½ × 10 × t² = 5t² t² = 9 → t = 3.0 s 再算水平：速度恒为 20 m/s，飞了 3 sNow horizontal: velocity is constant at 20 m/s for 3 s sx = 20 × 3 = 60 m

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：竖直上抛到最高点时竖直速度为 0，a = −g（方向向下）。Hint: at the highest point the vertical velocity is 0, and a = −g (downwards).

一个球以 30 m/s 竖直向上抛出（取 g = 10 m/s²）。它能升到多高？多久回到出发点？A ball is thrown vertically upwards at 30 m/s (g = 10 m/s²). How high does it go, and how long until it returns to the start?
点开看答案Show answer
最高点：v = 0，u = 30，a = −10，s = ?Highest point: v = 0, u = 30, a = −10, s = ? v² = u² + 2as → 0 = 900 + 2(−10)s → 0 = 900 − 20s s = 45 m 上升时间：v = u + at → 0 = 30 − 10t → t = 3 sTime up: v = u + at → 0 = 30 − 10t → t = 3 s 回到原点 = 上升 3 s + 下降 3 s = 6 s（上下对称）Back to the start = 3 s up + 3 s down = 6 s (symmetrical)
— 第 3 站完成 —— Stop 3 done —
4 牛顿三定律Newton's laws of motion Newton's laws牛顿定律
学完你会：用 F = ma 算加速度，并能用英文解释牛顿第三定律。By the end: you can use F = ma, and explain Newton's third law in English.
说人话：三条定律其实就三句话。第一定律：没人推你，你就保持原样（不动的继续不动，动的继续匀速）。第二定律：F = ma，力越大、质量越小，加速越猛——注意这里的 F 是合力，要把所有力加起来（反方向的减掉）。第三定律：你推墙，墙也推你，两个力一样大、方向相反。 In plain words: the three laws are three sentences. First law: if nothing pushes you, you keep doing what you were doing (still stays still, moving keeps moving at constant velocity). Second law: F = ma — bigger force or smaller mass means harder acceleration, and the F here is the resultant force, so you must add all the forces up (subtracting any that point the other way). Third law: if you push a wall, the wall pushes you back with an equal and opposite force.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • F = ma ：F 是合力（单位 N），m 是质量（kg），a 是加速度（m/s²）。: F is the resultant force (N), m the mass (kg), a the acceleration (m/s²).
  • 重量 W = mg，和质量不是一回事（质量到哪儿都一样，重量会变）。Weight W = mg, which is not the same as mass (mass is the same everywhere; weight changes).
  • 第三定律的两个力：大小相等、方向相反、作用在不同物体上。Third-law pair: equal in size, opposite in direction, acting on different objects.
🗣 考场英文怎么说（第三定律，几乎每年都考）：🗣 How to say it in the exam (third law — it comes up almost every year): The forces are equal in magnitude and opposite in direction, and they act on different bodies. 注意：一定要说 act on different bodies（作用在不同物体上），只说"相等相反"会扣分——因为作用在同一物体上的相等相反的力叫平衡力，不是第三定律。 Note: you must say act on different bodies. Saying only "equal and opposite" loses marks — two equal and opposite forces on the same body are balanced forces, not a third-law pair.

例题Example

一个 10 kg 的箱子放在水平地面上，受到 60 N 的水平拉力，摩擦力为 20 N。求加速度。A 10 kg box on horizontal ground is pulled with a force of 60 N against 20 N of friction. Find its acceleration.
先求合力：两个力方向相反，相减Resultant first: the two forces oppose each other, so subtract F = 60 − 20 = 40 N 再套 F = ma → a = F / mThen F = ma → a = F / m a = 40 / 10 = 4 m/s²

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：升降机在加速上升时，合力向上，所以支持力要大于重力。Hint: when a lift accelerates upwards, the resultant force is upwards, so the normal force must exceed the weight.

一个 60 kg 的人站在升降机里，升降机以 2 m/s² 向上加速（取 g = 10 m/s²）。求地板给他的支持力。A 60 kg person stands in a lift accelerating upwards at 2 m/s² (g = 10 m/s²). Find the normal force from the floor.
点开看答案Show answer
两个力：向上支持力 R，向下重力 W = mg = 600 NTwo forces: normal force R upwards, weight W = mg = 600 N downwards 合力向上：F = R − 600Resultant is upwards: F = R − 600 F = ma → R − 600 = 60 × 2 = 120F = ma → R − 600 = 60 × 2 = 120 R = 720 N （比体重 600 N 大，所以你会觉得"变重了"）(more than the 600 N weight — that is why you feel heavier)
— 第 4 站完成 —— Stop 4 done —
5 动量与碰撞Momentum and collisions Momentum动量
学完你会：用「碰前总动量 = 碰后总动量」解碰撞题。By the end: you solve collisions using "total momentum before = total momentum after".
说人话：动量 p = mv，可以理解成"这东西有多难停下来"——大卡车慢慢开也很难拦住。只要没有外力，碰撞前后的总动量一点都不会少。注意：动量有方向，向左的动量要带负号，这是最容易错的地方。 In plain words: momentum p = mv, which you can think of as "how hard is this thing to stop" — a lorry moving slowly is still hard to stop. As long as there is no external force, the total momentum before and after a collision is exactly the same. Note that momentum has a direction, so momentum to the left gets a minus sign — that is the classic mistake.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • p = mv ，单位 kg·m/s（或 N·s）。, unit kg·m/s (or N·s).
  • 动量守恒：m₁u₁ + m₂u₂ = m₁v₁ + m₂v₂Conservation: m₁u₁ + m₂u₂ = m₁v₁ + m₂v₂
  • 冲量 = 力 × 时间 = 动量的变化量（Ft = mv − mu）。Impulse = force × time = the change in momentum (Ft = mv − mu).
  • 三种碰撞：弹性（动量 + 动能都守恒）、非弹性（动量守恒，动能损失）、完全非弹性（撞完粘一起）。Three types: elastic (momentum and kinetic energy both conserved), inelastic (momentum conserved, energy lost), perfectly inelastic (they stick together).

例题Example

一个 2 kg 的物体以 3 m/s 撞上静止的 4 kg 物体，撞后粘在一起。求共同速度。A 2 kg object moving at 3 m/s hits a stationary 4 kg object and they stick together. Find their common velocity.
碰前总动量：2 × 3 + 4 × 0 = 6 kg·m/sTotal momentum before: 2 × 3 + 4 × 0 = 6 kg·m/s 碰后总动量：粘一起变成 6 kg，速度 v → 6 × vTotal momentum after: they form a 6 kg mass moving at v → 6 × v 6 = 6v → v = 1 m/s 方向：和原来 2 kg 物体的运动方向相同。Direction: the same as the original 2 kg object was moving.

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：方向相反 → 一个速度取负值。先定"向右为正"。Hint: opposite directions → one velocity must be negative. Take "to the right" as positive.

3 kg 的小车以 4 m/s 向右运动，撞上以 2 m/s 向左运动的 2 kg 小车，撞后粘在一起。求共同速度和方向。A 3 kg trolley moves right at 4 m/s and collides with a 2 kg trolley moving left at 2 m/s. They stick together. Find their common velocity and direction.
点开看答案Show answer
向右为正：u₁ = +4，u₂ = −2Taking right as positive: u₁ = +4, u₂ = −2 碰前：3 × 4 + 2 × (−2) = 12 − 4 = 8 kg·m/sBefore: 3 × 4 + 2 × (−2) = 12 − 4 = 8 kg·m/s 碰后：总质量 5 kg → 5v = 8After: total mass 5 kg → 5v = 8 v = 1.6 m/s，正数 → 向右, positive → to the right
— 第 5 站完成 —— Stop 5 done —
6 功、能量与功率Work, energy and power Work & energy功与能
学完你会：算功、动能、势能，并用能量守恒解"滑下来有多快"这类题。By the end: you can calculate work, kinetic and potential energy, and use conservation of energy for "how fast at the bottom" questions.
说人话：功 = 力 × 沿这个力方向移动的距离（推墙推不动，物理上做功为 0）。能量是"能干多少活"，动能是动着的能量，势能是"位置高"存着的能量。能量守恒就是说：高处存的那份，滑下来就变成了速度；如果有摩擦，就有一部分变成了热，不能算丢。 In plain words: work = force × distance moved along that force (push a wall and it doesn't move, so you have done zero work). Energy is "how much work you could do". Kinetic energy is the energy of moving; potential energy is energy stored by being high up. Conservation of energy means: what is stored up high turns into speed on the way down; if there is friction, some becomes heat and must not be forgotten.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • W = Fs cos θ ，θ 是力与位移的夹角（同向时 cos θ = 1）。, where θ is the angle between the force and the displacement (cos θ = 1 when they are parallel).
  • Ek = ½mv²  ·  Ep = mgh
  • P = W/t = Fv ，单位 W（瓦）。, unit W (watts).
  • 效率 = 有用的输出 / 总输入 × 100%。Efficiency = useful output / total input × 100%.

例题Example

把 50 kg 的箱子匀速举高 4 m（取 g = 9.8 m/s²）。求：(a) 克服重力做的功；(b) 用了 5 s，功率多大？A 50 kg box is lifted steadily through 4 m (g = 9.8 m/s²). Find (a) the work done against gravity and (b) the power, if it takes 5 s.
(a) 匀速 → 举力 = 重力 = mg = 50 × 9.8 = 490 N(a) Steady speed → lifting force = weight = mg = 50 × 9.8 = 490 N W = Fs = 490 × 4 = 1960 J (b) P = W / t = 1960 / 5 = 392 W(b) P = W / t = 1960 / 5 = 392 W

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：没有摩擦，势能全变成动能，m 会约掉。Hint: with no friction, all the potential energy becomes kinetic — and m cancels out.

一个物体从 20 m 高处由静止自由滑下（无摩擦，取 g = 10 m/s²）。求到达底部时的速度。An object slides from rest down a frictionless slope from a height of 20 m (g = 10 m/s²). Find its speed at the bottom.
点开看答案Show answer
能量守恒：mgh = ½mv²Conservation: mgh = ½mv² 两边 m 约掉：gh = ½v² → v² = 2ghm cancels: gh = ½v² → v² = 2gh v² = 2 × 10 × 20 = 400 v = 20 m/s
— 第 6 站完成 —— Stop 6 done —
7 受力分析与斜面Force diagrams and inclined planes Inclined plane斜面
学完你会：画受力图、分解力，算出斜面上的加速度。By the end: you can draw a force diagram, resolve forces, and find the acceleration on a slope.
说人话：斜面题的万能招是把重力劈成两半：一份沿着斜面（mg sinθ，让它往下滑），一份垂直压在斜面上（mg cosθ，被斜面顶住）。光滑斜面上只有沿斜面那一份在推它，所以 ma = mg sinθ，质量约掉了，加速度 a = g sinθ——不管是铁球还是木块，滑下来一样快。 In plain words: the universal move is to split the weight in two: one part along the slope (mg sinθ, which makes it slide) and one part pressing into the slope (mg cosθ, which the surface pushes back on). On a smooth slope only the along-the-slope part pulls it, so ma = mg sinθ. The mass cancels, giving a = g sinθ — an iron ball and a wooden block slide down at the same rate.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • 受力图五步：选物体 → 画重力 → 画接触力（支持力/摩擦垂直或平行接触面）→ 画绳的拉力 → 检查合力。Five steps for a force diagram: pick the body → draw weight → draw contact forces (normal and friction) → draw any tension → check the resultant.
  • 沿斜面：mg sinθ；垂直斜面：mg cosθ（θ 是斜面与水平面的夹角）。Along: mg sinθ; into the slope: mg cosθ (θ is the angle to the horizontal).
  • 光滑斜面 a = g sinθ；有摩擦再减去 μ g cosθ。Smooth slope: a = g sinθ. With friction, subtract μg cosθ.
  • 摩擦力 f ≤ μR，方向和相对运动相反。Friction f ≤ μR, and it opposes the relative motion.
🗣 考场英文怎么说（解释"为什么斜面上物体匀速下滑"）：🗣 How to say it in the exam (explaining constant speed down a slope): The component of the weight down the slope equals the frictional force, so the resultant force is zero and the object moves at constant speed.

例题Example

一个 5 kg 的物块放在 30° 的光滑斜面上由静止滑下（取 g = 10 m/s²）。求沿斜面的加速度。A 5 kg block slides from rest down a smooth 30° slope (g = 10 m/s²). Find its acceleration down the slope.
沿斜面的力只有重力的分量：F = mg sin30°The only force along the slope is the component of the weight: F = mg sin30° F = ma → ma = mg sin30°F = ma → ma = mg sin30° m 约掉：a = g sin30° = 10 × 0.5 = 5 m/s²m cancels: a = g sin30° = 10 × 0.5 = 5 m/s² 注意：答案和 5 kg 一点关系都没有。Note: the answer has nothing to do with the 5 kg.

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：先在竖直方向平衡求支持力 R，再用 f = μR 算摩擦。Hint: resolve vertically first to find the normal force R, then use f = μR for friction.

一个 4 kg 的物块在水平面上被 30 N 的水平力拉动，摩擦系数 μ = 0.5（取 g = 10 m/s²）。求加速度。A 4 kg block on horizontal ground is pulled by a 30 N horizontal force. The coefficient of friction is μ = 0.5 (g = 10 m/s²). Find the acceleration.
点开看答案Show answer
竖直方向平衡：R = mg = 4 × 10 = 40 NVertically balanced: R = mg = 4 × 10 = 40 N 摩擦力：f = μR = 0.5 × 40 = 20 NFriction: f = μR = 0.5 × 40 = 20 N 合力：F = 30 − 20 = 10 NResultant: F = 30 − 20 = 10 N a = F / m = 10 / 4 = 2.5 m/s²
— 第 7 站完成 —— Stop 7 done —

单元 1 走完了 🎉Unit 1 done 🎉

今天只要记住三句话就够：图的斜率是速度、面积是位移；F = ma 里的 F 是合力；斜面上把重力劈成 sin 和 cos 两份。 Three sentences are enough for today: gradient is velocity, area is displacement; the F in F = ma is the resultant; on a slope, split the weight into sin and cos.

单元 2 · 材料Unit 2 · Materials

点标题可以直接跳到那一站。每站 = 一张图 + 一道例题 + 一道练习题。Click a title to jump there. Each stop = one diagram + one worked example + one practice question.

1 密度 ρ = m / VDensity ρ = m / V Density密度
学完你会：用 ρ = m/V 算质量、体积，并做单位换算。By the end: you can use ρ = m/V in any direction, and convert units correctly.
说人话：密度就是"这东西挤不挤"——同样大小的一块，铁比木头重得多，因为铁里的东西挤得紧。密度是材料自己的性质，切一半密度不变（质量和体积同时减半，比值不变）。 In plain words: density is "how tightly packed this stuff is" — the same size lump of iron weighs far more than wood because its contents are packed closer. Density belongs to the material itself, so cutting it in half does not change the density (mass and volume both halve, so the ratio stays put).
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • ρ = m / V，常用单位 kg/m³。, usually in kg/m³.
  • 1 g/cm³ = 1000 kg/m³，从 g/cm³ 换到 kg/m³ 要乘 1000。1 g/cm³ = 1000 kg/m³ — going from g/cm³ to kg/m³ means multiplying by 1000.
  • 水的密度 = 1000 kg/m³ = 1 g/cm³。Water: 1000 kg/m³ = 1 g/cm³.

例题Example

一块铝的密度是 2700 kg/m³，体积为 0.02 m³。求它的质量。A piece of aluminium has density 2700 kg/m³ and volume 0.02 m³. Find its mass.
ρ = m / V → m = ρVρ = m / V → m = ρV m = 2700 × 0.02 = 54 kg

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：先统一单位，2.5 g/cm³ 换成 kg/m³。Hint: convert 2.5 g/cm³ into kg/m³ first.

一块石头密度 2.5 g/cm³，质量为 5 kg。求它的体积（用 m³ 表示）。A stone has density 2.5 g/cm³ and mass 5 kg. Find its volume in m³.
点开看答案Show answer
换算：2.5 g/cm³ = 2500 kg/m³Convert: 2.5 g/cm³ = 2500 kg/m³ V = m / ρ = 5 / 2500 = 0.002 m³V = m / ρ = 5 / 2500 = 0.002 m³
— 第 1 站完成 —— Stop 1 done —
2 力与形变：胡克定律Force and deformation: Hooke's law Hooke's law胡克定律
学完你会：用 F = kx 算弹簧伸长，并从图里读出 k。By the end: you can use F = kx and read the spring constant off a graph.
说人话：弹簧很"讲道理"：力加倍，伸长也加倍，这就是 F = kx。k 是这根弹簧的"硬度"，越难拉的 k 越大。但弹簧有脾气——拉过头就回不去了，那个临界点叫弹性极限，图上线一弯就是过了。 In plain words: a spring is very fair-minded: double the force, double the extension — that is F = kx. The constant k is how stiff the spring is; the harder it is to pull, the bigger k. But springs have a limit: pull too far and it will not go back. That point is the elastic limit, and it is where the graph starts to bend.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • F = kx：F 是力（N），k 是劲度系数（N/m），x 是伸长量不是总长！: F in N, k in N/m, and x is the extension, not the total length!
  • F–x 图的斜率就是 k。On an F–x graph, the gradient is k.
  • 弹簧串联变软（k 变小），并联变硬（k 变大）。Springs in series get softer (smaller k); in parallel they get stiffer (bigger k).
  • x 是"变长了多少"，不是"现在多长"——这是本单元第一号错点。x is "how much longer", not "how long now" — the number one mistake in this unit.

例题Example

一根弹簧原长 20 cm，k = 200 N/m。挂上一个 4 kg 的砝码后，它有多长？（取 g = 10 m/s²）A spring of unstretched length 20 cm has k = 200 N/m. A 4 kg mass is hung from it. How long is the spring now? (g = 10 m/s²)
先求力：F = mg = 4 × 10 = 40 NForce first: F = mg = 4 × 10 = 40 N 再求伸长量：x = F / k = 40 / 200 = 0.2 m = 20 cmThen the extension: x = F / k = 40 / 200 = 0.2 m = 20 cm 总长 = 原长 + 伸长 = 20 + 20 = 40 cmTotal length = original + extension = 20 + 20 = 40 cm

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：两根并联，一起承担这个力；先算等效 k。Hint: in parallel the two springs share the load — find the combined k first.

两根相同的弹簧并联，每根 k = 150 N/m。挂 3 kg 的物体（取 g = 10 m/s²），一共伸长多少？Two identical springs of k = 150 N/m are placed in parallel and a 3 kg mass is hung from them (g = 10 m/s²). What is the extension?
点开看答案Show answer
并联：k总 = 150 + 150 = 300 N/mIn parallel: ktotal = 150 + 150 = 300 N/m F = mg = 3 × 10 = 30 NF = mg = 3 × 10 = 30 N x = F / k = 30 / 300 = 0.10 m （10 cm）(10 cm)
— 第 2 站完成 —— Stop 2 done —
3 弹性势能：拉长存了多少能Elastic potential energy Elastic energy弹性势能
学完你会：算弹簧里存了多少能量，并知道为什么公式里有个 ½。By the end: you can find the energy stored in a spring, and explain where the ½ comes from.
说人话：把弹簧拉长，你做的功就存在弹簧里，松手它就还给你。存了多少？看 F–x 图下面那块三角形——为什么是三角形而不是矩形？因为你一开始几乎不用力，越拉越费劲，平均下来只用了最大力的一半，所以有个 ½。 In plain words: stretching a spring stores the work you do inside the spring, and it hands it back when you let go. How much? The triangle under the F–x graph. Why a triangle and not a rectangle? Because at the start you hardly need any force and it gets harder as you go, so on average you used half the maximum force — hence the ½.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • E = ½Fx  和and  E = ½kx²，两个都对，看题目给了什么。— both are correct; use whichever matches what you are given.
  • x 是平方：拉长 2 倍，能量变成 4 倍。x is squared: double the extension and the energy becomes four times as big.
  • 单位焦耳 J；过了弹性极限这个公式就不准了（有能量变成热）。Unit: joules J. Past the elastic limit the formula breaks down (some energy becomes heat).

例题Example

一根 k = 400 N/m 的弹簧被拉伸 0.15 m。求储存的弹性势能。A spring of k = 400 N/m is stretched by 0.15 m. Find the elastic potential energy stored.
E = ½kx² = ½ × 400 × (0.15)² = 200 × 0.0225 = 4.5 J

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：能量守恒，弹性势能全部变成动能。Hint: energy is conserved — all the elastic energy becomes kinetic energy.

上题的弹簧（k = 400 N/m，伸长 0.15 m）松手后把一个 0.2 kg 的小球弹出。求小球获得的速度。The spring from the example (k = 400 N/m, extension 0.15 m) launches a 0.2 kg ball. Find the speed of the ball.
点开看答案Show answer
弹性势能 = 4.5 J，全部变成动能Elastic energy 4.5 J all becomes kinetic energy ½mv² = 4.5 → ½ × 0.2 × v² = 4.5½mv² = 4.5 → ½ × 0.2 × v² = 4.5 0.1 v² = 4.5 → v² = 45 v = 6.7 m/s
— 第 3 站完成 —— Stop 3 done —
4 应力与应变Stress and strain Stress & strain应力应变
学完你会：算应力和应变，知道为什么要用它们代替"力和伸长"。By the end: you can calculate stress and strain, and say why we use them instead of raw force and extension.
说人话：同样 100 N 的力，拉细铁丝能拉断，拉粗钢筋毫无反应——所以光看力不行，要看"分摊到每平方米上多少力"，这就是应力。同理，10 cm 伸长 1 cm 和 1 m 伸长 1 cm 完全不是一回事，所以要看比例，这就是应变。归一化了以后，同材料结果就一样。 In plain words: the same 100 N will snap a thin wire but barely bother a thick steel bar — so the force alone is not enough, we need the force per square metre, which is stress. Likewise, stretching 10 cm by 1 cm is not the same as stretching 1 m by 1 cm, so we use the ratio — that is strain. Once normalised, the same material always gives the same answer.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • 应力 = F / A，单位 Pa（1 MPa = 10⁶ Pa）。Stress = F / A, unit Pa (1 MPa = 10⁶ Pa).
  • 应变 = x / L，没有单位（也常写成百分数）。Strain = x / L, no unit (often written as a percentage).
  • 面积 A = πd²/4，直径别忘了先除以 2 才是半径。Area A = πd²/4 — halve the diameter first to get the radius.
  • mm² → m² 要除以 10⁶。mm² → m² means dividing by 10⁶.

例题Example

一根钢丝原长 2.0 m，横截面积 2.0 × 10⁻⁶ m²，受到 1000 N 的拉力后伸长 5 mm。求应力和应变。A steel wire of length 2.0 m and cross-sectional area 2.0 × 10⁻⁶ m² stretches by 5 mm under a 1000 N load. Find the stress and the strain.
应力 = F / A = 1000 / (2.0 × 10⁻⁶) = 5.0 × 10⁸ PaStress = F / A = 1000 / (2.0 × 10⁻⁶) = 5.0 × 10⁸ Pa 应变 = x / L = 0.005 / 2.0 = 2.5 × 10⁻³Strain = x / L = 0.005 / 2.0 = 2.5 × 10⁻³

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：直径 1.0 mm → 半径 0.5 mm = 5×10⁻⁴ m，面积 = πr²。Hint: diameter 1.0 mm → radius 0.5 mm = 5×10⁻⁴ m, then area = πr².

一根直径 1.0 mm 的金属丝受到 200 N 的拉力。求应力（单位 Pa）。A wire of diameter 1.0 mm carries a load of 200 N. Find the stress in Pa.
点开看答案Show answer
半径 r = 0.5 mm = 5.0 × 10⁻⁴ mRadius r = 0.5 mm = 5.0 × 10⁻⁴ m A = πr² = π × (5×10⁻⁴)² = 7.85 × 10⁻⁷ m²A = πr² = π × (5×10⁻⁴)² = 7.85 × 10⁻⁷ m² 应力 = 200 / (7.85×10⁻⁷) = 2.55 × 10⁸ PaStress = 200 / (7.85×10⁻⁷) = 2.55 × 10⁸ Pa
— 第 4 站完成 —— Stop 4 done —
5 杨氏模量The Young modulus Young modulus杨氏模量
学完你会：用 E = FL/(Ax) 算杨氏模量，并知道它是材料的固有属性。By the end: you can use E = FL/(Ax) and explain that it is a property of the material alone.
说人话：杨氏模量就是问一句"这材料有多难拉伸"——数值越大越硬。它把力和尺寸都排除了，所以同一材料，做成粗的细的长的短的，E 都一样。这就是为什么工程师喜欢用它：查表就知道这块钢会怎么表现。 In plain words: the Young modulus answers "how hard is this material to stretch" — bigger means stiffer. It strips out the force and the size, so the same material has the same E whether it is thick, thin, long or short. That is why engineers like it: look it up in a table and you know how the steel will behave.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • E = stress / strain = FL / (Ax)
  • 单位 Pa，但数值巨大，常用 GPa（1 GPa = 10⁹ Pa）。Unit Pa, but the numbers are huge, so GPa is usual (1 GPa = 10⁹ Pa).
  • 弹性范围内的应力–应变图斜率就是 E。Within the elastic region, the gradient of the stress–strain graph is E.
  • 和弹簧 k 的关系：k = EA / L（同样材料，越粗越硬、越长越软）。Link to the spring constant: k = EA / L (thicker is stiffer, longer is softer).

例题Example

一根 2.0 m 长、横截面积 1.5 × 10⁻⁶ m² 的金属丝，在 300 N 拉力下伸长 2.0 mm。求杨氏模量。A wire of length 2.0 m and cross-sectional area 1.5 × 10⁻⁶ m² stretches by 2.0 mm under a 300 N load. Find the Young modulus.
E = FL / (Ax)，先统一单位：x = 2.0 mm = 2.0 × 10⁻³ mE = FL / (Ax), with x = 2.0 mm = 2.0 × 10⁻³ m E = (300 × 2.0) / (1.5 × 10⁻⁶ × 2.0 × 10⁻³) = 600 / (3.0 × 10⁻⁹) = 2.0 × 10¹¹ Pa 即 200 GPa，和钢的标准值吻合。That is 200 GPa, matching the standard value for steel.

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：先把公式变形求 x，注意 E 用 Pa 不是 GPa。Hint: rearrange for x first, and use E in Pa, not GPa.

一根 3.0 m 长的钢丝（E = 2.0 × 10¹¹ Pa，A = 1.0 × 10⁻⁶ m²）受到 500 N 拉力。伸长多少？A 3.0 m steel wire (E = 2.0 × 10¹¹ Pa, A = 1.0 × 10⁻⁶ m²) carries a 500 N load. What is the extension?
点开看答案Show answer
E = FL / (Ax) → x = FL / (AE)E = FL / (Ax) → x = FL / (AE) x = (500 × 3.0) / (1.0 × 10⁻⁶ × 2.0 × 10¹¹) = 1500 / (2.0 × 10⁵) = 7.5 × 10⁻³ m （7.5 mm）(7.5 mm)
— 第 5 站完成 —— Stop 5 done —
6 应力–应变图：读材料的性格Stress–strain graphs: reading a material's personality Stress–strain应力应变图
学完你会：从图上认出弹性极限、极限强度、断裂点，并区分韧性与脆性。By the end: you can identify the elastic limit, UTS and breaking point, and tell ductile from brittle.
说人话：这张图就是材料的性格测试。开头直线段：老老实实，松手能弹回（弹性区）。过了弯点（弹性极限）：开始"记仇"，松手回不去了（塑性变形）。最高点是它能扛的极限，之后开始"脖子变细"然后断。铜这样的韧性材料会先拉长很长；玻璃这样的脆性材料一点不拉长，直接崩。 In plain words: this graph is a personality test for a material. The initial straight part: well behaved, it springs back when you let go (elastic region). Past the bend (elastic limit): it starts holding a grudge, and will not go back (plastic deformation). The peak is the most it can take, after which it starts to neck and then breaks. Ductile materials like copper stretch a long way first; brittle ones like glass barely stretch at all before snapping.
记住这几点（这几个词考卷上直接给分）：Remember these (these words are marks on the paper):
  • 弹性变形 elastic deformation：去掉力后能完全恢复。Elastic deformation: fully recovers when the load is removed.
  • 塑性变形 plastic deformation：去掉力后回不去，永久变形。Plastic deformation: does not recover — permanently changed.
  • 韧性 ductile：断裂前能拉很长（铜、低碳钢）。Ductile: stretches a lot before breaking (copper, mild steel).
  • 脆性 brittle：几乎不塑性变形就断（玻璃、陶瓷、铸铁）。Brittle: breaks with almost no plastic deformation (glass, ceramics, cast iron).
  • 曲线下的总面积 = 单位体积吸收的能量（ toughness， toughness）。The total area under the curve is the energy absorbed per unit volume (toughness).
🗣 考场英文怎么说（区分韧性与脆性）：🗣 How to say it in the exam (ductile vs brittle): A ductile material undergoes a large plastic deformation before breaking. A brittle material breaks with little or no plastic deformation.

例题Example

一根金属丝加载到某点后，撤去外力发现它比原来长了 3 mm。这说明了什么？A wire is loaded to a certain point, and when the load is removed it is found to be 3 mm longer than before. What does this tell you?
撤力后没有恢复 → 发生了塑性变形。It did not recover → plastic deformation has occurred. 说明加载超过了弹性极限（也超过了比例极限）。This means the load went beyond the elastic limit (and beyond the limit of proportionality). 不过它还没断，说明还没到断裂点。It has not snapped, so it has not reached the breaking point.

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：想"桥会不会突然断掉"，以及断之前有没有预兆。Hint: ask whether the bridge would fail suddenly, and whether there is any warning beforehand.

造桥为什么用低碳钢而不用玻璃或铸铁？用「韧性 / 脆性」这两个词解释。Why is mild steel used for bridges rather than glass or cast iron? Explain using "ductile" and "brittle".
点开看答案Show answer
低碳钢是韧性（ductile）材料：过载时会先大幅变形、明显下垂，有预兆，能提前发现并抢修。Mild steel is ductile: when overloaded it deforms a lot and visibly sags, giving a warning so it can be repaired in time. 玻璃和铸铁是脆性（brittle）材料：几乎不变形就突然断裂，毫无预兆，对桥梁极其危险。Glass and cast iron are brittle: they snap suddenly with almost no deformation, with no warning at all — extremely dangerous for a bridge.
— 第 6 站完成 —— Stop 6 done —
7 测杨氏模量的实验Measuring the Young modulus Experiment实验
学完你会：说出这个实验的器材、步骤和三条减小误差的办法。By the end: you can describe the apparatus, the method, and three ways to reduce error.
说人话：这个实验唯一的难点是：伸长量太小了，一根 2 m 的钢丝挂几公斤，可能只长 0.5 mm。所以整个实验的设计思路就两个字：放大。丝要长（伸长才明显）、直径要用螺旋测微器量准（A 差一点点，E 就差很多）、画图取斜率（比拿一个点硬算稳）。 In plain words: the one difficulty here is that the extension is tiny — a 2 m steel wire under a few kilograms may stretch only 0.5 mm. So the whole design comes down to one word: magnify. Use a long wire (bigger extension), measure the diameter with a micrometer (a small error in A throws E right off), and take the gradient of a graph (steadier than trusting a single reading).
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • 器材：长金属丝 + 砝码 + 螺旋测微器（量直径）+ 米尺（量长度）+ 游标/标尺（量伸长）。Apparatus: long wire + slotted masses + micrometer (diameter) + metre rule (length) + scale (extension).
  • 误差来源：直径的测量（因为要平方，影响最大）、读数视差、丝没拉直。Errors: the diameter (it gets squared, so it matters most), parallax when reading, and a wire that is not straight.
  • 改进：测直径多测几次取平均、加卸载荷都读数、画 F–x 图取斜率。Improvements: repeat and average the diameter, read on both loading and unloading, and take the gradient of an F–x graph.
  • 安全提示：戴护目镜，丝断了会弹回来。Safety: wear safety goggles — a snapping wire can fly back.
🗣 考场英文怎么说（实验改进题的高频句）：🗣 How to say it in the exam (high-frequency sentences for "improve the experiment"): Measure the diameter in several places and at different orientations, then take the mean. Plot a graph of force against extension and use the gradient, rather than a single pair of readings.

例题Example

为什么测量直径的误差对杨氏模量结果影响特别大？Why does an error in measuring the diameter affect the Young modulus result so much?
E = FL / (Ax)，面积 A = πd²/4E = FL / (Ax), and the area A = πd²/4 所以 E 和 d² 成反比 → 直径差 1%，面积差约 2%，E 就差约 2%。So E is inversely proportional to d² → a 1% error in d gives roughly a 2% error in E. 而且 d 本身很小（约 0.5 mm），同样 0.01 mm 的读数误差，百分比就很大。And d is small (about 0.5 mm), so the same 0.01 mm reading error is a large percentage.

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：想想"挂一个砝码算一次"和"挂十个砝码画图"哪个更可信。Hint: which is more trustworthy — one pair of readings, or ten plotted on a graph?

为什么要用 F–x 图的斜率来求 E，而不是只用一组 F 和 x 直接代入公式？Why use the gradient of an F–x graph to find E rather than substituting one pair of F and x values?
点开看答案Show answer
斜率用了所有数据点，个别读数的随机误差会被平均掉。The gradient uses every data point, so random errors in individual readings average out. 图还能一眼看出有没有异常点（离群值）以及是否超过了弹性极限（线开始弯）。A graph also shows at a glance whether there is an anomalous point, and whether the elastic limit has been passed (the line starts to curve). 单个点算出来的值，误差会 100% 带进结果。A single pair carries its error straight into the answer, unsoftened.
— 第 7 站完成 —— Stop 7 done —

单元 2 走完了 🎉Unit 2 done 🎉

记住这三句：F = kx 里的 x 是伸长量不是总长；弹性势能 = F–x 图下的三角形；杨氏模量 = 应力 ÷ 应变，是材料自己的属性。 Three sentences: the x in F = kx is the extension, not the total length; elastic energy is the triangle under the F–x graph; Young modulus = stress ÷ strain, a property of the material itself.

单元 3 · 波Unit 3 · Waves

AS 内容 · 概念多、计算少，是解释题的重灾区AS content · many concepts, few calculations — and a favourite home for "explain" questions

本单元包括内容What this unit covers 今天要走 7 站7 stops today

点标题可以直接跳到那一站。这一单元解释题特别多，注意 🗣 方框里的句子。Click a title to jump there. This unit is full of "explain" questions — watch the 🗣 boxes.

1 波的两个身份：λ 与 fA wave's two identities: λ and f Wavelength & frequency波长与频率
学完你会：看着波形读波长，用 v = fλ 做三类换算。By the end: you can read the wavelength off a waveform and use v = fλ in all three directions.
说人话：想象你站在海边看浪。波长 λ 是两个浪尖之间的距离；频率 f 是每秒有几个浪拍到你腿上。每秒过来 2 个浪、每个浪之间隔 3 米，那浪跑的速度就是 2 × 3 = 6 米每秒——这就是 v = fλ，不用背，理解了这个画面就记住了。 In plain words: picture yourself at the seashore. The wavelength λ is the gap between two crests; the frequency f is how many waves hit your legs each second. If 2 waves arrive per second and crests are 3 m apart, the waves travel at 2 × 3 = 6 m/s — that is v = fλ. No memorising needed once you have the picture.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • v = f λ，三个量知道两个就能求第三个。— know any two, find the third.
  • T = 1 / f，周期是频率的倒数（单位 s）。— the period is the reciprocal of frequency (in s).
  • 波速由介质决定：同一根绳子上，频率变了波长跟着变，但 v 不变。Wave speed is fixed by the medium: on the same rope, changing f changes λ, but v stays the same.
  • 声速约 340 m/s；光速 3.0 × 10⁸ m/s。Sound ≈ 340 m/s; light = 3.0 × 10⁸ m/s.

例题Example

一个音叉频率是 440 Hz，声速取 340 m/s。求波长和周期。A tuning fork has frequency 440 Hz. Take the speed of sound as 340 m/s. Find the wavelength and the period.
λ = v / f = 340 / 440 = 0.773 m（约 77 cm）λ = v / f = 340 / 440 = 0.773 m (about 77 cm) T = 1 / f = 1 / 440 = 2.27 × 10⁻³ s（约 2.3 ms）T = 1 / f = 1 / 440 = 2.27 × 10⁻³ s (about 2.3 ms)

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：绿光在真空里跑，v 就是光速。Hint: green light travels in a vacuum, so v is the speed of light.

绿光波长 5.5 × 10⁻⁷ m，求它的频率。Green light has wavelength 5.5 × 10⁻⁷ m. Find its frequency.
点开看答案Show answer
f = v / λ = 3.0 × 10⁸ / 5.5 × 10⁻⁷f = v / λ = 3.0 × 10⁸ / 5.5 × 10⁻⁷ = 5.45 × 10¹⁴ Hz= 5.45 × 10¹⁴ Hz
— 第 1 站完成 —— Stop 1 done —
2 横波与纵波Transverse and longitudinal waves Transverse / longitudinal横波 / 纵波
学完你会：给一个波，判断它是横波还是纵波，并说出理由。By the end: given any wave, you can say whether it is transverse or longitudinal, and why.
说人话：关键只看一件事——介质里的粒子往哪个方向动。横波：粒子上下动，波往前走，两个方向垂直。纵波：粒子前后动，波也往前走，两个方向平行。声音就是空气分子前后挤来挤去，所以是纵波。 In plain words: only one thing matters — which way the particles of the medium move. Transverse: particles move up and down while the wave moves forwards, so the two are perpendicular. Longitudinal: particles move back and forth along the same line the wave travels, so the two are parallel. Sound is air molecules shoving back and forth, so it is longitudinal.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • 横波：振动 ⟂ 传播。光、绳波、水波。Transverse: vibration ⟂ travel. Light, rope waves, water waves.
  • 纵波：振动 ∥ 传播。声波、弹簧推拉波。Longitudinal: vibration ∥ travel. Sound, waves on a pushed spring.
  • 纵波里有密部和疏部，密部之间的距离就是一个 λ。A longitudinal wave has compressions and rarefactions; the distance between compressions is one λ.
  • 波传能量不传物质——这句话解释题反复考。Waves transfer energy, not matter — this sentence comes up again and again in "explain" questions.
🗣 考场英文怎么说（横波/纵波的定义题）：🗣 How to say it in the exam (defining transverse / longitudinal): In a transverse wave, the particles vibrate at right angles to the direction of energy transfer. In a longitudinal wave, the particles vibrate parallel to the direction of energy transfer. A wave transfers energy without transferring matter.

例题Example

空气中的一个分子，在声波经过时做的是什么运动？它最终会不会被"吹"到听者耳朵里？As a sound wave passes, how does an air molecule move? Does it eventually get "blown" into the listener's ear?
分子在自己的平衡位置附近来回振动（前后），不是一路向前跑。The molecule oscillates about its equilibrium position (back and forth); it does not travel forwards. 所以不会被吹到耳朵里。传过去的是能量和振动状态，空气本身留在原地。So no, it is not blown into the ear. What travels is energy and the pattern of vibration; the air itself stays put.

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：地震时先感觉到上下颠还是左右晃？那两种波的速度不一样。Hint: in an earthquake, do you first feel up-down shaking or side-to-side? The two wave types travel at different speeds.

地震会产生 P 波（纵波）和 S 波（横波）。已知 P 波速度约 6 km/s，S 波约 3.5 km/s，为什么远处的人先感到上下颠簸，后感到左右摇晃？An earthquake produces P waves (longitudinal) and S waves (transverse). P waves travel at about 6 km/s and S waves at about 3.5 km/s. Why does a distant observer first feel up-down shaking and later side-to-side shaking?
点开看答案Show answer
P 波更快，先到达；它是纵波，所以先感觉到上下（沿传播方向）的颠簸。P waves are faster and arrive first; being longitudinal, they produce shaking along the direction of travel. S 波较慢，后到达；它是横波，带来垂直于传播方向的左右摇晃。S waves are slower and arrive later; being transverse, they produce shaking perpendicular to the travel direction. 这个时间差还能用来反推震源距离。That time gap is exactly what lets seismologists work out the distance to the epicentre.
— 第 2 站完成 —— Stop 2 done —
3 偏振：只有横波能做到Polarisation: only transverse waves can do it Polarisation偏振
学完你会：解释为什么偏振能证明光是横波，以及为什么声音不能偏振。By the end: you can explain why polarisation proves light is transverse, and why sound cannot be polarised.
说人话：拿一根绳子穿过栅栏的竖缝。你上下抖绳子，波能过去；你左右抖，波被栅栏挡住。偏振片就是这道栅栏。声音是纵波，它的振动方向和传播方向同一条线，没有"横着的方向"可以被筛掉，所以永远偏振不了。 In plain words: thread a rope through the vertical slots of a fence. Shake it up and down and the wave passes; shake it side to side and the fence blocks it. A polariser is that fence. Sound is longitudinal — its vibration lies along the same line as its travel, so there is no sideways direction to filter out, and it can never be polarised.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • 偏振只发生在横波上——这是横波的判据。Only transverse waves can be polarised — that is the test.
  • 偏振片转 90°，透过的光强降到 0。Turn the polariser 90° and the transmitted intensity falls to zero.
  • 应用：偏光太阳镜、液晶屏、3D 电影眼镜。Uses: polarised sunglasses, LCD screens, 3D cinema glasses.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：🗣 How to say it in the exam: Polarisation can only occur for transverse waves. Sound is a longitudinal wave, so it cannot be polarised. When the polariser is rotated through 90°, the intensity falls to zero.

例题Example

一位同学说："如果声音能偏振，就能做出隔音的偏振耳机。"这句话错在哪？A student says: "If sound could be polarised, we could make polarising headphones that block noise." What is wrong with that?
前提就错了：声音是纵波，纵波不能偏振。The premise is wrong: sound is a longitudinal wave, and longitudinal waves cannot be polarised. 偏振筛的是"垂直于传播方向的振动"，而声波根本没有这种振动。Polarisation filters vibrations perpendicular to the direction of travel, and sound has none of those.

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：转第二片的时候，透过来的光会怎么变？Hint: as you rotate the second polariser, what happens to the light coming through?

在偏振片后面再放一片同样的偏振片，从 0° 慢慢转到 90°，光强如何变化？转到 180° 和 360° 呢？A second identical polariser is placed after the first and rotated slowly from 0° to 90°. What happens to the intensity? What about at 180° and 360°?
点开看答案Show answer
0°：两片方向一致，最亮。At 0°: the two axes line up, so it is brightest. 转到 90°：越来越暗，90° 时全黑。Towards 90°: it gets darker and darker, and at 90° it goes completely dark. 180°：又和原来一致，重新变亮；270° 又变暗；360° 回到最亮。每 180° 循环一次。At 180°: the axes line up again, so it is bright again; at 270° it is dark; at 360° it is brightest. The pattern repeats every 180°.
— 第 3 站完成 —— Stop 3 done —
4 叠加原理The principle of superposition Superposition叠加
学完你会：判断两列波叠加后是变强还是变弱，并说出"相加"这个核心动作。By the end: you can say whether two waves will reinforce or cancel, and name the key word — "add".
说人话：两个波在同一根绳子上相遇，绳子上每一点的位移就是两个波各自造成的位移加起来。峰加峰 = 更大的峰（相长）；峰加谷 = 互相抵消（相消）。最妙的是：它们穿过彼此之后，形状完全不变，就像从没遇见过一样。 In plain words: when two waves meet on the same rope, the displacement at every point is the sum of the displacements each wave would have produced alone. Peak plus peak = a bigger peak (constructive); peak plus trough = cancellation (destructive). The subtle part: after they pass through each other, both are completely unchanged, as if they had never met.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • 叠加原理：合位移 = 各分位移的矢量和。Principle of superposition: the resultant displacement is the vector sum of the individual displacements.
  • 相长（同相 / 路程差 = nλ）：振幅变大。Constructive (in phase / path difference = nλ): larger amplitude.
  • 相消（反相 / 路程差 = (n+½)λ）：振幅变小甚至为 0。Destructive (out of phase / path difference = (n+½)λ): smaller amplitude, possibly zero.
  • 相遇后波形不受影响，各自继续传播。After meeting, the waves are unaffected and carry on.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：🗣 How to say it in the exam: When two waves meet, the resultant displacement is the sum of the individual displacements. Constructive interference occurs when the waves are in phase, giving a larger amplitude. Destructive interference occurs when the waves are in antiphase, giving a smaller (or zero) amplitude.

例题Example

两列相干波振幅分别是 3 cm 和 2 cm。求相长时和相消时的合振幅。Two coherent waves have amplitudes 3 cm and 2 cm. Find the resultant amplitude for constructive and for destructive interference.
相长：3 + 2 = 5 cmConstructive: 3 + 2 = 5 cm 相消：3 − 2 = 1 cm（不是 0！只有两列振幅相等才抵消到 0）Destructive: 3 − 2 = 1 cm (not zero! cancellation to zero only happens when the amplitudes are equal)

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：路程差是波长的多少倍？整数倍还是半整数倍？Hint: how many wavelengths is the path difference — a whole number, or a whole number and a half?

波长 40 cm 的两列相干波到达某点，路程分别是 12.0 m 和 11.8 m。该点是亮还是暗？Two coherent waves of wavelength 40 cm arrive at a point having travelled 12.0 m and 11.8 m. Is that point bright (max) or dark (min)?
点开看答案Show answer
路程差 = 12.0 − 11.8 = 0.20 m = 20 cmPath difference = 12.0 − 11.8 = 0.20 m = 20 cm 20 / 40 = 0.5 = ½ λ → 属于 (n+½)λ，反相20 / 40 = 0.5 = ½ λ → of the form (n+½)λ, so antiphase 所以是相消干涉 → 暗点（极小）。So it is destructive interference → a dark point (minimum).
— 第 4 站完成 —— Stop 4 done —
5 干涉与双缝实验Interference and the double-slit Two-source interference双缝干涉
学完你会：用 λ = ax/D 算波长，并判断某条是亮纹还是暗纹。By the end: you can use λ = ax/D to find the wavelength, and say whether a fringe is bright or dark.
说人话：两个缝出来的波是同步的（相干）。走到屏上某点，一个可能走了 1000 个波，另一个走了 1000.5 个波——差半个波长，一个的峰正好对上另一个的谷，就暗了。差整数个波长，峰对峰，就亮了。所以屏上出现一圈一圈明暗相间的条纹。 In plain words: the waves leaving the two slits are in step (coherent). At some point on the screen one may have travelled exactly 1000 wavelengths and the other 1000.5 — a half-wavelength difference, so one's peak lands on the other's trough and it goes dark. Differ by a whole number of wavelengths and peak meets peak, so it is bright. That is why the screen shows alternating bright and dark fringes.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • λ = a x / D，四个量知道三个求第四个。— know any three, find the fourth.
  • 两个波源必须相干：同频率 + 相位差恒定。The two sources must be coherent: same frequency and constant phase difference.
  • 缝间距 a 越小，条纹间距 x 越大（更好测量）。A smaller slit separation a gives a larger fringe spacing x (easier to measure).
  • 用激光做光源，因为它是单色且相干的。Use a laser as the source, since it is monochromatic and coherent.
🗣 考场英文怎么说（实验描述题）：🗣 How to say it in the exam (describing the experiment): The two slits act as coherent sources, since they are illuminated by the same laser. A bright fringe is formed where the path difference is a whole number of wavelengths. Measure the separation across several fringes and divide by the number of gaps, to reduce the uncertainty.

例题Example

双缝间距 a = 0.25 mm，缝到屏 D = 2.0 m，测得相邻亮纹间距 x = 4.8 mm。求光的波长。Slit separation a = 0.25 mm, slit-to-screen distance D = 2.0 m, and adjacent bright fringes are x = 4.8 mm apart. Find the wavelength.
先统一单位：a = 2.5 × 10⁻⁴ m，x = 4.8 × 10⁻³ mConvert to metres first: a = 2.5 × 10⁻⁴ m, x = 4.8 × 10⁻³ m λ = ax / D = (2.5 × 10⁻⁴)(4.8 × 10⁻³) / 2.0λ = ax / D = (2.5 × 10⁻⁴)(4.8 × 10⁻³) / 2.0 = 6.0 × 10⁻⁷ m（600 nm，橙红光）= 6.0 × 10⁻⁷ m (600 nm, orange-red light)

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：先算出有多少个条纹间距，再除以数量。Hint: work out how many fringe gaps there are, then divide by that number.

用 λ = 5.0 × 10⁻⁷ m 的光，a = 0.40 mm，D = 3.0 m。屏上 11 条亮纹（从最左到最右）总跨度是多少？Light of wavelength 5.0 × 10⁻⁷ m, a = 0.40 mm, D = 3.0 m. What is the total span across 11 bright fringes (from the leftmost to the rightmost)?
点开看答案Show answer
x = λD / a = (5.0 × 10⁻⁷ × 3.0) / 4.0 × 10⁻⁴ = 3.75 × 10⁻³ mx = λD / a = (5.0 × 10⁻⁷ × 3.0) / 4.0 × 10⁻⁴ = 3.75 × 10⁻³ m 11 条亮纹之间只有 10 个间隔（这是最容易错的一步）Between 11 bright fringes there are only 10 gaps (the classic slip-up) 总跨度 = 10 × 3.75 mm = 37.5 mmTotal span = 10 × 3.75 mm = 37.5 mm
— 第 5 站完成 —— Stop 5 done —
6 衍射：绕过角落Diffraction: bending round corners Diffraction衍射
学完你会：说出衍射最明显的条件，并解释生活中的现象。By the end: you can state when diffraction is most noticeable, and explain everyday examples.
说人话：波遇到障碍物或狭缝时会"拐弯"，这叫衍射。拐得多不多，取决于缝的大小和波长是不是差不多。声波波长几十厘米到几米，跟门缝、墙角尺寸相仿，所以能绕过去；光的波长只有几百纳米，比门缝小一百万倍，几乎只走直线——所以能隔墙听见人说话，却看不见人。 In plain words: waves bend round obstacles or through gaps — that is diffraction. How much they bend depends on whether the gap size and the wavelength are comparable. Sound wavelengths range from tens of centimetres to metres, similar to a doorway or a corner, so sound bends round them; light's wavelength is a few hundred nanometres, a million times smaller than a doorway, so it travels essentially straight — which is why you can hear people through a wall but not see them.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • 衍射最明显的条件：缝宽（或障碍物尺寸）≈ 波长。Diffraction is most noticeable when the gap (or obstacle) is about the same size as the wavelength.
  • 缝越窄、波长越长 → 散得越开。Narrower gap or longer wavelength → wider spread.
  • 衍射不改变波速、频率和波长，只改变传播方向。Diffraction does not change speed, frequency or wavelength — only the direction of travel.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：🗣 How to say it in the exam: Diffraction is the spreading of a wave as it passes through a gap or around an edge. Diffraction is most significant when the wavelength is comparable to the size of the gap. Diffraction does not change the speed, frequency or wavelength of the wave.

例题Example

为什么教室门外能清楚听见里面的讲话声，却看不到讲话的人？Why can you clearly hear someone talking inside a classroom from the doorway, but not see them?
说话声频率约 500 Hz → λ = 340 / 500 = 0.68 m，和门宽（约 1 m）接近。Speech is around 500 Hz → λ = 340 / 500 = 0.68 m, comparable with the door width (about 1 m). 所以声波在门口明显衍射，向各方向散开，走廊上都能听见。So the sound diffracts strongly at the doorway and spreads in all directions along the corridor. 可见光 λ ≈ 5 × 10⁻⁷ m，比门窄几百万倍，几乎不衍射，基本直线传播。Visible light has λ ≈ 5 × 10⁻⁷ m, millions of times smaller than the door, so it barely diffracts and travels essentially in straight lines.

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：把"波长"和"建筑尺寸"比一比。Hint: compare the wavelength with the size of the building.

为什么手机信号（波长约 30 cm）能绕过建筑物到达手机，而卫星电视用的微波（波长约 3 cm）必须"对准"卫星天线？Why can a mobile phone signal (wavelength about 30 cm) reach a phone round the side of a building, while satellite TV microwaves (wavelength about 3 cm) need the dish aimed precisely at the satellite?
点开看答案Show answer
手机信号 λ ≈ 0.3 m，和窗户、墙角、车身尺寸同一量级 → 明显衍射，能绕过去。Phone signals have λ ≈ 0.3 m, the same order of magnitude as windows, corners and cars → strong diffraction, so they bend round. 卫星微波 λ ≈ 0.03 m，远小于建筑物 → 几乎不衍射，只能直线传播。Satellite microwaves have λ ≈ 0.03 m, far smaller than buildings → almost no diffraction, so they travel in straight lines. 所以碟形天线必须对准卫星；而且波束窄也让能量更集中、信号更强。That is why the dish must be pointed at the satellite; the narrow beam also concentrates the energy and gives a stronger signal.
— 第 6 站完成 —— Stop 6 done —
7 驻波：看起来不走了Stationary waves: waves that seem to stand still Stationary waves驻波
学完你会：分辨波节波腹，用 λ/2 算距离，并说出驻波和行波的三点区别。By the end: you can identify nodes and antinodes, use the λ/2 spacing, and list three differences from a progressive wave.
说人话：抖动绳子的一端，波传到墙上反射回来。过去的波和回来的波叠在一起，就出现了驻波——看起来波形停在那儿不走了。有些位置永远不动（波节 N），有些位置晃得最厉害（波腹 A）。吉他、小提琴的弦就是这样振动的：两端固定是波节，中间鼓出来是波腹。 In plain words: shake one end of a rope, the wave reaches the wall and reflects back. The outgoing and returning waves superpose to form a stationary wave — the pattern appears to stand still. Some positions never move (nodes, N) while others swing the most (antinodes, A). Guitar and violin strings vibrate exactly like this: the fixed ends are nodes, the bulging middle is an antinode.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • 波节：振幅恒为 0；波腹：振幅最大。Node: amplitude always zero; antinode: maximum amplitude.
  • 相邻波节（或相邻波腹）间距 = λ / 2。Distance between adjacent nodes (or adjacent antinodes) = λ / 2.
  • 驻波不传递能量；行波传递能量。A stationary wave transfers no energy; a progressive wave does.
  • 驻波所有点同时到达最大位移；行波是依次。In a stationary wave every point reaches maximum displacement together; in a progressive wave they do so in turn.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：🗣 How to say it in the exam: A node is a point where the amplitude is always zero; an antinode is a point of maximum amplitude. The distance between adjacent nodes is λ / 2. A stationary wave does not transfer energy, unlike a progressive wave.

例题Example

一根弦上形成驻波，相邻两个波节相距 18 cm。求波长；若频率 400 Hz，求波速。A stationary wave on a string has adjacent nodes 18 cm apart. Find the wavelength, and the wave speed if the frequency is 400 Hz.
λ / 2 = 18 cm → λ = 36 cm = 0.36 mλ / 2 = 18 cm → λ = 36 cm = 0.36 m v = fλ = 400 × 0.36 = 144 m/sv = fλ = 400 × 0.36 = 144 m/s

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：两端固定意味着两端都是波节，画一画基频是什么样子。Hint: fixed ends means both ends are nodes — sketch what the fundamental looks like.

一根两端固定的弦长 60 cm，形成基频（只有一个波腹）驻波。求波长和基频频率（波速 120 m/s）。A string fixed at both ends is 60 cm long and vibrates in its fundamental mode (one antinode). Find the wavelength and the fundamental frequency (wave speed 120 m/s).
点开看答案Show answer
基频形状：两端波节 + 中间一个波腹 → 弦长 = λ / 2Fundamental shape: node at each end with one antinode between → string length = λ / 2 λ / 2 = 60 cm → λ = 120 cm = 1.2 mλ / 2 = 60 cm → λ = 120 cm = 1.2 m f = v / λ = 120 / 1.2 = 100 Hzf = v / λ = 120 / 1.2 = 100 Hz
— 第 7 站完成 —— Stop 7 done —

单元 3 走完了 🎉Unit 3 done 🎉

记住这三句：v = fλ，波速由介质决定；能偏振 ⟹ 一定是横波；亮纹路程差 = nλ，暗纹 = (n+½)λ，相邻波节间距 λ/2。这一单元解释题特别多，🗣 方框里的句子考前过一遍。 Three sentences: v = fλ, and the medium fixes the speed; if it can be polarised it must be transverse; bright fringes need a path difference of nλ, dark ones (n+½)λ, and adjacent nodes are λ/2 apart. This unit is heavy on "explain" questions — read the 🗣 boxes again before the exam.

点标题可以直接跳到那一站。单位换算（mA、kΩ、mm²）是本单元最大的隐形失分点。Click a title to jump there. Unit conversions (mA, kΩ, mm²) are the biggest hidden source of lost marks here.

1 电流与电荷：I = Q / tCurrent and charge: I = Q / t Current电流
学完你会：用 I = Q/t 算电流、电荷、时间，并做 mA / μA 换算。By the end: you can use I = Q/t in any direction and convert between A, mA and μA.
说人话：把电线想成水管，电流就是水流量——每秒流过去多少水（电荷）。题目问"多久能充进去多少电"，就是 Q = I t。注意一个历史遗留问题：电流方向规定为正电荷移动的方向，而金属里真正动的是带负电的电子，方向正好相反。考试说"电流方向"时，一律按正电荷来。 In plain words: think of a wire as a water pipe — current is the flow rate, how much water (charge) passes per second. When a question asks how long it takes to store a certain charge, that is Q = I t. One historical quirk: the direction of current is defined as the direction positive charge would move, while in a metal it is negatively charged electrons that actually move — the opposite way. In exams, always give the conventional direction.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • I = Q / t，等价于 Q = I t。, equivalently Q = I t.
  • Q = n e，e = 1.60 × 10⁻¹⁹ C（元电荷）。Q = n e, where e = 1.60 × 10⁻¹⁹ C (the elementary charge).
  • 串联电路里电流处处相等（电荷不会堆积）。In a series circuit the current is the same everywhere (charge cannot pile up).
  • 电流方向 = 正电荷方向，与电子流方向相反。Conventional current is opposite to the electron flow.

例题Example

手机电池容量 3000 mAh，以 1.5 A 恒流放电，理论上能放多久？A phone battery is rated 3000 mAh. Discharged at a steady 1.5 A, how long would it last in theory?
3000 mAh = 3.0 Ah = 3.0 A × 3600 s = 1.08 × 10⁴ C3000 mAh = 3.0 Ah = 3.0 A × 3600 s = 1.08 × 10⁴ C 更快的算法：t = 3.0 Ah / 1.5 A = 2.0 hThe quick way: t = 3.0 Ah / 1.5 A = 2.0 h

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：先求总电荷 Q = It，再除以每个电子带的电荷 e。Hint: find the total charge first, then divide by the charge on one electron.

一段导线中电流 2.0 mA，持续 5.0 分钟。求通过的电荷量，以及相当于多少个电子。A current of 2.0 mA flows for 5.0 minutes. Find the charge that passes, and how many electrons that is.
点开看答案Show answer
I = 2.0 × 10⁻³ A，t = 5.0 × 60 = 300 sI = 2.0 × 10⁻³ A, t = 5.0 × 60 = 300 s Q = I t = 2.0 × 10⁻³ × 300 = 0.60 CQ = I t = 2.0 × 10⁻³ × 300 = 0.60 C n = Q / e = 0.60 / 1.6 × 10⁻¹⁹ = 3.75 × 10¹⁸ 个n = Q / e = 0.60 / 1.6 × 10⁻¹⁹ = 3.75 × 10¹⁸ electrons
— 第 1 站完成 —— Stop 1 done —
2 电势差与电动势Potential difference and e.m.f. p.d. & e.m.f.电势差与电动势
学完你会：用 V = W/Q 计算，并说清 e.m.f. 和 p.d. 的区别。By the end: you can use V = W/Q, and explain the difference between e.m.f. and p.d.
说人话：把电荷想象成背着能量旅行的人。电动势 E 是电池这个"电梯"把每库仑电荷送上楼，给了它多少焦耳能量；电势差 V 是电荷经过灯泡、电阻时"花掉"了多少焦耳。两者都用伏特（1 V = 1 J/C）。关键区别：电池一接上用电器，端电压就会低于电动势，因为电池自己也有电阻（内阻），会先吃掉一点。 In plain words: picture charge as a traveller carrying energy. The e.m.f. E is the lift: how many joules the battery gives each coulomb. The p.d. V is what the charge spends passing through a lamp or resistor. Both are in volts (1 V = 1 J/C). The key difference: as soon as you connect a load, the terminal p.d. drops below the e.m.f., because the cell has its own resistance (internal resistance) and takes a share first.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • V = W / Q，1 V = 1 J/C。, and 1 V = 1 J/C.
  • e.m.f. = 电源把其他能转成电能的本领（单位 V）。e.m.f. = the energy per unit charge the source supplies (in V).
  • 开路（不接负载）时，端电压 = e.m.f.。On open circuit (no load) the terminal p.d. equals the e.m.f.
  • 一接负载，因为内阻，端电压 < e.m.f.。With a load connected, internal resistance makes the terminal p.d. less than the e.m.f.
🗣 考场英文怎么说（定义题必背）：🗣 How to say it in the exam (definitions worth memorising): The potential difference between two points is the energy transferred per unit charge. The e.m.f. of a source is the energy transferred per unit charge from another form into electrical energy. On open circuit, the terminal p.d. equals the e.m.f. of the cell.

例题Example

一个 12 V 的电源，把 5.0 C 的电荷送过外电路。电荷获得的能量是多少？若这些电荷全部通过一个灯泡并释放能量，灯泡得到多少焦耳？A 12 V source drives 5.0 C of charge round a circuit. How much energy do they receive? If all of it is then delivered to a lamp, how many joules does the lamp get?
W = V Q = 12 × 5.0 = 60 J（电源给出）W = V Q = 12 × 5.0 = 60 J (supplied by the source) 理想情况下（忽略内阻与导线），灯泡同样得到 60 J。Ideally (ignoring internal resistance and the wires) the lamp also receives 60 J.

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：V = W/Q，所以 Q = W/V。Hint: V = W/Q, so Q = W/V.

一个 9.0 V 电池给外电路提供了 180 J 的能量。通过了多少库仑的电荷？A 9.0 V battery supplies 180 J of energy to the external circuit. How much charge has passed?
点开看答案Show answer
Q = W / V = 180 / 9.0 = 20 CQ = W / V = 180 / 9.0 = 20 C
— 第 2 站完成 —— Stop 2 done —
3 电阻与欧姆定律Resistance and Ohm's law Ohm's law欧姆定律
学完你会：用 V = IR 三向求解，并判断元件是否遵守欧姆定律。By the end: you can use V = IR in all three directions, and say whether a component is ohmic.
说人话：电阻就是"这个元件有多挡路"。电压是推它的力，电流是实际流过去的量——同样的推力，挡得越狠，流过去的越少。欧姆定律说的是：对金属这类"听话"的元件，推力（V）加倍，流量（I）也加倍，R = V/I 始终不变。要注意因果：R 是元件自己的属性，不是由 V 或 I 决定的，只是恰好可以用 V/I 算出来。 In plain words: resistance is "how much this component gets in the way". The p.d. is the push, the current is what actually flows — with the same push, more obstruction means less flow. Ohm's law says that for well-behaved components such as metals, doubling the push (V) doubles the flow (I), so R = V/I stays constant. Mind the logic though: R is a property of the component; it is not caused by V or I, it just happens to equal V/I.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • V = I R，三个量知道两个求第三个。— know any two, find the third.
  • 欧姆定律：恒温下，金属导体的 V ∝ I。Ohm's law: at constant temperature, V ∝ I for a metallic conductor.
  • R 不随 V 变——R = V/I 只是算法，不是因果关系。R does not change with V — R = V/I is how you measure it, not what causes it.
  • P = V I = I²R = V²/R，功率三式等价。— three equivalent ways to write power.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：🗣 How to say it in the exam: Ohm's law states that the current in a conductor is proportional to the potential difference across it, provided the temperature is constant. The resistance is the ratio of p.d. to current; it is a property of the component. A component is ohmic if its V–I graph is a straight line through the origin.

例题Example

一个电阻两端电压 6.0 V，通过电流 24 mA。求电阻值和它消耗的功率。A resistor has 6.0 V across it and a current of 24 mA through it. Find its resistance and the power it dissipates.
先换单位：I = 24 mA = 0.024 AConvert first: I = 24 mA = 0.024 A R = V / I = 6.0 / 0.024 = 250 ΩR = V / I = 6.0 / 0.024 = 250 Ω P = V I = 6.0 × 0.024 = 0.144 WP = V I = 6.0 × 0.024 = 0.144 W

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：用 P = V²/R 一步到位。Hint: use P = V²/R in one step.

一个 100 Ω 的电阻接在 12 V 电源上。求电流和功率。A 100 Ω resistor is connected across a 12 V supply. Find the current and the power.
点开看答案Show answer
I = V / R = 12 / 100 = 0.12 AI = V / R = 12 / 100 = 0.12 A P = V² / R = 144 / 100 = 1.44 WP = V² / R = 144 / 100 = 1.44 W
— 第 3 站完成 —— Stop 3 done —
4 I–V 特性曲线：元件性格图I–V characteristics: a component's personality I–V characteristicsI–V 特性
学完你会：画出三种元件的 I–V 曲线，并解释灯泡为什么弯、二极管为什么单向。By the end: you can sketch the I–V curve of all three components and explain why the lamp bends and the diode is one-way.
说人话：定值电阻是老实人，给多少电压流多少电流，直线。灯泡不一样：电压一大，灯丝温度升高，金属原子振动加剧、电子更难通过，电阻变大，所以电流增长变慢，图线往下弯。二极管是单向阀门：正向要超过约 0.6 V 才"开门"，之后电流猛涨；反向几乎完全不通。 In plain words: a fixed resistor is the honest one — current rises in proportion to p.d., a straight line. A lamp is different: push the p.d. up and the filament gets hot, the metal atoms vibrate more, electrons struggle past, so the resistance rises and the current grows more slowly — the graph bends over. A diode is a one-way valve: in the forward direction it stays shut until about 0.6 V, then current surges; in reverse it barely conducts at all.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • 电阻：I–V 过原点直线，遵守欧姆定律。Resistor: straight line through the origin; ohmic.
  • 灯泡：温度升高 → R 变大 → 曲线向 V 轴弯。Lamp: hotter → larger R → the curve bends towards the V axis.
  • 二极管：正向阈值约 0.6 V，反向几乎不导通。Diode: forward threshold about 0.6 V; almost no reverse current.
  • 求某点电阻用 R = V / I，不要用图线斜率。Find R at a point from R = V / I — not from the gradient.
🗣 考场英文怎么说（解释灯泡曲线）：🗣 How to say it in the exam (explaining the lamp curve): As the potential difference increases, the temperature of the filament increases. The ions vibrate more, so electrons collide more often, and the resistance increases. The I–V graph therefore curves away from the current axis.

例题Example

某灯泡 I–V 图上，V = 2.0 V 时 I = 0.20 A；V = 8.0 V 时 I = 0.40 A。求两点的电阻，并说明变化。On a lamp's I–V graph, I = 0.20 A at V = 2.0 V, and I = 0.40 A at V = 8.0 V. Find the resistance at each point and comment.
R₁ = 2.0 / 0.20 = 10 ΩR₁ = 2.0 / 0.20 = 10 Ω R₂ = 8.0 / 0.40 = 20 ΩR₂ = 8.0 / 0.40 = 20 Ω 电压翻倍，电流也翻倍的话 R 应该不变；现在 R 从 10 Ω 涨到 20 Ω，说明灯丝变热、电阻变大。Doubling V and I would leave R unchanged; here R rises from 10 Ω to 20 Ω, showing the filament has heated up and its resistance increased.

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：反向接的时候，二极管相当于什么？Hint: when reverse-biased, what does a diode behave like?

一个二极管和一个 220 Ω 电阻串联接在 6.0 V 电源上（正向导通，二极管压降取 0.6 V）。求电流。A diode and a 220 Ω resistor are connected in series to a 6.0 V supply (forward biased; take the diode's p.d. as 0.6 V). Find the current.
点开看答案Show answer
串联分压：二极管先拿走 0.6 V，电阻分到 6.0 − 0.6 = 5.4 VSeries: the diode takes 0.6 V, leaving 6.0 − 0.6 = 5.4 V across the resistor I = V / R = 5.4 / 220 = 0.0245 A ≈ 25 mAI = V / R = 5.4 / 220 = 0.0245 A ≈ 25 mA
— 第 4 站完成 —— Stop 4 done —
5 电阻率 ρ = RA / LResistivity ρ = RA / L Resistivity电阻率
学完你会：用 R = ρL/A 算电阻或电阻率，把 mm² 正确换成 m²。By the end: you can use R = ρL/A either way round, and convert mm² to m² correctly.
说人话：还是水管的比方：管子越长，水越难过去（R ∝ L）；管子越粗，水越好过（R ∝ 1/A）。剩下那个"这种材料本身有多堵"的系数，就是电阻率 ρ——它是材料的属性，跟做成什么形状无关。铜的 ρ 极小（好导体），橡胶的极大（绝缘体），差了十几个数量级。 In plain words: back to the pipe: the longer the pipe, the harder it is to push water through (R ∝ L); the wider the pipe, the easier (R ∝ 1/A). What is left over — how obstructive the material is in itself — is the resistivity ρ, a property of the material regardless of shape. Copper's ρ is tiny (a good conductor), rubber's enormous (an insulator), differing by many orders of magnitude.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • R = ρ L / A，ρ 的单位是 Ω·m。, with ρ measured in Ω·m.
  • L 加倍 → R 加倍；A 加倍 → R 减半。Double L → double R; double A → halve R.
  • 圆形截面 A = π d² / 4，直径 d 必须先换成 m。For a circular wire A = π d² / 4, with the diameter d in metres first.
  • 1 mm² = 1 × 10⁻⁶ m²（平方后进率翻倍）。1 mm² = 1 × 10⁻⁶ m² (squaring doubles the power of ten).

例题Example

铜的电阻率 1.7 × 10⁻⁸ Ω·m。一根长 2.0 m、直径 0.40 mm 的铜线，电阻是多少？Copper has resistivity 1.7 × 10⁻⁸ Ω·m. Find the resistance of a copper wire 2.0 m long and 0.40 mm in diameter.
d = 0.40 mm = 4.0 × 10⁻⁴ md = 0.40 mm = 4.0 × 10⁻⁴ m A = π d² / 4 = π(4.0 × 10⁻⁴)² / 4 = 1.26 × 10⁻⁷ m²A = π d² / 4 = π(4.0 × 10⁻⁴)² / 4 = 1.26 × 10⁻⁷ m² R = (1.7 × 10⁻⁸ × 2.0) / 1.26 × 10⁻⁷ = 0.27 ΩR = (1.7 × 10⁻⁸ × 2.0) / 1.26 × 10⁻⁷ = 0.27 Ω

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：同一根线拉长到 2 倍，体积不变，所以截面积会变成多少？Hint: stretching to twice the length keeps the volume fixed — so what happens to the area?

一根电阻为 8.0 Ω 的金属丝被均匀拉长到原来的 2 倍（体积不变）。新的电阻是多少？A metal wire of resistance 8.0 Ω is stretched uniformly to twice its original length (volume unchanged). What is its new resistance?
点开看答案Show answer
体积 V_ol = A L 不变：L 变 2 倍 → A 变为 1/2Volume A L is constant: doubling L makes A half as large R ∝ L / A，所以 R 变为 2 ÷ (1/2) = 4 倍Since R ∝ L / A, R becomes 2 ÷ (1/2) = 4 times larger 新电阻 = 8.0 × 4 = 32 ΩNew resistance = 8.0 × 4 = 32 Ω
— 第 5 站完成 —— Stop 5 done —
6 串并联电路Series and parallel circuits Series & parallel串联与并联
学完你会：算串并联总电阻，并判断电流、电压在两种接法下怎么分。By the end: you can find the combined resistance, and say how current and p.d. split in each arrangement.
说人话：串联就是一条独木桥，所有人排一队过——所以电流处处相同，但每人消耗的力气（电压）加起来等于总电压。并联是开了两条岔路，每条路两端的高度差一样——所以电压相同，人分成两股（电流分）。并联的总电阻反而变小，因为多开了一条路，整体更好走了。 In plain words: series is a single plank bridge — everyone queues up to cross, so the current is the same everywhere, but the effort (p.d.) each person uses adds up to the total. Parallel is two diverging paths with the same height difference across each, so the p.d. is the same and the crowd splits (the current divides). The combined resistance in parallel is smaller, because an extra path makes the journey easier overall.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • 串联：I 相同；V 分；R = R₁ + R₂ + …Series: same I; V splits; R = R₁ + R₂ + …
  • 并联：V 相同；I 分；1/R = 1/R₁ + 1/R₂Parallel: same V; I splits; 1/R = 1/R₁ + 1/R₂
  • 两个并联快捷式：R = R₁R₂/(R₁+R₂)。Shortcut for two: R = R₁R₂/(R₁+R₂).
  • 并联总电阻永远小于最小的那一个——用它自检。The parallel total is always less than the smallest branch — use that as a check.

例题Example

6.0 Ω 和 3.0 Ω 两个电阻并联，再与 4.0 Ω 串联，接在 12 V 电源上。求总电阻和总电流。A 6.0 Ω and a 3.0 Ω resistor in parallel, in series with a 4.0 Ω resistor, connected to a 12 V supply. Find the total resistance and the total current.
并联部分：R_p = (6.0 × 3.0)/(6.0 + 3.0) = 18/9 = 2.0 ΩParallel part: R_p = (6.0 × 3.0)/(6.0 + 3.0) = 18/9 = 2.0 Ω 总电阻 = 2.0 + 4.0 = 6.0 ΩTotal = 2.0 + 4.0 = 6.0 Ω I = 12 / 6.0 = 2.0 AI = 12 / 6.0 = 2.0 A

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：并联两端电压相同，各支路电流可用 I = V/R 分别算。Hint: both branches have the same p.d., so find each branch current with I = V/R.

10 Ω 和 15 Ω 并联后接在 6.0 V 上。求并联总电阻、各支路电流和总电流。A 10 Ω and a 15 Ω resistor are connected in parallel across 6.0 V. Find the combined resistance, each branch current, and the total current.
点开看答案Show answer
R = (10 × 15)/(10 + 15) = 150/25 = 6.0 ΩR = (10 × 15)/(10 + 15) = 150/25 = 6.0 Ω I₁ = 6.0 / 10 = 0.60 A；I₂ = 6.0 / 15 = 0.40 AI₁ = 6.0 / 10 = 0.60 A; I₂ = 6.0 / 15 = 0.40 A 总电流 = 0.60 + 0.40 = 1.0 A（验算：6.0 / 6.0 = 1.0 A ✓）Total = 0.60 + 0.40 = 1.0 A (check: 6.0 / 6.0 = 1.0 A ✓)
— 第 6 站完成 —— Stop 6 done —
7 内阻与分压器Internal resistance and the potential divider Internal resistance内阻
学完你会：用 E = V + Ir 算内阻，用分压公式算输出电压。By the end: you can use E = V + Ir to find internal resistance, and the divider formula for an output p.d.
说人话：电池内部也有电阻，叫内阻 r。电流 I 一流动，就在电池内部先"扣掉" I r 这么多电压，所以外面测到的端电压 V = E − I r。这就是为什么旧电池空载测着还有 1.5 V，一接上电机就掉到 1.1 V——旧电池内阻大，一拉电流就垮。分压器则是用两个电阻串联，从总电压里"切"出需要的那一小份。 In plain words: a cell has resistance of its own, the internal resistance r. As soon as a current I flows, the cell deducts I r internally, so the terminal p.d. is V = E − I r. That is why an old battery reads 1.5 V with nothing connected but collapses to 1.1 V when you attach a motor — its internal resistance is large, so it sags as soon as you draw current. A potential divider is just two resistors in series, used to tap off the fraction of the supply you need.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • E = V + I r（电动势 = 端电压 + 内压降）。 (e.m.f. = terminal p.d. + internal drop).
  • 端电压 V = E − I r，电流越大端电压越低。Terminal p.d. V = E − I r: more current means a lower terminal p.d.
  • V–I 图上：纵截距 = E，斜率绝对值 = r。On a V–I graph the intercept is E and the magnitude of the gradient is r.
  • 分压器：V_out = V_in × R₂/(R₁+R₂)（R₂ 是"取电压"的那个）。Divider: V_out = V_in × R₂/(R₁+R₂), where R₂ is the one you tap across.
🗣 考场英文怎么说：🗣 How to say it in the exam: Some of the e.m.f. is used driving current through the internal resistance, so the terminal p.d. is less than the e.m.f. As the current increases, the lost volts (I r) increase and the terminal p.d. falls. A graph of terminal p.d. against current has a gradient equal to −r.

例题Example

一个电池接 4.0 Ω 电阻时端电压 1.6 V，电流 0.40 A。求电动势和内阻。A cell connected to a 4.0 Ω resistor gives a terminal p.d. of 1.6 V and a current of 0.40 A. Find its e.m.f. and internal resistance.
验算外电压：I R = 0.40 × 4.0 = 1.6 V ✓ 与端电压一致Check: I R = 0.40 × 4.0 = 1.6 V ✓ matches the terminal p.d. 总电阻 R + r = E / I，先求 E：需要另一个条件——用 E = I(R + r)，即 E = 0.40(4.0 + r)From E = I(R + r): E = 0.40(4.0 + r) 又 E = V + I r = 1.6 + 0.40 r。两式联立：0.40(4.0 + r) = 1.6 + 0.40 r → 1.6 + 0.40r = 1.6 + 0.40r，恒成立。Also E = V + I r = 1.6 + 0.40 r. Setting them equal: 0.40(4.0 + r) = 1.6 + 0.40 r, which is an identity. 说明只有一组数据求不出 E 和 r，必须两組不同 R 的数据（或给出 E）。这正是实验题的做法：改变 R，画 V–I 图，从截距读 E、斜率读 r。So one set of readings cannot fix both E and r — you need two different loads (or a given E). That is exactly the experiment: vary R, plot V against I, and read E from the intercept and r from the gradient.

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：把两种情况分别写成 E = V + I r，然后联立。Hint: write E = V + I r for both cases, then solve them together.

某电池接 2.0 Ω 时电流 0.50 A；接 5.0 Ω 时电流 0.25 A。求电动势 E 和内阻 r。A cell drives 0.50 A through a 2.0 Ω resistor, and 0.25 A through a 5.0 Ω resistor. Find the e.m.f. E and internal resistance r.
点开看答案Show answer
情况一：E = 0.50 (2.0 + r) = 1.0 + 0.50 rCase 1: E = 0.50 (2.0 + r) = 1.0 + 0.50 r 情况二：E = 0.25 (5.0 + r) = 1.25 + 0.25 rCase 2: E = 0.25 (5.0 + r) = 1.25 + 0.25 r 联立：1.0 + 0.50r = 1.25 + 0.25r → 0.25r = 0.25 → r = 1.0 ΩEquate: 1.0 + 0.50r = 1.25 + 0.25r → 0.25r = 0.25 → r = 1.0 Ω 代回：E = 1.0 + 0.50 × 1.0 = 1.5 VSubstitute back: E = 1.0 + 0.50 × 1.0 = 1.5 V
— 第 7 站完成 —— Stop 7 done —

单元 4 走完了 🎉Unit 4 done 🎉

记住这三句：I = Q/t，V = W/Q，V = IR；并联越并越小，总电阻一定小于最小支路；端电压 V = E − Ir，电流越大掉得越多。单位换算（mA、mm²）每次都先换再算。 Three sentences: I = Q/t, V = W/Q, V = IR; parallel totals are always smaller than the smallest branch; terminal p.d. V = E − Ir, dropping further as the current rises. Convert your units (mA, mm²) before you calculate, every time.
