点标题可以直接跳到那一站。这一单元背得多、算得少，第 3、4 站尤其值得反复看。Click a title to jump there. This unit is heavy on recall, light on algebra — stops 3 and 4 are worth reading twice.

1 原子长什么样：α 粒子散射实验What an atom looks like: α-particle scattering Atomic structure原子结构
学完你会：说清"为什么这个实验说明原子有个小而重的正电核"。By the end: you can explain why this experiment shows a small, heavy, positive nucleus.
说人话：在卢瑟福之前，人们以为原子是"布丁"——正电荷摊开成一团，电子像葡萄干嵌在里面。如果真是这样，射进去的 α 粒子应该都稍微拐一点点弯。结果是：绝大多数像穿过空气一样直穿，极少数像撞上墙一样弹回来。想通这句话，结论就出来了——原子内部几乎是空的，只有正中间有个又小、又重、带正电的核。 In plain words: before Rutherford, people pictured the atom as a plum pudding — positive charge spread out with electrons stuck in it. If that were true, every alpha particle would bend slightly. What actually happened: nearly all went straight through as if through air, and a few bounced back as if they had hit a wall. Once you accept that, the conclusion follows — the atom is almost entirely empty, with a tiny, heavy, positively charged nucleus at the centre.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • 原子直径约 10⁻¹⁰ m，原子核约 10⁻¹⁵ m——相差 10⁵ 倍。Atom ≈ 10⁻¹⁰ m, nucleus ≈ 10⁻¹⁵ m — a factor of 10⁵.
  • 核里几乎集中了全部质量和全部正电荷。The nucleus holds almost all the mass and all the positive charge.
  • 电子在核外很远的"空旷区"里运动。Electrons move in the vast empty space outside the nucleus.
🗣 考场英文怎么说How to say it in the exam Most α-particles pass straight through ⇒ the atom is mostly empty space.
A small number are deflected through large angles ⇒ the nucleus is positively charged.
Very few are scattered backwards ⇒ the nucleus is very small and contains most of the mass. （⇒ 读作 "so"，意思是"所以"。）

例题Example

原子直径约 1.0 × 10⁻¹⁰ m，核直径约 1.0 × 10⁻¹⁵ m。求两者体积之比（都当成球体）。An atom is about 1.0 × 10⁻¹⁰ m across and its nucleus about 1.0 × 10⁻¹⁵ m. Estimate the ratio of their volumes (treat both as spheres).
半径比 = (1.0×10⁻¹⁰) / (1.0×10⁻¹⁵) = 10⁵Ratio of radii = (1.0×10⁻¹⁰) / (1.0×10⁻¹⁵) = 10⁵ 体积比 = (半径比)³ = (10⁵)³ = 10¹⁵Ratio of volumes = (ratio of radii)³ = (10⁵)³ = 10¹⁵ 所以核只占原子体积的一千万亿分之一——原子确实是"空"的。So the nucleus takes up only one part in 10¹⁵ of the atom — the atom really is empty.

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：先把长度全部换成 m，再算半径比，最后立方。Hint: convert everything to metres, find the ratio of radii, then cube it.

原子核直径 8.0 × 10⁻¹⁵ m，原子直径 2.4 × 10⁻¹⁰ m。求体积比。A nucleus is 8.0 × 10⁻¹⁵ m across; the atom is 2.4 × 10⁻¹⁰ m across. Find the ratio of their volumes.
点开看答案Show answer
半径比 = 2.4×10⁻¹⁰ / 8.0×10⁻¹⁵ = 3.0 × 10⁴Ratio of radii = 2.4×10⁻¹⁰ / 8.0×10⁻¹⁵ = 3.0 × 10⁴ 体积比 = (3.0×10⁴)³ = 27 × 10¹² = 2.7 × 10¹³Ratio of volumes = (3.0×10⁴)³ = 27 × 10¹² = 2.7 × 10¹³
— 第 1 站完成 —— Stop 1 done —
2 核符号与同位素：A、Z、NNuclear notation and isotopes: A, Z, N Notation核符号
学完你会：看到任何一个核符号，立刻说出质子、中子、电子各有多少。By the end: given any nuclide symbol, you can state the number of protons, neutrons and electrons at once.
说人话：核符号就像一个人的"身份证 + 体重"。Z（质子数）是身份证——它决定这是哪种元素，6 号一定是碳，92 号一定是铀，改了就变成别的东西了。A（核子数）是体重——同一种元素可以胖一点瘦一点，也就是同位素：质子数一样、中子数不一样。化学性质由电子决定，而电子数等于质子数，所以同位素的化学性质几乎一模一样，差别只在"核稳不稳定"。 In plain words: a nuclide symbol is like an ID card plus a weight. Z is the ID — it decides which element you are: 6 is always carbon, 92 is always uranium. A is the weight — the same element can be a bit heavier or lighter, which gives isotopes: same protons, different neutrons. Chemistry depends on electrons, and electrons equal protons, so isotopes behave almost identically in chemistry. The only real difference is whether the nucleus is stable.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • A = Z + N（核子数 = 质子数 + 中子数）。(nucleons = protons + neutrons).
  • Z 决定元素，改 Z 就是"炼金术"——化学反应做不到。Z decides the element; changing it is transmutation, which chemistry cannot do.
  • 同位素：同 Z、异 N，化学性质相同，核性质不同。Isotopes: same Z, different N — same chemistry, different nuclear behaviour.
  • 1 u（原子质量单位）≈ 1.66 × 10⁻²⁷ kg。1 u (atomic mass unit) ≈ 1.66 × 10⁻²⁷ kg.

例题Example

写出 ²³⁸₉₂U 与 ²³⁵₉₂U 的中子数，并说明它们是什么关系。State the number of neutrons in uranium-238 and uranium-235, and say how the two are related.
N = A − Z：²³⁸U 有 238 − 92 = 146 个中子N = A − Z: U-238 has 238 − 92 = 146 neutrons ²³⁵U 有 235 − 92 = 143 个中子U-235 has 235 − 92 = 143 neutrons 质子数都是 92，中子数不同，所以它们是同位素。Both have 92 protons but different neutron counts, so they are isotopes.

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：中性原子的电子数 = 质子数 = Z。Hint: in a neutral atom, electrons = protons = Z.

对 ¹⁴₆C 和 ¹²₆C，分别写出质子数、中子数和电子数；若 ¹⁴₆C 变成一个负离子（多 1 个电子），电子数是多少？For carbon-14 and carbon-12, state the number of protons, neutrons and electrons. If carbon-14 gains one electron to become a negative ion, how many electrons does it have?
点开看答案Show answer
¹⁴₆C：质子 6，中子 14 − 6 = 8，电子 6C-14: 6 protons, 14 − 6 = 8 neutrons, 6 electrons ¹²₆C：质子 6，中子 6，电子 6C-12: 6 protons, 6 neutrons, 6 electrons 负离子：电子 = 6 + 1 = 7Negative ion: 6 + 1 = 7 electrons
— 第 2 站完成 —— Stop 2 done —
3 三种辐射：α、β、γThe three types of radiation: α, β, γ α, β, γ三种辐射
学完你会：背下三种辐射的对照表，并会用"纸 / 铝 / 铅"设计鉴别方案。By the end: you know the comparison table by heart and can identify a source using paper, aluminium and lead.
说人话：把辐射想象成三种不同"脾气"的球。α 是个又大又重的保龄球：一路撞倒所有东西（电离最强），但撞几次就没劲了，一张纸都过不去。β 是个乒乓球：轻一些，能飞过纸，被几毫米铝板挡住。γ 是光：几乎不跟物质打招呼（电离最弱），所以能穿很远，只能靠厚铅或混凝土"慢慢消耗"它。记住这条反比关系，题目怎么问都不怕。 In plain words: picture three balls with very different personalities. α is a heavy bowling ball: it knocks over everything it meets (strongly ionising) but runs out of energy almost at once — paper stops it. β is a table-tennis ball: lighter, gets past paper, stopped by a few millimetres of aluminium. γ is light: it barely interacts at all (weakly ionising), so it travels far and only thick lead or concrete gradually absorbs it. Remember this inverse relationship and you can answer any version of the question.
记住这几点（这张表值得整段背下来）：Remember these (this table is worth learning by heart):
  • α：氦核 ⁴₂He，带 +2e，穿透最弱（纸可挡），电离最强，磁场中略微偏转。: a helium nucleus ⁴₂He, charge +2e, weakest penetration (paper), strongest ionisation, deflected slightly in a magnetic field.
  • β⁻：高速电子 ⁰₋₁e，带 −1e，穿透中等（几 mm 铝），电离中等，磁场中偏转明显。: a fast electron ⁰₋₁e, charge −1e, medium penetration (a few mm of Al), medium ionisation, deflected strongly.
  • γ：电磁波（光子），不带电，穿透最强（厚铅/混凝土），电离最弱，磁场中不偏转。: electromagnetic radiation, no charge, greatest penetration (thick lead/concrete), weakest ionisation, not deflected by a magnetic field.
  • β⁺（正电子）在 A-Level 里只需要知道"它是电子的反粒子"。For A-Level you only need to know that β⁺ (a positron) is the electron's antiparticle.
特别提醒：Watch out: 题目问"哪种辐射最危险"要分场合：在体外，γ 最危险（能穿进身体）；一旦进入体内，α 最危险（电离最强，直接破坏细胞）。答题前先看清"source inside or outside the body"。 "Which is the most dangerous?" depends where the source is: outside the body, γ is worst because it reaches your organs; inside the body, α is worst because it ionises so strongly right next to your cells. Always check whether the question says inside or outside.

例题Example

某放射源：加一张纸后计数率几乎不变；再加 5 mm 铝板后计数率降到本底。这是什么辐射？A source: adding a sheet of paper barely changes the count rate; adding 5 mm of aluminium brings it down to background. What radiation is it?
纸挡不住 → 不是 α（α 会被纸挡住）。Paper does not stop it → not α (α would be absorbed). 5 mm 铝挡住 → 不是 γ（γ 需要厚铅）。5 mm of aluminium stops it → not γ (γ needs thick lead). 所以是 β 辐射。So it is β radiation.

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：想清楚每种辐射需要多长的"射程"和多少"阻挡"。Hint: think about the range each type needs, and what stops it.

工厂要测传送带上钢板的厚度，射线需要能穿过钢板、但厚度一变读数就明显变化。选 α、β、γ 中的哪一种？说明理由。A factory monitors the thickness of steel sheet on a conveyor. The radiation must pass through the sheet, but the reading must change noticeably when the thickness changes. Which type would you choose, and why?
点开看答案Show answer
选 β。Use β. α 连空气里走几厘米都不行，更穿不过钢板。α cannot even travel far in air, let alone through steel. γ 太容易穿过，厚度变化对读数影响很小，测不灵敏。γ passes through too easily, so the reading barely changes with thickness. β 刚好：能穿过薄钢板，厚度一变吸收量就明显变化。β is just right: it gets through thin steel, yet the absorption changes measurably with thickness.
— 第 3 站完成 —— Stop 3 done —
4 核衰变方程：守住两条Decay equations: two things must balance Decay equations衰变方程
学完你会：补全任何 α 或 β 衰变方程里的未知核。By the end: you can complete any α or β decay equation and identify the missing nuclide.
说人话：写衰变方程只有一个技巧——把等号两边的左上角分别加起来、左下角分别加起来，看是否相等。α 衰变就是"扔掉一个氦核"，所以 A 减 4、Z 减 2。β⁻ 衰变反直觉一点：核里有个中子受不了了，变成质子 + 电子，电子被踢出去，于是中子少一个、质子多一个，A 不变、Z 加 1。至于 γ，它只是"多余的能量以光的形式释放"，A 和 Z 都不动。 In plain words: there is only one trick: add up the top-left numbers on each side, then the bottom-left numbers, and check they match. α decay means throwing out a helium nucleus, so A drops by 4 and Z by 2. β⁻ decay is the counter-intuitive one: a neutron in the nucleus turns into a proton plus an electron, the electron is ejected, so one neutron becomes one proton — A unchanged, Z up by 1. γ is simply surplus energy leaving as light; A and Z stay put.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • α 衰变：A 减 4，Z 减 2。α decay: A − 4, Z − 2.
  • β⁻ 衰变：A 不变，Z 加 1；本质是 n → p + e⁻ + ν̄。β⁻ decay: A unchanged, Z + 1; it is n → p + e⁻ + ν̄.
  • γ 衰变：A、Z 都不变，只是能量降低。γ decay: neither A nor Z changes; only the energy drops.
  • 中微子 ν（和反中微子 ν̄）带着"失踪"的能量和动量，是守恒律逼出来的。The (anti)neutrino carries the "missing" energy and momentum — conservation laws force it to exist.
特别提醒：Watch out: 很多同学误以为 β 粒子是"核外电子被甩出去"。不是。核外电子是"绕轨道运行"的，核衰变产生的电子是现场从中子变出来的。考场上写 "the electron comes from the nucleus, not from the orbiting electrons" 能直接得分。 A very common mistake is thinking a β particle is an orbiting electron that got thrown out. It is not. The β electron is created at the moment a neutron decays. Writing "the electron is produced in the nucleus, not from the orbiting electrons" is a mark-earning sentence.

例题Example

²²⁶₈₈Ra 发生一次 α 衰变，然后产物再发生一次 β⁻ 衰变。写出最终的核。Radium-226 undergoes α decay, and the daughter then undergoes β⁻ decay. Identify the final nuclide.
α 后：A = 226 − 4 = 222，Z = 88 − 2 = 86 → ²²²₈₆RnAfter α: A = 226 − 4 = 222, Z = 88 − 2 = 86 → radon-222 再 β⁻：A 不变 222，Z = 86 + 1 = 87 → ²²²₈₇FrThen β⁻: A stays 222, Z = 86 + 1 = 87 → francium-222

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：β⁻ 让 Z 加 1，然后去元素周期表里找 84 号是谁。Hint: β⁻ increases Z by 1, then look up element 84.

²¹⁰₈₃Bi 发生 β⁻ 衰变，写出完整的核反应方程（含反中微子）。Bismuth-210 undergoes β⁻ decay. Write the full equation, including the antineutrino.
点开看答案Show answer
A 不变：210；Z：83 + 1 = 84 → 钋 PoA unchanged: 210; Z: 83 + 1 = 84 → polonium 方程：²¹⁰₈₃Bi → ²¹⁰₈₄Po + ⁰₋₁e + ν̄Equation: Bi-210 → Po-210 + β⁻ + ν̄ 检查：上 210 = 210 + 0 ✓；下 83 = 84 − 1 ✓Check: top 210 = 210 + 0 ✓; bottom 83 = 84 − 1 ✓
— 第 4 站完成 —— Stop 4 done —
5 衰变规律与半衰期Decay law and half-life Half-life半衰期
学完你会：用"数半衰期"的快速法，也会用 N = N₀e⁻λt 精确计算。By the end: you can both count half-lives for a quick answer and use N = N₀e⁻λt for an exact one.
说人话：半衰期就是"减半所需的时间"。它非常顽固——加热、加压、磨成粉、溶解，都没法让它变快或变慢，因为这是核内部的事，跟核外电子（化学反应那一层）完全无关。做题有两条路：快速法——数经历了几个半衰期，就连续除以几个 2；精确法——用 N = N₀e⁻λt，其中 λ = ln2 / t½。考试里若给的时间不是半衰期的整数倍，就必须用精确法。 In plain words: the half-life is "the time it takes to halve". It is remarkably stubborn — heating, pressurising, grinding or dissolving makes no difference, because decay is a nuclear process, nothing to do with the outer electrons where chemistry happens. There are two ways to answer: the quick way — count how many half-lives have passed and keep dividing by 2; the exact way — N = N₀e⁻λt, with λ = ln 2 / t½. If the time in the question is not a whole number of half-lives, you must use the exact method.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • 定义：半衰期 = 样品中未衰变核数（或活度）减半所需的时间。Definition: the half-life is the time for the number of undecayed nuclei (or the activity) to halve.
  • N = N₀ e⁻λt，活度、计数率都遵循同一条规律。, and the activity and count rate follow exactly the same law.
  • λ = ln 2 / t½，λ 叫衰变常数，单位是 s⁻¹。, where λ is the decay constant, measured in s⁻¹.
  • 衰变是随机的：对单个原子核无法预测，对大量原子核才能统计。Decay is random: unpredictable for one nucleus, statistical for many.
🗣 考场英文怎么说How to say it in the exam Decay is a random process: we cannot predict when a particular nucleus will decay.
The half-life is the time taken for the number of undecayed nuclei to halve.
The half-life is unaffected by temperature, pressure or chemical state.

例题Example

某放射性样品半衰期 8.0 天，初始活度 400 Bq。求 24 天后的活度。A sample has a half-life of 8.0 days and an initial activity of 400 Bq. Find its activity after 24 days.
24 / 8.0 = 3 个半衰期24 / 8.0 = 3 half-lives 400 → 200 → 100 → 50 Bq（也可写 400 × (½)³ = 50 Bq）400 → 200 → 100 → 50 Bq (equivalently 400 × (½)³ = 50 Bq)

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：先算经过几个半衰期，再用 λ = ln2/t½ 和 e⁻λt 验证一下。Hint: count the half-lives first, then check with λ = ln2/t½ and e⁻λt.

碳-14 的半衰期是 5730 年。一块木头里碳-14 的活度降到新鲜木头的 1/8，这块木头大约存在了多少年？Carbon-14 has a half-life of 5730 years. The carbon-14 activity in a piece of wood has fallen to 1/8 of that in fresh wood. How old is the wood?
点开看答案Show answer
1/8 = (½)³ → 经过了 3 个半衰期1/8 = (½)³ → 3 half-lives have passed t = 3 × 5730 = 1.72 × 10⁴ 年（约 17190 年）t = 3 × 5730 = 1.72 × 10⁴ years (about 17 190 years)
— 第 5 站完成 —— Stop 5 done —
6 质量亏损与 E = mc²Mass defect and E = mc² E = mc²质能方程
学完你会：算质量亏损，并在 kg → J 与 u → MeV 两条路上自由换算。By the end: you can find the mass defect and convert freely between kg → J and u → MeV.
说人话：爱因斯坦最著名的一句话，说的是质量和能量是同一种东西的两种叫法。核反应后称一称，发现产物比原来轻了一丁点，这"少掉的质量"并没有消失，而是整包变成了释放出来的能量。因为 c² 是个巨大的数（9 × 10¹⁶），所以一丁点质量就能换出巨大能量——这就是核电站和原子弹的底气。做题时记住两个换算捷径：1 u 对应 931.5 MeV，1 eV = 1.6 × 10⁻¹⁹ J。 In plain words: Einstein's famous line says that mass and energy are two names for the same thing. Weigh the products of a nuclear reaction and they are a shade lighter than the reactants; that missing mass has not gone anywhere, it has been converted wholesale into released energy. Because c² is enormous (9 × 10¹⁶), a tiny mass buys a huge amount of energy — that is the basis of both nuclear power stations and nuclear weapons. Two short cuts for exams: 1 u is worth 931.5 MeV, and 1 eV = 1.6 × 10⁻¹⁹ J.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • E = Δm c²，Δm 必须用 kg，c = 3.00 × 10⁸ m/s。, with Δm in kg and c = 3.00 × 10⁸ m/s.
  • 换算：1 u = 1.66 × 10⁻²⁷ kg = 931.5 MeV。Conversions: 1 u = 1.66 × 10⁻²⁷ kg = 931.5 MeV.
  • 1 eV = 1.60 × 10⁻¹⁹ J（一个电子通过 1 伏特电压获得的能量）。1 eV = 1.60 × 10⁻¹⁹ J (the energy one electron gains across 1 volt).
  • Δm > 0 → 放能；Δm < 0 → 吸能。Δm > 0 → energy is released; Δm < 0 → energy must be supplied.

例题Example

某核反应的质量亏损为 3.0 × 10⁻³ u。求释放的能量，分别用 MeV 和 J 表示。A nuclear reaction has a mass defect of 3.0 × 10⁻³ u. Find the energy released, in MeV and in J.
用捷径：E = 3.0×10⁻³ × 931.5 = 2.79 MeV ≈ 2.8 MeVShort cut: E = 3.0×10⁻³ × 931.5 = 2.79 MeV ≈ 2.8 MeV 换成焦耳：2.79 × 10⁶ eV × 1.60×10⁻¹⁹ J/eV = 4.47 × 10⁻¹³ JIn joules: 2.79 × 10⁶ eV × 1.60×10⁻¹⁹ J/eV = 4.47 × 10⁻¹³ J

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：先用 1 u = 1.66×10⁻²⁷ kg 换成 kg，再用 E = Δm c²。Hint: convert to kg using 1 u = 1.66×10⁻²⁷ kg first, then use E = Δm c².

质量亏损 2.0 × 10⁻³ u。求 Δm（kg）和释放的能量 E（J）。The mass defect is 2.0 × 10⁻³ u. Find Δm in kg and the energy released in J.
点开看答案Show answer
Δm = 2.0×10⁻³ × 1.66×10⁻²⁷ = 3.32 × 10⁻³⁰ kgΔm = 2.0×10⁻³ × 1.66×10⁻²⁷ = 3.32 × 10⁻³⁰ kg E = Δm c² = 3.32×10⁻³⁰ × (3.0×10⁸)² = 3.32×10⁻³⁰ × 9.0×10¹⁶E = Δm c² = 3.32×10⁻³⁰ × (3.0×10⁸)² = 3.32×10⁻³⁰ × 9.0×10¹⁶ E = 2.99 × 10⁻¹³ J（约 3.0 × 10⁻¹³ J，也等于 1.86 MeV）E = 2.99 × 10⁻¹³ J (≈ 3.0 × 10⁻¹³ J, or 1.86 MeV)
— 第 6 站完成 —— Stop 6 done —
7 结合能、裂变与聚变Binding energy, fission and fusion Binding energy结合能
学完你会：解释为什么裂变和聚变都能放能，并会算每核子结合能。By the end: you can explain why both fission and fusion release energy, and calculate binding energy per nucleon.
说人话：结合能就是"把原子核拆成一堆散零件需要的能量"，反过来说也是"这些零件凑成核时放出的能量"。结合得越紧，能量越低，也就越稳。把"每个核子平均摊到多少结合能"画成图，就得到那条著名的曲线：铁-56 附近最高（约 8.8 MeV），两边都低。于是自然界只有两条"下坡路"：轻核聚在一起（聚变，太阳在干的事），重核裂成两半（裂变，核电站在干的事）——两者都是往山顶走，走上去多出来的能量就放出来。 In plain words: the binding energy is the energy needed to pull a nucleus apart into separate nucleons — or, looking the other way, the energy released when those nucleons come together. The more tightly bound, the lower the energy, and the more stable the nucleus. Plot the binding energy per nucleon and you get the famous curve: a peak near iron-56 (about 8.8 MeV), lower at both ends. So nature has only two downhill routes: light nuclei joining up (fusion, what the Sun does) and heavy nuclei splitting in two (fission, what a power station does). Both move towards the peak, and the extra binding energy is released on the way.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • 结合能 = 把核拆散所需能量 = Δm c²。Binding energy = energy to separate the nucleons = Δm c².
  • 每核子结合能 = 结合能 ÷ A，越大越稳定；铁-56 最大。Binding energy per nucleon = binding energy ÷ A; bigger means more stable; iron-56 is the maximum.
  • 裂变：重核（如 ²³⁵U 吸收一个中子）裂成两个中等核 + 几个中子。Fission: a heavy nucleus (e.g. U-235 absorbing a neutron) splits into two medium nuclei plus neutrons.
  • 聚变：轻核（如氘 + 氚）合成较重的核，需要极高温度克服斥力。Fusion: light nuclei (e.g. deuterium + tritium) join together, needing extreme temperature to overcome repulsion.
特别提醒：Watch out: 别把"结合能大"和"结合能/核子大"混为一谈。铀的总结合能比铁大得多，但摊到 235 个核子上就不如铁紧，所以铀不稳定、能裂变。比的是人均，不是总量。 Do not confuse total binding energy with binding energy per nucleon. Uranium has a far larger total binding energy than iron, but shared over 235 nucleons it is less tightly bound, which is why uranium can release energy by fission. Compare the per-nucleon figure, not the total.

例题Example

⁴₂He 的质量亏损是 0.0304 u。求它的总结合能（MeV）和每核子结合能（MeV）。The mass defect of a helium-4 nucleus is 0.0304 u. Find its binding energy in MeV, and the binding energy per nucleon.
E = 0.0304 × 931.5 = 28.3 MeVE = 0.0304 × 931.5 = 28.3 MeV A = 4，所以每核子 = 28.3 / 4 = 7.08 MeVA = 4, so per nucleon = 28.3 / 4 = 7.08 MeV

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：先算总结合能，再除以核子数 A。Hint: find the total binding energy, then divide by the number of nucleons.

某核的总结合能为 492 MeV，核子数 A = 56，质量亏损折合 0.528 u。求每核子结合能，并判断它比氦-4 稳定还是更不稳定。A nucleus has total binding energy 492 MeV with A = 56. Calculate the binding energy per nucleon and say whether it is more or less stable than helium-4 (7.08 MeV per nucleon).
点开看答案Show answer
每核子 = 492 / 56 = 8.79 MeVPer nucleon = 492 / 56 = 8.79 MeV 8.79 > 7.08，所以更稳定（这正是铁-56 附近，全表最高）。8.79 > 7.08, so it is more stable (this is the region around iron-56, the top of the curve).

单元 5 收尾 · 三句话带走Unit 5 wrap-up · three sentences

① 核符号里 A = Z + N，改 Z 就换了元素，只改 N 就是同位素。1. A = Z + N in a nuclide symbol: change Z and you change the element, change only N and you have an isotope.

② α 是氦核、β 是电子、γ 是光；穿透力与电离能力正好相反。2. α is a helium nucleus, β is an electron, γ is light; penetrating power and ionising power are opposites.

③ 半衰期数着除 2，Δm 乘 931.5 就是 MeV，铁-56 是结合能的山顶。3. Count half-lives and divide by 2; multiply Δm by 931.5 to get MeV; iron-56 is the summit of the binding energy curve.

这一单元几乎全是"背了就得分"的内容，考前把第 3 站的对照表和第 4 站的两条铁律再过一遍。This unit is almost pure recall — reread the comparison table in stop 3 and the two golden rules in stop 4 before the exam.

1 有效数字、单位与前缀Significant figures, units and prefixes Units单位
学完你会：数出有效数字位数、熟练换算前缀，并知道答案该写几位。By the end: you can count significant figures, convert prefixes confidently, and choose how many figures to give.
说人话：有效数字就是"这句话里有几个字是真的"。前面用来定位小数点的 0 不算数（0.0045 的"信息量"全在 45 上），末尾的 0 必须算——1.20 表示"我量到了 1.20，不是 1.2"，这是两种不同的把握程度。考试中最实用的规则：答案的位数跟着题目数据里最少的那一个走，通常 2～3 位，中间过程多留一位。 In plain words: significant figures tell you how much of the number to trust. Zeros at the front that only place the decimal point do not count (all the information in 0.0045 is in the 45), but zeros at the end do count — 1.20 says "I measured 1.20, not 1.2", which is a different claim. The most useful exam rule: match the fewest significant figures in the data, usually 2 or 3, keeping an extra figure part-way through.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • 科学计数法最省事：1.20 × 10³ 一眼看出 3 位有效数字。Standard form is clearest: 1.20 × 10³ shows 3 s.f. at a glance.
  • 前缀：p n μ m · k M G，考试不会提供换算表。Prefixes: p n μ m · k M G — the exam will not give you the table.
  • 中间计算不要提前四舍五入，最后一步再修约。Do not round in the middle of a calculation; round only at the end.
  • 答案没写单位 = 白做；梯度的单位是 y 轴单位 / x 轴单位。No unit means no marks; a gradient's unit is y-unit / x-unit.

例题Example

把 4.50 × 10⁻³ A 写成 mA，并说明它有几位有效数字。Write 4.50 × 10⁻³ A in mA, and state how many significant figures it has.
1 mA = 10⁻³ A，所以 4.50 × 10⁻³ A = 4.50 mA1 mA = 10⁻³ A, so 4.50 × 10⁻³ A = 4.50 mA 4、5、0 共 3 位有效数字（末尾的 0 要算）。4, 5, 0 gives 3 s.f. (the trailing zero counts).

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：先统一成基本单位（m、kg、s、A），再带入公式。Hint: convert everything to base units (m, kg, s, A) before substituting.

一根金属丝长 850 mm，横截面积 0.020 mm²，电阻 4.3 Ω。求电阻率 ρ = RA / L（单位 Ω·m），答案保留 2 位有效数字。A wire is 850 mm long with cross-sectional area 0.020 mm² and resistance 4.3 Ω. Find the resistivity ρ = RA / L in Ω·m, to 2 significant figures.
点开看答案Show answer
L = 850 mm = 0.850 m；A = 0.020 mm² = 0.020 × 10⁻⁶ m² = 2.0 × 10⁻⁸ m²L = 850 mm = 0.850 m; A = 0.020 mm² = 0.020 × 10⁻⁶ m² = 2.0 × 10⁻⁸ m² ρ = 4.3 × 2.0×10⁻⁸ / 0.850 = 1.0 × 10⁻⁷ Ω·mρ = 4.3 × 2.0×10⁻⁸ / 0.850 = 1.0 × 10⁻⁷ Ω·m （精确值 1.0118…，保留 2 位为 1.0 × 10⁻⁷ Ω·m）(exact value 1.0118…, i.e. 1.0 × 10⁻⁷ Ω·m to 2 s.f.)
— 第 1 站完成 —— Stop 1 done —
2 常用仪器怎么读Reading the instruments Instruments仪器
学完你会：说出每种仪器的精度，并在读数时避开视差与零误差。By the end: you can quote the precision of each instrument and avoid parallax and zero errors.
说人话：物理实验的第一条常识是先看清你的尺子有多粗。用最小刻度 1 mm 的尺子量一根 5 cm 的弹簧，误差就是 2%；换成螺旋测微器量金属丝直径（0.5 mm 左右），误差才 2%。所以：量长度大、精度要求低的用尺；量厚度、小直径用螺旋测微器；介于两者之间（外径、内径、深度）用游标卡尺。另外两条容易丢分：眼睛要正对刻度（斜看会产生视差），用完先合拢看是不是 0（零误差要在所有读数里统一扣掉）。 In plain words: the first rule of practical physics is know how blunt your ruler is. Measuring a 5 cm spring with a millimetre rule gives a 2% error; measuring a 0.5 mm wire with a micrometer also gives 2%. So: use a rule for long lengths where precision matters little; a micrometer for thickness and small diameters; a vernier caliper in between (external, internal, depth). Two more easy marks to lose: keep your eye level with the scale (viewing at an angle causes parallax) and close the jaws first to check the zero (a zero error must be subtracted from every reading).
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • 米尺 ±1 mm；游标卡尺 ±0.1 mm（有的 ±0.02 mm）；螺旋测微器 ±0.01 mm。Metre rule ±1 mm; vernier caliper ±0.1 mm (sometimes ±0.02 mm); micrometer ±0.01 mm.
  • 测薄物时多圈并测：测 10 张纸的厚度再除以 10，误差被摊薄。For thin objects, measure many at once: measure 10 sheets and divide by 10, spreading the error.
  • 零误差是一种系统误差，取再多次平均也消不掉。A zero error is systematic — no amount of averaging will remove it.
  • 测周期时数 20 个周期再除，把反应时间摊到 1/20。Time 20 oscillations and divide, spreading the reaction-time error by 20.

例题Example

用米尺测一根金属丝长度，两端读数分别为 1.2 cm 和 98.7 cm。求长度及百分比不确定度（单次读数误差 ±1 mm）。A wire is measured with a metre rule: the ends read 1.2 cm and 98.7 cm. Find the length and the percentage uncertainty (each reading has an error of ±1 mm).
L = 98.7 − 1.2 = 97.5 cmL = 98.7 − 1.2 = 97.5 cm 两个端点各 ±1 mm，相减时绝对不确定度相加：ΔL = 1 + 1 = 2 mm = 0.2 cmEach end has ±1 mm; subtracting adds the absolute uncertainties: ΔL = 1 + 1 = 2 mm = 0.2 cm 百分比 = 0.2 / 97.5 × 100% = 0.21%Percentage = 0.2 / 97.5 × 100% = 0.21%

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：合并时绝对不确定度相加，最后换算成百分比。Hint: add the absolute uncertainties first, then turn the total into a percentage.

用螺旋测微器测金属丝直径，读数为 0.52 mm，仪器精度 ±0.01 mm。求百分比不确定度；若要把它降到 1% 以下，仪器精度至少要多高？A micrometer reads a wire diameter of 0.52 mm with a precision of ±0.01 mm. Find the percentage uncertainty. To bring it below 1%, how precise must the instrument be?
点开看答案Show answer
百分比 = 0.01 / 0.52 × 100% = 1.9%Percentage = 0.01 / 0.52 × 100% = 1.9% 要小于 1%：Δd < 0.01 × 0.52 = 0.0052 mmFor less than 1%: Δd < 0.01 × 0.52 = 0.0052 mm 即精度要优于 0.005 mm（普通螺旋测微器做不到，所以要"绕 20 圈并测"这类办法）。So you need better than 0.005 mm — an ordinary micrometer cannot do it, which is why we resort to tricks like winding 20 turns and measuring the total.
— 第 2 站完成 —— Stop 2 done —
3 误差：随机 vs 系统Errors: random vs systematic Errors误差
学完你会：判断一个误差是随机还是系统，并说出对应的补救办法。By the end: you can classify an error as random or systematic and say how to deal with it.
说人话：把测量想象成打靶。随机误差像手抖：这一枪偏左、下一枪偏右，但多打几枪、取平均位置，就越来越接近靶心——所以多次测量取平均是它的解药。系统误差像瞄歪了的准星：每一枪都很整齐，但整体偏到一边去了，这时候再怎么平均都没用，必须修仪器、校零点、改方法。考试只要问"如何减小误差"，先分清是哪一类再答。 In plain words: think of measuring as shooting at a target. Random error is a shaky hand: one shot left, the next right, but the average of many shots closes in on the bullseye — so repeat and average is the cure. Systematic error is a misaligned sight: every shot lands neatly, but all of them off to one side. Averaging will not help at all; you must fix the instrument, correct the zero, or change the method. When a question asks how to reduce error, classify it first, then answer.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • 随机误差 → 结果上下波动 → 解药是重复测量取平均、画最佳拟合线。Random error → readings scatter either side → cure: repeat and average, draw a best-fit line.
  • 系统误差 → 结果一致偏向一边 → 解药是校准、扣零误差、改实验设计。Systematic error → results all shift the same way → cure: calibrate, correct the zero, redesign.
  • 准确度 accuracy = 离真值多近；精密度 precision = 数据多集中。Accuracy = closeness to the true value; precision = how tightly the readings agree.
  • "误差 error" ≠ "错误 mistake"：读错数、算错数是错误，不在误差讨论范围内。An error is not a mistake: misreading or miscalculating is a blunder, not an error in this sense.
🗣 考场英文怎么说How to say it in the exam A random error causes readings to be spread about the true value; it can be reduced by repeating and averaging.
A systematic error causes all readings to be shifted by the same amount; repeating does not reduce it — the instrument must be recalibrated or the zero error corrected.

例题Example

判断下列情况属于随机误差还是系统误差：(a) 每次读尺时眼睛位置略有不同；(b) 游标卡尺合拢时读数为 +0.03 mm；(c) 用天平前没有调零。Classify each as random or systematic: (a) eye position varies slightly between readings; (b) the caliper reads +0.03 mm when closed; (c) the balance was not zeroed before use.
(a) 随机误差——每次偏的方向不同，多测几次取平均可减小。(a) random — it varies in direction, so repeating and averaging helps. (b) 系统误差（零误差）——每个读数都多 0.03 mm，要从结果中扣除。(b) systematic (zero error) — every reading is 0.03 mm too big; subtract it. (c) 系统误差——所有质量读数一致偏大或偏小。(c) systematic — every mass reading is shifted the same way.

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：问自己一句——"多测几次取平均，能不能让它变小？"Hint: ask yourself, "would repeating and averaging make this smaller?"

用单摆测 g 时，把摆长量成了"从悬点到球心"的距离，但漏掉了摆球半径。这属于哪类误差？会让 g 偏大还是偏小？Measuring g with a pendulum, the length is taken from the support to the top of the bob, forgetting the bob's radius. What type of error is this, and does it make g too large or too small?
点开看答案Show answer
属于系统误差——每一次都少算了同一个半径，方向固定。It is systematic — the same radius is missed every time, always in the same direction. L 偏小，由 g = 4π²L / T² 可知 g 偏小。L is too small, and since g = 4π²L / T², g comes out too small. 改进：量到球心（悬点到球顶 + 半径），或量"绳长 + 球直径/2"。Fix: measure to the centre of the bob (support to top + radius).
— 第 3 站完成 —— Stop 3 done —
4 不确定度怎么算Working out uncertainty Uncertainty不确定度
学完你会：给任何计算结果配上不确定度，并找出"误差主要来自哪一项"。By the end: you can attach an uncertainty to any calculated result and spot which measurement dominates the error.
说人话：不确定度就是"这个答案大概能差多少"。两个量相加相减时，误差按绝对量叠加（两个 ±1 mm 相减变成 ±2 mm）；相乘相除时，误差按百分比叠加（2% 乘上 3% 变成 5%）。它最有价值的地方不是最后那个 ±，而是能告诉你该先去改进哪一项——百分比最大的那个量就是瓶颈，把别的量做到 0.1% 也白搭。 In plain words: the uncertainty says roughly how far your answer might be off. When you add or subtract, absolute uncertainties add (±1 mm minus ±1 mm gives ±2 mm); when you multiply or divide, percentage uncertainties add (2% times 3% gives 5%). The real value is not the final ± but what it tells you: the term with the largest percentage is the bottleneck, and improving any other measurement to 0.1% achieves nothing.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • 单次读数：Δx = 仪器精度（如 ±1 mm、±0.01 mm）。Single reading: Δx = the instrument's precision (±1 mm, ±0.01 mm, …).
  • 多次读数：Δx = (最大值 − 最小值) / 2。Repeated readings: Δx = (max − min) / 2.
  • 加减 → 绝对不确定度相加；乘除 → 百分比不确定度相加；次方 → 百分比 × 指数。Add/subtract → add absolute; multiply/divide → add percentages; powers → percentage × index.
  • 结果写成 x = 值 ± Δ值 单位，两者的小数位数要对齐（如 1.23 ± 0.04 m）。Write the result as x = value ± Δvalue unit, with matching decimal places (e.g. 1.23 ± 0.04 m).
特别提醒：Watch out: "取平均"能减小随机误差带来的不确定度，但仪器精度那一项不会因为多测几次而消失。用"一半极差"算出的 Δx 如果小于仪器精度，就老老实实写仪器精度。 Averaging reduces the uncertainty from random scatter, but it cannot remove the instrument's precision. If half the range comes out smaller than the instrument precision, quote the instrument precision.

例题Example

用 ρ = m / V 求密度：m = 24.0 g ± 0.1 g，V = 8.0 cm³ ± 0.2 cm³。求 ρ 及其百分比不确定度。Find the density from ρ = m / V with m = 24.0 g ± 0.1 g and V = 8.0 cm³ ± 0.2 cm³, and give the percentage uncertainty.
ρ = 24.0 / 8.0 = 3.0 g/cm³ρ = 24.0 / 8.0 = 3.0 g/cm³ %m = 0.1 / 24.0 × 100% = 0.42%；%V = 0.2 / 8.0 × 100% = 2.5%%m = 0.1 / 24.0 × 100% = 0.42%; %V = 0.2 / 8.0 × 100% = 2.5% %ρ = 0.42% + 2.5% = 2.9%；绝对不确定度 = 0.029 × 3.0 = 0.09 g/cm³%ρ = 0.42% + 2.5% = 2.9%; absolute = 0.029 × 3.0 = 0.09 g/cm³ 结果：ρ = 3.00 ± 0.09 g/cm³；瓶颈是体积，该先换更准的量筒。Result: ρ = 3.00 ± 0.09 g/cm³; the bottleneck is the volume, so improve that first.

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：r 带平方，指数 2 要乘进百分比；π 是常数，不算误差。Hint: r is squared, so multiply its percentage by 2; π is a constant and carries no uncertainty.

金属丝直径 d = 0.52 mm ± 0.01 mm，长度 L = 0.850 m ± 0.001 m。求横截面积 A = πd²/4 的百分比不确定度。A wire has diameter d = 0.52 mm ± 0.01 mm. Find the percentage uncertainty in the cross-sectional area A = πd²/4.
点开看答案Show answer
%d = 0.01 / 0.52 × 100% = 1.9%%d = 0.01 / 0.52 × 100% = 1.9% A ∝ d²，所以 %A = 2 × 1.9% = 3.8%A ∝ d², so %A = 2 × 1.9% = 3.8% π 和 4 是精确常数，不带不确定度，不用管。π and 4 are exact constants and carry no uncertainty.
— 第 4 站完成 —— Stop 4 done —
5 作图的规矩Rules for drawing graphs Graphs作图
学完你会：按评分标准画出一张能拿满分的图，并正确求出梯度。By the end: you can produce a full-marks graph and find its gradient correctly.
说人话：图是给阅卷老师看的第一印象，规矩比内容还重要。轴上必须写"物理量 / 单位"；刻度要选得让点铺满大半张纸（不要用 1、2、3 这种浪费纸的刻度，也不必非从 0 开始）；点画成细细的叉或小点加圈，别涂成大圆团；然后画一条从点中间穿过的直线，而不是把点连成折线。求梯度时，在拟合线上取相距尽量远的两点（至少占半张纸），而不是拿原始数据点——这是最常被扣分的一条。 In plain words: the graph is the examiner's first impression, so the rules matter as much as the physics. Label each axis "quantity / unit"; choose scales so the points fill most of the page (no need to start at zero); plot small neat crosses, not fat blobs; then draw one straight line through the middle of the points rather than joining them up. When finding the gradient, pick two points far apart on your line (at least half the page), not the raw data points — this is the most commonly penalised step.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • 横轴 = 自变量（你主动改变的量），纵轴 = 因变量（随之改变的量）。Horizontal = the independent variable (what you change), vertical = the dependent variable (what responds).
  • 刻度用 1、2、5 的整倍数（1、2、5、10、20…），别用 3、7 这种难读的。Use scales in steps of 1, 2 or 5 (×10ⁿ) — never 3 or 7.
  • 有误差棒就画上，它表示该点的不确定度，拟合线应穿过大多数误差棒。Draw error bars when you have them; the line should pass through most bars.
  • 明显的异常点要圈出来并说明，但不要擅自删掉重画。Circle any clear anomalous point and comment on it — do not quietly delete it.

例题Example

一张 V–I 图上，最佳拟合线经过 (0.20 A, 1.10 V) 和 (0.80 A, 4.30 V) 两点。求梯度及其物理含义。On a V–I graph the best-fit line passes through (0.20 A, 1.10 V) and (0.80 A, 4.30 V). Find the gradient and state what it represents.
梯度 = (4.30 − 1.10) / (0.80 − 0.20) = 3.20 / 0.60 = 5.33 V/AGradient = (4.30 − 1.10) / (0.80 − 0.20) = 3.20 / 0.60 = 5.33 V/A V/A 就是 Ω，由 V = IR 可知梯度 = 电阻 R ≈ 5.3 Ω。V/A is Ω, so by V = IR the gradient is the resistance, R ≈ 5.3 Ω.

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：先确定"谁跟着谁变"——长度是你改的，周期是测出来的。Hint: decide which quantity responds — you change the length, you measure the period.

用单摆测 g 时，画 T²–L 图得到一条过原点的直线，梯度约 4.0 s²/m。求 g，并说明为什么画 T² 而不是画 T。Measuring g with a pendulum, a graph of T² against L is a straight line through the origin with gradient 4.0 s²/m. Find g, and explain why we plot T² rather than T.
点开看答案Show answer
由 T = 2π√(L/g) 得 T² = (4π²/g) L，梯度 = 4π²/gFrom T = 2π√(L/g) we get T² = (4π²/g) L, so the gradient is 4π²/g g = 4π² / 4.0 = 39.48 / 4.0 = 9.87 m/s²g = 4π² / 4.0 = 39.48 / 4.0 = 9.87 m/s² 画 T² 是为了把它变成直线：T 对 L 是平方根曲线，弯的线没法求梯度，也没法判断正误。We plot T² to turn it into a straight line: T against L is a square-root curve, and you cannot take a meaningful gradient from a curve.
— 第 5 站完成 —— Stop 5 done —
6 用梯度与截距求物理量Finding a quantity from gradient or intercept y = mx + c直线化
学完你会：把任意直线关系翻译成"梯度等于什么"，并反解出物理量。By the end: you can translate any straight-line relationship into "the gradient equals …" and solve for the quantity.
说人话：这一站是整个实验部分的"万能钥匙"。原理只有一句：只要把公式摆成 y = mx + c，m 和 c 就自动变成了你想要的物理量的某种组合。比如 V = E − Ir：把 V 当 y、I 当 x，那么截距就是电动势 E，梯度的绝对值就是内阻 r（负号表示电流越大、端电压越低）。考试最舒服的地方在于：你不用先知道答案，画完直线答案就写在图上了。 In plain words: this stop is the skeleton key to the whole practical paper. The idea is one line: once the formula reads y = mx + c, m and c automatically become combinations of the quantity you want. Take V = E − Ir: with V as y and I as x, the intercept is the e.m.f. E and the magnitude of the gradient is the internal resistance r (the minus sign says the terminal p.d. falls as the current rises). The pleasant part is that you do not need to know the answer in advance — draw the line and it tells you.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • 先变形再画图：公式里有根号、平方、分式的，先想办法拉直。Rearrange before plotting: if the formula has roots, squares or fractions, linearise it first.
  • 截距 c 必须看坐标轴是否从 0 开始，横轴不从 0 起时截距要小心。When reading the intercept, check whether the axis starts at zero.
  • 梯度 = Δy / Δx，用线上相距远的两点，并带上单位。Gradient = Δy / Δx, from two widely separated points on the line, with its unit.
  • 过原点说明成正比；不过原点往往代表有个初始偏置（如零误差）。Through the origin means proportional; a non-zero intercept often signals a systematic offset such as a zero error.

例题Example

测电池内阻实验得到 V = 1.50 − 0.80 I（V 单位 V，I 单位 A）。求电动势 E 和内阻 r，并说明 I = 0.5 A 时的端电压。An experiment gives V = 1.50 − 0.80 I (V in volts, I in amps). Find the e.m.f. and internal resistance, and the terminal p.d. at I = 0.5 A.
对比 V = E − Ir：E = 1.50 V，r = 0.80 ΩComparing with V = E − Ir: E = 1.50 V, r = 0.80 Ω I = 0.5 A 时：V = 1.50 − 0.80 × 0.5 = 1.10 VAt I = 0.5 A: V = 1.50 − 0.80 × 0.5 = 1.10 V

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：先把 F = kx + c 与实际情形对应，看看 c 是什么含义。Hint: match the situation to y = mx + c and decide what c means.

弹簧挂重物后总长度 L 与所挂质量 m 满足 L = 0.120 + 0.024 m（L 单位 m，m 单位 kg）。求弹簧原长与劲度系数 k（取 g = 9.8 m/s²）。A spring's length L relates to the load m by L = 0.120 + 0.024 m (L in m, m in kg). Find the unstretched length and the spring constant k (take g = 9.8 m/s²).
点开看答案Show answer
m = 0 时 L = 0.120 m，所以原长 = 0.120 mAt m = 0, L = 0.120 m, so the unstretched length is 0.120 m 梯度 0.024 m/kg，即每挂 1 kg 伸长 0.024 mGradient 0.024 m/kg: each kilogram stretches it by 0.024 m F = mg = 1 × 9.8 = 9.8 N 时伸长 0.024 m → k = F / x = 9.8 / 0.024 = 408 N/mWith F = mg = 9.8 N giving x = 0.024 m: k = F / x = 9.8 / 0.024 = 408 N/m
— 第 6 站完成 —— Stop 6 done —
7 实验设计与改进（6 分题套路）Designing and improving an experiment Planning设计
学完你会：独立写出一份完整的实验方案，并针对具体问题给出有效的改进建议。By the end: you can write a complete plan on your own and suggest improvements that address specific errors.
说人话：实验设计题不是考你"有没有灵感"，而是考你有没有清单。先把变量分三类：自变量（只有它变）、因变量（你去测它）、控制变量（其他统统按住不动）。然后照清单往下写：装置怎么搭、每个量用什么仪器测、取至少 6 组数据、重复取平均、画图标误差棒、最后说安全。至于"改进"，必须指着具体的误差说——"数 20 个周期再除"针对反应时间，"量到球心"针对系统误差，而"我会更小心"等于没说。 In plain words: the planning question does not test inspiration, it tests whether you have a checklist. Start by sorting the variables: the independent variable (the only thing you change), the dependent variable (what you measure), and the control variables (everything else, held firmly still). Then work down the list: how the apparatus is set up, which instrument measures each quantity, at least six data sets, repeats to average, a graph with error bars, and finally safety. As for improvements, name the specific error: "time 20 oscillations and divide" tackles reaction time, "measure to the centre of the bob" tackles a systematic error, and "be more careful" says nothing at all.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • 变量三分类：自变量 / 因变量 / 控制变量，写方案时逐一交代。Sort the variables into independent / dependent / controlled and state each one.
  • 数据至少 6 组，覆盖范围尽量大（够画一条可信的直线）。Take at least six sets of data over as wide a range as possible.
  • 能画图就画图：图比表格更能说明规律，也更容易拿梯度分。Plot a graph wherever you can: it shows the pattern better than a table and earns gradient marks.
  • 安全要具体：护目镜、配重防倒、夹稳、别让重物砸脚、热源隔离。Make safety specific: goggles, counterweights, firm clamping, keeping feet clear, shielding heat sources.
🗣 考场英文怎么说How to say it in the exam Independent variable: … ; dependent variable: … ; keep … , … and … constant.
Measure … using a … (precision …). Repeat each reading three times and calculate a mean.
Plot a graph of … against … and draw a line of best fit.
Safety: wear goggles / add a counterweight / clamp the stand firmly.

例题Example

设计实验验证 F = kx。要求写出变量、仪器、步骤与一处改进。Design an experiment to verify F = kx, stating the variables, instruments, steps and one improvement.
自变量：所挂质量 m；因变量：伸长量 x；控制：同一根弹簧、同一温度。Independent: the load m; dependent: the extension x; controlled: the same spring, same temperature. 仪器：米尺（±1 mm）测长度、天平测质量；挂 6 组不同质量，每次读数前等它静止。Instruments: a metre rule (±1 mm) for length, a balance for mass; hang six different loads, waiting for rest each time. 画 F = mg 对 x 的图，若为过原点直线则 F ∝ x，梯度就是 k。Plot F = mg against x; a straight line through the origin confirms F ∝ x, and its gradient is k. 改进：读数时视线与刻度齐平避免视差；或用游标卡尺/位移传感器测伸长量。Improvement: keep the eye level with the scale to avoid parallax, or measure the extension with a vernier scale or position sensor.

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：想想这个实验里最大的误差来自哪里——人的反应时间，还是摆长测量。Hint: ask where the biggest error lies — human reaction time, or the measurement of the length.

用单摆测 g 的实验中，列出三条具体改进建议，并各说明它针对的是随机误差还是系统误差。In a pendulum experiment to find g, suggest three specific improvements and say which type of error each addresses.
点开看答案Show answer
① 数 20 个周期再除 → 针对随机误差（把反应时间摊薄到 1/20）。1. time 20 oscillations and divide → tackles random error (spreads the reaction time over 20 swings). ② 量到球心（悬点到球顶 + 半径）→ 针对系统误差。2. measure to the centre of the bob → tackles systematic error. ③ 小角度摆动（< 10°）且用长摆 → 针对系统误差（公式 T = 2π√(L/g) 只在小角度成立）。3. keep the swing below about 10° and use a long string → tackles systematic error, since T = 2π√(L/g) holds only for small angles. （其他可接受：用光电门计时 → 随机 + 系统；把支架夹稳 → 随机。）(Also acceptable: a light gate to time the swings; clamping the stand firmly to stop it swaying.)

单元 6 收尾 · 三句话带走Unit 6 wrap-up · three sentences

① 答案跟着数据的最少位数走，单位一个都不能少，中间过程多留一位。1. Match the fewest significant figures in the data, never drop a unit, and keep an extra figure part-way through.

② 随机误差取平均能治，系统误差得改方法；加减加绝对值、乘除加百分比。2. Averaging cures random error; only a redesign cures systematic error. Add absolute uncertainties for + and −, percentages for × and ÷.

③ 把公式掰成 y = mx + c，梯度与截距会自己说出答案；6 分题按方法 / 测量 / 控制 / 重复 / 处理 / 安全六块写全。3. Rearrange into y = mx + c and let the gradient and intercept give you the answer; cover all six blocks of the planning question.

这一单元不单独考，但每一道实验题、每一份卷子的计算部分都在用。把它当作工具箱，别的单元随时回来取。This unit is never examined on its own, yet every practical question and every calculation depends on it. Treat it as a toolbox you come back to throughout.

— 第 7 站完成 —— Stop 7 done —

点标题可以直接跳到那一站。每站 = 一张图 + 一道例题 + 一道练习题（答案点开才看）。Click a title to jump there. Each stop = one diagram + one worked example + one practice question.

1 弧度与角速度 ωRadians and angular speed ω Angular speed角速度
学完你会：把"每秒转多少圈""每秒转多少角度""每秒走多远"三个说法互相换算。By the end: you can convert freely between turns per second, radians per second, and metres per second.
说人话：描述"转得快不快"有两种语言。一种是老办法：每秒转几圈（f 或 n）；另一种是每秒转过多少弧度，叫角速度 ω。一圈是 2π 弧度，所以 ω = 2πf。而线上那个点的实际速度（米每秒）要看它离圆心多远：v = rω。同一个转盘上，外圈走得快、内圈走得慢，但角速度一样——都在同一个时间转过同样的角度。 In plain words: there are two ways to describe "how fast it spins". The everyday one: turns per second (f). The physics one: radians per second, called angular speed ω. One turn is 2π radians, so ω = 2πf. The actual speed of a point on the disc depends on how far out it sits: v = rω. On the same turntable the outer edge moves faster than the inner part, but everything has the same angular speed — it all sweeps the same angle in the same time.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • 弧度 θ = s / r；一整圈 = 2π rad = 360°。Radian θ = s / r; a full turn = 2π rad = 360°.
  • 角速度 ω = θ / t，单位 rad/s。Angular speed ω = θ / t, in rad/s.
  • 连接两种语言：ω = 2πf = 2π / T。Link them: ω = 2πf = 2π / T.
  • 沿线速度 v = rω（必须把 ω 换成 rad/s，不能用"转每秒"直接乘）。Linear speed v = rω (ω must be in rad/s — never multiply by turns per second).
  • 弧度是一个没单位的量（本质是两个长度相除），所以公式里它经常"凭空消失"。A radian is dimensionless (one length divided by another), which is why it seems to vanish inside formulas.

例题Example

一张老唱片以 33⅓ 转/分 转动，唱针放在离中心 12 cm 处。求：(a) 角速度 ω；(b) 该点的线速度 v。A vinyl record spins at 33⅓ revolutions per minute. A stylus sits 12 cm from the centre. Find (a) the angular speed ω and (b) its linear speed v.
(a) 先换成"每秒几圈"：f = 33.3 / 60 ≈ 0.556 Hz。(a) First convert to turns per second: f = 33.3 / 60 ≈ 0.556 Hz. ω = 2πf = 2π × 0.556 = 3.49 rad/s (b) r = 12 cm = 0.12 m：(b) r = 12 cm = 0.12 m: v = rω = 0.12 × 3.49 = 0.42 m/s

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：先把"分钟"换成"秒"，再套 ω = 2πf。Hint: convert minutes to seconds first, then use ω = 2πf.

一台离心机以 3000 转/分 转动，样品放在距轴 8.0 cm 处。求角速度 ω 与该点线速度 v。A centrifuge spins at 3000 rpm. A sample sits 8.0 cm from the axis. Find ω and v.
点开看答案Show answer
f = 3000 / 60 = 50 Hz；ω = 2π × 50 = 314 rad/s v = rω = 0.080 × 314 = 25 m/s

2 向心加速度 a = v²/rCentripetal acceleration a = v²/r Centripetal acc.向心加速度
学完你会：解释为什么"匀速"圆周运动还有加速度，并能算它的大小。By the end: you can explain why uniform circular motion still counts as acceleration, and work out its size.
说人话：加速度的定义是"速度的变化率"，而速度是带方向的。绕圈的时候哪怕快慢一点没变，方向每时每刻都在转，所以一定有加速度。这个加速度不大不小，正好指向圆心，叫向心加速度，大小是 a = v²/r。注意一个反直觉的地方：半径越小，同一个速度需要越大的加速度——这就是为什么高速公路上的小转角要限速。 In plain words: acceleration means rate of change of velocity, and velocity has a direction. Going round a circle, even at constant speed the direction changes every instant, so there must be an acceleration. It points exactly towards the centre — the centripetal acceleration, a = v²/r. Note the counter-intuitive bit: for the same speed, a smaller radius needs a bigger acceleration — which is why tight motorway curves carry lower speed limits.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • a = v²/r = rω²，方向始终指向圆心（方向一直在变，别写"向心加速度方向恒定"）。a = v²/r = rω², always towards the centre (the direction keeps turning — never call it constant).
  • 速度大小不变、方向变 → 仍然加速（这是最常考的定义题）。Constant speed with changing direction is still acceleration (a favourite exam definition).
  • 向心加速度不做功：它和速度垂直，所以不改变动能。Centripetal acceleration does no work: it is perpendicular to the velocity, so kinetic energy is unchanged.
  • 半径小一半，所需加速度翻倍；速度翻倍，加速度翻四倍。Halve the radius and a doubles; double the speed and a quadruples.

例题Example

一辆车以 20 m/s 通过半径 50 m 的弯道。求它的向心加速度；如果速度提到 40 m/s 呢？A car takes a bend of radius 50 m at 20 m/s. Find its centripetal acceleration, and then at 40 m/s.
a = v²/r = 20² / 50 = 400 / 50 = 8.0 m/s² 速度翻倍：a = 40² / 50 = 1600 / 50 = 32 m/s²（约 3g，轮胎抓地力根本撑不住）Double the speed: a = 40² / 50 = 1600 / 50 = 32 m/s² (about 3g — tyre grip cannot deliver that)

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：这条里用 rω² 更方便，ω 已经是 rad/s。Hint: rω² is the easier route here since ω is already in rad/s.

<｜hy_place▁holder▁no▁813｜>上一物体距转轴 0.25 m，转盘以 6.0 rad/s 匀速转动。求该点的向心加速度。An object on a turntable sits 0.25 m from the axis, spinning at 6.0 rad/s. Find its centripetal acceleration.
点开看答案Show answer
a = rω² = 0.25 × 6.0² = 0.25 × 36 = 9.0 m/s² 方向：始终指向转轴。Direction: always towards the axis.
— 第 2 站完成 —— Stop 2 done —
3 向心力 F = mv²/rCentripetal force F = mv²/r Centripetal force向心力
学完你会：在受力图里指出"谁"在充当向心力，并算出需要的大小。By the end: you can point at the free-body diagram and say which force is doing the centripetal job, and how big it must be.
说人话：最关键的一句话：向心力不是多出来的一种力，它是某个真实存在的力被"安排"去做的任务。绳子拴着小球转圈时，是张力在当向心力；月球绕地球时，是引力在当；汽车拐弯时，是轮胎的摩擦力在当。画受力图时千万不要在拉力之外再画一个"离心力"——那不是真实受力。 In plain words: the single most important sentence: centripetal force is not an extra force. It is the job that some real force has been given. A conker on a string? The tension does it. The Moon? Gravity does it. A car cornering? Friction from the tyres does it. When you draw the free-body diagram, never add an outward "centrifugal force" alongside the real forces — it is not a real force.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • F = mv²/r = mrω²，方向指向圆心。F = mv²/r = mrω², directed towards the centre.
  • 写方程的套路：指向圆心的合力 = mv²/r（先算合力，再等于它）。The standard move: resultant force towards the centre = mv²/r (work out the resultant first, then equate).
  • 绳子/摩擦力不够大就维持不了这个圆运动会甩出去（切向飞出，不是径向外冲）。If the string or friction cannot supply enough, the object leaves at a tangent — not radially outwards.
  • 因为垂直于速度，向心力不做功，动能和速率不变。Being perpendicular to the velocity, centripetal force does no work; kinetic energy and speed stay put.

例题Example

质量 0.40 kg 的小球系在 0.80 m 长的绳上，在水平面内以 4.0 m/s 匀速转圈。求绳子张力。A 0.40 kg ball on a 0.80 m string moves in a horizontal circle at 4.0 m/s. Find the tension.
绳子张力单独<｜hy_place▁holder▁no▁813｜>当向心力：The tension alone provides the centripetal force: T = mv²/r = 0.40 × 4.0² / 0.80 = 0.40 × 16 / 0.80 = 8.0 N （对比：静止吊着时只有 mg ≈ 3.9 N，转起来绳子紧得多。）(For comparison, hanging still it would only be mg ≈ 3.9 N — spinning makes the string much tighter.)

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：质量单位先换成 kg。Hint: convert the mass into kg first.

质量 1200 kg 的汽车以 15 m/s 通过半径 30 m 的弯道。求轮胎需要提供多大的横向摩擦力。A 1200 kg car takes a bend of radius 30 m at 15 m/s. How large a sideways frictional force must the tyres supply?
点开看答案Show answer
F = mv²/r = 1200 × 15² / 30 = 1200 × 225 / 30 = 9000 N
— 第 3 站完成 —— Stop 3 done —
4 圆锥摆与倾斜路面Conical pendulum and banked track Conical pendulum圆锥摆
学完你会：把一个斜着的力拆成竖直和水平两份，分别配 mg 和 mv²/r。By the end: you can resolve a slanted force into vertical and horizontal parts and match them to mg and mv²/r.
说人话：这两道题是同一道题。绳子或支持力都是斜着的：它的竖直分量负责托住重量（竖直方向原地不动），水平分量负责把物体往圆心拽。于是永远两行方程：竖直方向平衡 T cosθ = mg，水平方向就是向心力 T sinθ = mv²/r。两式相除还能消掉 T：tanθ = v²/(rg)。这条正是赛道倾角公式。 In plain words: these two situations are the same question. The string or the normal reaction is slanted: its vertical component holds up the weight (nothing moves vertically), and its horizontal component drags the object towards the centre. So it is always two equations: vertically T cosθ = mg, horizontally T sinθ = mv²/r. Divide one by the other and T disappears: tanθ = v²/(rg) — which is exactly the banking-angle formula.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • 竖直方向不算向心力：物体高度不变，竖直合力为零。Nothing vertical goes into the centripetal force: the height is constant, so vertical forces balance.
  • 两式相除得到 tanθ = v²/(rg)，θ 与质量无关。Dividing gives tanθ = v²/(rg), independent of mass.
  • 圆锥摆里的 r 是圆轨道半径，不是绳长；r = L sinθ。In a conical pendulum, r is the radius of the circle, not the string length: r = L sinθ.
  • 倾斜路面按设计速度行驶时不需要摩擦力（这是考点常见问法）。A banked track at its design speed needs no friction at all (a common exam twist).

例题Example

长 1.5 m 的绳做成圆锥摆，绳与竖直方向成 30°。求小球转动的线速度 v。A conical pendulum has a 1.5 m string at 30° to the vertical. Find the speed v of the bob.
r = L sinθ = 1.5 × sin30° = 0.75 m 用 tanθ = v²/(rg)：Using tanθ = v²/(rg): v² = rg tanθ = 0.75 × 9.8 × tan30° = 7.35 × 0.577 = 4.24 v = 2.06 m/s

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：tanθ = 竖直/水平 → θ = tan⁻¹(v²/(rg))。Hint: tanθ = v²/(rg), so θ = tan⁻¹(v²/(rg)).

一段赛道半径 80 m，希望车辆以 25 m/s 通过时不需要摩擦力。求路面倾角 θ。A track of radius 80 m should let cars take it at 25 m/s with no friction needed. Find the banking angle θ.
点开看答案Show answer
tanθ = v²/(rg) = 25² / (80 × 9.8) = 625 / 784 = 0.797 θ = tan⁻¹(0.797) = 38.6°
— 第 4 站完成 —— Stop 4 done —
5 万有引力定律 F = GMm/r²Newton's law of gravitation Gravity万有引力
学完你会：用 F = GMm/r² 算任意两天体之间的引力，并知道 r 到底从哪儿量起。By the end: you can use F = GMm/r² between any two bodies, and know exactly where r is measured from.
说人话：牛顿发现了一件惊人统一的事：让苹果落地的力，和让月亮绕着地球转的力，是同一个力。公式里的 G 是个极小的小数（6.67×10⁻¹¹），所以日常东西之间的引力小到测不出来；只有其中一方是星球时，这个力才明显。注意 r 是从球心到球心量，不是从地表量——站在地面上，r 就是地球半径。 In plain words: Newton's startling unification: the force that drops an apple is exactly the force that swings the Moon around the Earth. The G in the formula is a tiny number (6.67×10⁻¹¹), which is why gravity between everyday objects is unmeasurably small; it only shows up when one of the pair is a planet. Note that r is measured centre to centre, not surface to surface — standing on the ground, r is the radius of the Earth.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • F = GMm/r²，G = 6.67 × 10⁻¹¹ N m² kg⁻²。F = GMm/r², with G = 6.67 × 10⁻¹¹ N m² kg⁻².
  • 距离翻倍 → 引力变为 1/4；距离变 3 倍 → 1/9（平方反比）。Double the distance → quarter the force; triple → one ninth (inverse square).
  • 把地球当一个质量集中在球心的质点来处理，这是"球对称"才成立的性质。Treating the Earth as a point mass at its centre works only because it is spherically symmetric.
  • 这是一对作用力与反作用力，两边大小永远相等。Always an action–reaction pair: equal in size on both bodies.

例题Example

求地球对一名 60 kg 学生的引力。地球质量 6.0×10²⁴ kg，半径 6.4×10⁶ m。Find the Earth's gravitational pull on a 60 kg student. Earth mass 6.0×10²⁴ kg, radius 6.4×10⁶ m.
F = GMm/r² = (6.67×10⁻¹¹ × 6.0×10²⁴ × 60) / (6.4×10⁶)² 分子：6.67×10⁻¹¹ × 3.6×10²⁶ = 2.40×10¹⁶Numerator: 6.67×10⁻¹¹ × 3.6×10²⁶ = 2.40×10¹⁶ 分母：4.10×10¹³Denominator: 4.10×10¹³ F = 2.40×10¹⁶ / 4.10×10¹³ = 586 N （和 mg = 60×9.8 = 588 N 基本一致——两条路同一回事）(matches mg = 60 × 9.8 = 588 N — the two routes agree)

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：距离要变成原来的 3 倍，引力变成多少？用平方反比判断即可。Hint: the distance triples — use the inverse-square law rather than starting from scratch.

一颗卫星在地表处受到的引力是 9000 N。若把它移到距地心 3 倍地球半径处，引力变成多大？A satellite experiences 9000 N of gravitational pull at the surface. What is the pull at three Earth radii from the centre?
点开看答案Show answer
距离变成 3 倍 → 引力变成 1/3² = 1/9Distance triples → force falls to 1/3² = 1/9 F = 9000 / 9 = 1000 N
— 第 5 站完成 —— Stop 5 done —
6 引力场强度 g = GM/r²Gravitational field strength g = GM/r² Field strength场强
学完你会：说出 g 的两种身份（重力加速度、引力场强度），并会比较不同高度的 g。By the end: you can state the two identities of g (gravitational field strength and acceleration of free fall) and compare g at different heights.
说人话：g 有两种叫法，本质是同一个东西。当作引力场强度时，它是单位质量受的力（N/kg）；当作重力加速度时，它是自由下落的加速度（m/s²）。数值完全一样。把 F = GMm/r² 除以 m，就得到 g = GM/r²——注意与被测物体的质量无关，这正是"铁球和羽毛同时落地"的道理。 In plain words: g has two names for one idea. As gravitational field strength it is the force per unit mass (N/kg); as the acceleration of free fall it is how fast things speed up downwards (m/s²). Same number. Divide F = GMm/r² by m and you get g = GM/r² — note it does not depend on the mass of the object being pulled, which is precisely why a hammer and a feather land together.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • g = GM/r²，单位可写 N/kg 或 m/s²，两者等价。g = GM/r², quoted either in N/kg or m/s² — they are equivalent.
  • g 与物体自身质量无关；所有物体在同一点下落得一样快。g is independent of the object's own mass; everything falls alike at the same point.
  • 质量 m 的物体受重力 W = mg，它其实就是 GMm/r²。The weight of a mass m is W = mg, which is just GMm/r² rewritten.
  • 高度 h 处的 g：g' = g R²/(R+h)²（R 为星球半径）。At height h: g' = g R²/(R + h)², where R is the planet's radius.
  • 题目说"质量 60 kg"，在月球上质量仍是 60 kg，只是重量变小。If someone "has mass 60 kg", on the Moon their mass is still 60 kg — only the weight changes.

例题Example

月球质量约是地球的 1/81，半径约是地球的 1/3.7。估算月球表面的 g。The Moon's mass is about 1/81 of the Earth's and its radius about 1/3.7. Estimate g on the Moon's surface.
用比例：g ∝ M / R²Work by ratio: g ∝ M / R² g月 / g地 = (1/81) / (1/3.7)² = (1/81) × 13.7 = 0.169 g月 = 9.8 × 0.169 = 1.66 m/s² （约为地球的 1/6）(about one sixth of Earth's)

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：先把"离地高度"换成"离地心距离"，再用比例。Hint: convert height above the surface into distance from the centre first, then use ratios.

地球半径 6400 km。求在地表上方 6400 km 处（即离地心 2 倍地球半径）的 g。Earth radius is 6400 km. Find g at 6400 km above the surface, i.e. at twice the Earth radius from the centre.
点开看答案Show answer
r 变 2 倍 → g 变 1/4r doubles → g falls to a quarter g' = 9.8 / 4 = 2.45 m/s²
— 第 6 站完成 —— Stop 6 done —
7 卫星轨道与同步卫星Satellite orbits and geostationary Orbits轨道
学完你会：从"引力充当向心力"推出轨道速度公式，并解释同步卫星为什么必须停在特定高度。By the end: you can derive orbital speed from "gravity does the centripetal job" and explain why geostationary satellites must sit at one particular height.
说人话：把上一站的引力和第 3 站的向心力画上等号，就得到轨道的全部公式：GMm/r² = mv²/r。质量 m 两边约掉了（所以重的轻的卫星轨道一样），得到 v = √(GM/r)。再把 v = 2πr/T 代进去，还能得到 T² ∝ r³——这就是开普勒第三定律。同步卫星要求周期正好 24 小时，于是它只有一个可能高度（约 36000 km），还必须停在赤道正上方，否则它会一天到晚在天上画 8 字。 In plain words: put last stop's gravity equal to stop 3's centripetal force and the whole of orbital mechanics falls out: GMm/r² = mv²/r. The satellite's own mass m cancels (so heavy and light satellites ride the same orbit), giving v = √(GM/r). Slip in v = 2πr/T and you also get T² ∝ r³ — Kepler's third law. A geostationary satellite needs a period of exactly 24 hours, so there is only one height it can occupy (about 36 000 km), and it must sit directly over the equator, otherwise it would trace a figure-of-eight across the sky.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • 起点永远是 GMm/r² = mv²/r；由此 v = √(GM/r)。Always start from GMm/r² = mv²/r; hence v = √(GM/r).
  • 轨道越高 → v 越小、周期 T 越长。Higher orbit → slower speed, longer period.
  • T² ∝ r³（开普勒第三定律），比较两个轨道最省事。T² ∝ r³ (Kepler's third law) — the quickest way to compare two orbits.
  • 同步卫星：周期 24 h、轨道在赤道平面、高度约 36000 km，地面上看着不动。Geostationary: period 24 h, over the equator, at about 36 000 km — it appears fixed in the sky.
  • 轨道上的卫星仍是"失重"而不是"没引力"——引力正忙着当向心力。Orbiting astronauts are weightless but not gravity-free — gravity is busy being the centripetal force.

例题Example

求贴近地表飞行的卫星（近地轨道）的速度。地球 GM = 4.0×10¹⁴ SI，半径 R = 6.4×10⁶ m。Find the speed of a satellite skimming just above the Earth's surface. Take GM = 4.0×10¹⁴ (SI) and R = 6.4×10⁶ m.
v = √(GM/r) = √(4.0×10¹⁴ / 6.4×10⁶) = √(6.25×10⁷) v = 7.9 km/s （这就是第一宇宙速度，约 28000 km/h）(the first cosmic velocity, roughly 28 000 km/h) 周期 T = 2πr/v = 2π × 6.4×10⁶ / 7900 ≈ 5090 s ≈ 85 分钟Period T = 2πr/v = 2π × 6.4×10⁶ / 7900 ≈ 5090 s ≈ 85 min

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：用 T² ∝ r³ 做比例，别从头硬算。Hint: use T² ∝ r³ in ratio form — don't start from scratch.

近地轨道周期约 85 分钟。若某卫星轨道半径是近地轨道半径的 4 倍，它的周期是多少？A low orbit has a period of about 85 min. What is the period of a satellite whose orbital radius is 4 times as large?
点开看答案Show answer
T₂ / T₁ = (r₂/r₁)^(3/2) = 4^(3/2) = 8 T₂ = 85 × 8 = 680 分钟 ≈ 11.3 小时min ≈ 11.3 h

单元 8 · 简谐振动Unit 8 · Simple Harmonic MotionA2

A2 · 整个 A-Level 物理里最"一份公式走天下"的一块：弹簧、钟摆、秋千，全是同一个 a = −ω²xA2 · the most "one formula fits all" topic in the syllabus: springs, pendulums and swings are all a = −ω²x

点标题可以直接跳到那一站。每站 = 一张图 + 一道例题 + 一道练习题（答案点开才看）。Click a title to jump there. Each stop = one diagram + one worked example + one practice question.

1 SHM 的定义：a = −ω²xThe definition: a = −ω²x Definition定义
学完你会：用一句话判断一个运动是不是 SHM，并把 ω 找出来。By the end: you can decide in one sentence whether a motion is SHM, and pull ω out of the equation.
说人话：简谐振动的定义只有一个要求：加速度与位移成正比，方向永远朝着中间，写成 a = −ω²x。那个负号是灵魂——它表示"你在右边就往左拽，你在左边就往右拽"。比例系数写成 ω² 是为了好看，因为这样 ω 就正好是角频率（和第 7 单元的 ω 是同一个东西）。要判断一个运动是不是 SHM，只需要看能不能凑出这个形式：凑得出来，ω² 就是 x 前面的系数。 In plain words: SHM asks exactly one thing: acceleration proportional to displacement and always pointing back to the middle, written a = −ω²x. The minus sign is the soul of it — right of centre you get pulled left, left of centre you get pulled right. Writing the constant as ω² is cosmetic but convenient, because then ω is exactly the angular frequency (the same ω as Unit 7). To test whether a motion is SHM, just try to force it into that shape: if you can, ω² is whatever sits in front of x.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • 定义：a = −ω²x。看到这个式子就是 SHM。Definition: a = −ω²x. Spot this shape and it is SHM.
  • 判断套路：写出合力 → 用 F = ma → 凑成 a = (常数) × (−x)，那个常数就是 ω²。The routine: write the resultant force, use F = ma, and shape it into a = (constant) × (−x); that constant is ω².
  • 弹簧例子：F = −kx → ma = −kx → ω² = k/m。Spring example: F = −kx → ma = −kx → ω² = k/m.
  • 在平衡位置（中间）：x = 0，a = 0，但速度最大。At the equilibrium position: x = 0, a = 0, but speed is maximum.
  • 在两端：速度为零，但加速度最大。At the extremes: speed is zero, but acceleration is maximum.

例题Example

劲度系数 k = 80 N/m 的弹簧上挂质量 0.20 kg 的物体，写出它的 ω² 与 ω。A 0.20 kg mass hangs on a spring of stiffness k = 80 N/m. Find ω² and ω.
弹簧力 F = −kx，牛二定律 ma = −kx：Spring force F = −kx and Newton's second law ma = −kx: a = −(k/m) x → ω² = k/m = 80 / 0.20 = 400 s⁻² ω = √400 = 20 rad/s

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：先判断这是不是 SHM（能否写成 −常数×x），再读 ω²。Hint: check it can be written as −(constant)×x, then read off ω².

某物体的运动满足 a = −25 x（SI 单位）。它是不是 SHM？若是，求 ω 和周期 T。A body moves so that a = −25 x (SI units). Is this SHM? If so, find ω and the period T.
点开看答案Show answer
是 SHM：a ∝ −x。ω² = 25 → ω = 5.0 rad/sYes — a ∝ −x. ω² = 25 → ω = 5.0 rad/s T = 2π/ω = 2π / 5.0 = 1.26 s
— 第 1 站完成 —— Stop 1 done —
2 x-t、v-t、a-t 三条曲线The x-t, v-t and a-t curves Graphs图像
学完你会：看一眼图形说出物体此刻在往哪走、是加速还是减速。By the end: you can look at any point on the graph and say which way it is moving and whether it is speeding up.
说人话：把 x-t 图想成一个影子：一个点绕着圆匀速转，它的影子在墙上上下动，出来的就是正弦曲线。由此得到三条曲线的错位关系：速度比位移超前四分之一周期（位移最大的时候刚要回头，速度为零；跑到中间的时候冲得最快），加速度与位移完全反相（你往右，它就往左拖）。考试最喜欢问"此刻速度方向""此刻加速度方向"，就靠这两句。 In plain words: picture the x-t graph as a shadow: a point goes round a circle steadily and its shadow slides up and down a wall — out comes the sine curve. That gives the offsets: velocity leads displacement by a quarter period (at maximum displacement you are just about to turn round, so v = 0; passing the middle you are going fastest), and acceleration is exactly anti-phase with displacement (you go right, it drags left). Exam questions asking "which way is it moving now?" or "which way is it accelerating?" hinge entirely on these two sentences.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • 标准写法 x = x₀ sin(ωt)（从中间起步）或 x = x₀ cos(ωt)（从端点松手）。Standard forms: x = x₀ sin(ωt) (starting from the middle) or x = x₀ cos(ωt) (released from an end).
  • v-t 图是 x-t 图的斜率；a-t 图永远与 x-t 图上下翻转。The v-t graph is the gradient of x-t; a-t is always x-t flipped upside down.
  • 相位说法：v 超前 x 90°，a 与 x 差 180°。In phase language: v leads x by 90°, a is 180° out of phase with x.
  • 在同一个位置上，速度有两个可能方向（去程和回程），题目若问矢量要写 ±。At any given displacement there are two possible directions of travel — quote ± if the question asks for a vector.

例题Example

某物体 x = 0.050 sin(10 t)（SI）。求振幅、角频率、周期，以及 t = 0 时的速度与加速度。A body has x = 0.050 sin(10 t) (SI). Find the amplitude, angular frequency and period, plus its velocity and acceleration at t = 0.
对照标准式：Compare with the standard form: x₀ = 0.050 m，ω = 10 rad/s，T = 2π/10 = 0.628 s t = 0：sin0 = 0 → x = 0（在中心），所以 v = ωx₀ = 10 × 0.050 = 0.50 m/s（正向），a = 0At t = 0: sin 0 = 0 → x = 0, so v = ωx₀ = 10 × 0.050 = 0.50 m/s and a = 0

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：先读振幅和 ω；t = 0 时在端点还是中心？Hint: read off the amplitude and ω; at t = 0, is it at an end or at the centre?

某物体 x = 0.080 cos(5.0 t)（SI）。求振幅、周期、最大速度和最大加速度的大小。A body has x = 0.080 cos(5.0 t) (SI). Find the amplitude, period, maximum speed and maximum acceleration.
点开看答案Show answer
x₀ = 0.080 m，ω = 5.0 rad/s，T = 2π/5.0 = 1.26 s vmax = ωx₀ = 5.0 × 0.080 = 0.40 m/s amax = ω²x₀ = 25 × 0.080 = 2.0 m/s²
— 第 2 站完成 —— Stop 2 done —
3 速度与位移公式 v = ω√(x₀² − x²)Speed and displacement: v = ω√(x₀² − x²) v-x formula速度位移式
学完你会：知道物体在任意位置有多快，不用先算时间。By the end: you can find the speed at any position without working out the time first.
说人话：这条公式其实是能量守恒换了个皮：总能量固定（正比于 x₀²），摆在你所在位置的"势能"和正在走的"动能"此消彼长，于是 v = ω√(x₀² − x²)。三个特例记住就够用：走到端点 v = 0，经过中心 v = ωx₀（最大），最大加速度 = ω²x₀。凡是题目问"在某处的速度"，直接用它，比算相位快得多。 In plain words: this formula is really conservation of energy in disguise: the total energy is fixed (set by x₀²) and it swaps back and forth between stored energy at your displacement and kinetic energy in your motion, giving v = ω√(x₀² − x²). Three special cases are all you need: at an end v = 0, through the centre v = ωx₀ (the maximum), and maximum acceleration = ω²x₀. Any question asking "how fast at this position" — use it, it beats working out the phase.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • v = ±ω√(x₀² − x²)；只看快慢就写正值。v = ±ω√(x₀² − x²); drop the ± if only the speed is asked for.
  • v最大 = ωx₀（在平衡位置）；a最大 = ω²x₀（在端点）。v max = ωx₀ (at equilibrium); a max = ω²x₀ (at the ends).
  • 求平均速度别用它——只有瞬时速度。Don't use it for average speed — it gives the instantaneous speed only.
  • 单位检查：ω 用 rad/s，x 用 m，出来的 v 直接是 m/s。Sanity check units: ω in rad/s and x in m gives v directly in m/s.

例题Example

振幅 0.10 m、ω = 8.0 rad/s 的振子，求它在位移 x = 0.060 m 处的速度。An oscillator has amplitude 0.10 m and ω = 8.0 rad/s. Find its speed at displacement x = 0.060 m.
v = ω√(x₀² − x²) = 8.0 × √(0.10² − 0.060²) = 8.0 × √(0.010 − 0.0036) = 8.0 × √0.0064 = 8.0 × 0.080 = 0.64 m/s 对照：过中心时最快 = ωx₀ = 8.0 × 0.10 = 0.80 m/s，比刚才确实更快。Check: through the centre v max = ωx₀ = 8.0 × 0.10 = 0.80 m/s, which is indeed faster.

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：反过来求 x₀ 时，先把两边除以 ω 再平方。Hint: to find x₀ in reverse, divide by ω first, then square.

某振子 ω = 4.0 rad/s，经过中心时速度是 1.2 m/s；当位移为振幅的一半时，它的速度是多少？An oscillator with ω = 4.0 rad/s passes the centre at 1.2 m/s. What is its speed when the displacement is half the amplitude?
点开看答案Show answer
vmax = ωx₀ = 1.2 → x₀ = 1.2 / 4.0 = 0.30 m v = ω√(x₀² − (x₀/2)²) = 4.0 × √(0.09 − 0.0225) = 4.0 × 0.260 = 1.04 m/s
— 第 3 站完成 —— Stop 3 done —
4 周期：弹簧振子与单摆Period: mass-spring and pendulum Period周期
学完你会：算出弹簧和单摆的周期，并判断改变质量、长度、振幅分别会怎样。By the end: you can find the period of a spring or a pendulum, and predict what changes it.
说人话：周期公式不用死记——从第 1 站的 ω² 直接推过来就行：T = 2π/ω。弹簧的 ω² = k/m，所以 T = 2π√(m/k)：挂得越重摆动越慢，弹簧越硬摆得越快。单摆的 ω² = g/L（小角度时才成立），所以 T = 2π√(L/g)：摆长四倍，周期只翻倍（根号关系）。两者都不受振幅影响——甩得大一点跟小一点，一趟来回时间一样，这叫等时性，是摆钟能做计时的原因。 In plain words: don't memorise the period formulas — derive them from stop 1's ω² with T = 2π/ω. For a spring, ω² = k/m so T = 2π√(m/k): a heavier load swings slower, a stiffer spring swings faster. For a pendulum, ω² = g/L (only for small angles) so T = 2π√(L/g): quadruple the length and the period merely doubles (square-root relationship). Neither depends on amplitude — a big swing and a small swing take the same time, the property called isochronism, which is why pendulum clocks can keep time at all.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • ω = 2π/T，f = 1/T；所以 T = 2π/ω。ω = 2π/T and f = 1/T; hence T = 2π/ω.
  • 弹簧：T = 2π√(m/k)；单摆：T = 2π√(L/g)（小角度）。Spring: T = 2π√(m/k); pendulum: T = 2π√(L/g) (small angles).
  • 单摆的 ω² = g/L 只在角度很小时成立，大角度会偏离。For a pendulum, ω² = g/L holds only at small angles; large swings drift away from it.
  • 周期与振幅无关；改质量影响弹簧、不影响单摆。Period is independent of amplitude; mass matters for the spring but not for the pendulum.
  • l 变成 4 倍，T 只有 2 倍——别被线性直觉骗了。Four times the length gives only twice the period — don't trust your linear instincts.

例题Example

一根长 1.0 m 的单摆，在 g = 9.8 m/s² 处的周期是多少？若把它拿到月球（g = 1.6 m/s²）呢？A 1.0 m pendulum at g = 9.8 m/s² has what period? What about on the Moon (g = 1.6 m/s²)?
T = 2π√(L/g) = 2π√(1.0/9.8) = 2π × 0.319 = 2.01 s 月球上：T = 2π√(1.0/1.6) = 2π × 0.791 = 4.97 s（摆钟在月球上走得慢多了）On the Moon: T = 2π√(1.0/1.6) = 2π × 0.791 = 4.97 s (a pendulum clock would run very slow)

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：T ∝ √m，质量变成 4 倍时 T 变几倍？Hint: T ∝ √m — four times the mass makes T what?

弹簧下端挂 0.10 kg 时周期为 0.80 s。若换成 0.40 kg，周期变为多少？A spring with 0.10 kg hanging has a period of 0.80 s. What period with 0.40 kg?
点开看答案Show answer
质量 ×4 → T ×√4 = ×2Mass ×4 → T ×√4 = ×2 T = 0.80 × 2 = 1.60 s
— 第 4 站完成 —— Stop 4 done —
5 简谐振动里的能量交换Energy exchange in SHM Energy能量
学完你会：把第 3 站的速度公式和能量守恒连起来，明白为什么总能量 ∝ 振幅²。By the end: you can connect stop 3's speed formula to conservation of energy, and see why the total goes as amplitude².
说人话：SHM 的全过程就是能量在两个口袋之间倒来倒去：在端点，全是势能（弹簧拉满或摆到最高）；跑到中间，势能全变成了动能（冲得飞快）。因为没有摩擦，两个口袋加起来始终一样多。既然势能与位移平方成正比，就得到一条很有用的结论：总能量正比于振幅的平方——振幅加倍，能量变四倍。 In plain words: SHM is a story of energy sloshing between two pockets. At the ends it is all potential (spring fully stretched, pendulum at its highest). Through the middle it has all become kinetic (rushing past fast). With no friction the sum never changes. Since potential energy goes as displacement squared, a very useful result follows: total energy is proportional to the square of the amplitude — double the amplitude and you store four times the energy.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • 无阻尼时总能量守恒，且 E ∝ x₀²。With no damping the total energy is constant and E ∝ x₀².
  • 弹簧：E = ½kx² + ½mv²，端点处 E = ½kx₀² = ½mv最大²。Spring: E = ½kx² + ½mv²; at the ends E = ½kx₀² = ½m v max².
  • 单摆的势能变化用 mgh 算，h 是相对最低点升高的高度。For a pendulum use mgh, where h is the height gained above the lowest point.
  • 问"经过某点的速度"，用能量守恒往往最省事。Asking "speed at a certain point"? Energy conservation is usually the fastest route.

例题Example

弹簧 k = 200 N/m，振子质量 0.50 kg，振幅 0.040 m。求总能量和经过中心时的最大速度。A spring of stiffness 200 N/m carries a 0.50 kg mass with amplitude 0.040 m. Find the total energy and the maximum speed.
E = ½kx₀² = ½ × 200 × 0.040² = 100 × 0.0016 = 0.16 J 经过中心时全是动能：½mv² = 0.16At the centre it is all kinetic: ½mv² = 0.16 v = √(2 × 0.16 / 0.50) = √0.64 = 0.80 m/s

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：振幅变 2 倍，能量变几倍？最大速度与振幅成什么比例？Hint: doubling the amplitude multiplies the energy by what? And what happens to the maximum speed?

上题的装置若振幅加大到 0.080 m，总能量和最大速度各是多少？With the same apparatus, if the amplitude is increased to 0.080 m, what are the total energy and the maximum speed?
点开看答案Show answer
E = ½ × 200 × 0.080² = 100 × 0.0064 = 0.64 J （4 倍）(four times) v = √(2 × 0.64 / 0.50) = √2.56 = 1.60 m/s （2 倍）(twice)
— 第 5 站完成 —— Stop 5 done —
6 阻尼振动Damped oscillations Damping阻尼
学完你会：说出轻阻尼 / 临界阻尼 / 过阻尼三种情形的区别与典型用途。By the end: you can state the difference between light, critical and heavy damping, and give a use for each.
说人话：阻尼就是"有东西在偷偷抽走能量"。抽得不多叫轻阻尼：还能来回摆几下，但一次比一次小，而周期几乎不变（这点很反直觉，也很爱考）。抽得恰到好处叫临界阻尼：不来回摆，以最短时间回到中间——车门缓冲器、指针式仪表用的就是这个。抽得太狠叫过阻尼：慢吞吞回中间，连路都走不动。 In plain words: damping just means something is quietly draining the energy. A gentle drain is light damping: you still swing back and forth, each swing smaller than the last, with the period almost unchanged (surprising, and a favourite exam point). Drain it exactly right and you get critical damping: no overshoot, the fastest possible return to the middle — that is what car door closers and analogue meters use. Drain it too hard and you get heavy damping: a sluggish crawl back to the middle.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • 轻阻尼：振幅指数衰减，周期基本不变。Light damping: amplitude decays exponentially; period essentially unchanged.
  • 临界阻尼：最快回到平衡位置且不越过它——车门、仪表。Critical damping: fastest return without overshoot — car doors, meters.
  • 过阻尼：不再摆动，但回到中心很慢。Heavy damping: no oscillation, but a slow return to the middle.
  • 阻尼不改变"固有频率"，只改变振幅（并把能量耗成热）。Damping does not shift the natural frequency; it only attacks the amplitude (turning energy into heat).

例题Example

某阻尼振子第 1 次峰值振幅为 8.0 cm，第 5 次为 2.0 cm。判断阻尼类型，并说明周期是否会明显变化。A damped oscillator peaks at 8.0 cm on its first swing and 2.0 cm on its fifth. Identify the damping type and say whether the period changes noticeably.
能数出多次往复 → 轻阻尼。Several countable swings → light damping. 轻阻尼下周期基本不变，只是每趟损失大致同比例的能量，所以振幅逐次变小。Under light damping the period stays practically constant; a roughly fixed fraction of energy is lost each swing, so the amplitude shrinks steadily.

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：用"要不要来回摆"和"回中快不快"两条标准判断。Hint: judge by two tests — does it oscillate, and how fast does it settle?

汽车悬挂希望颠簸后尽快稳定、又不想上下颠个不停，应选哪种阻尼？为什么不用更重的阻尼？Car suspension should settle quickly without bouncing endlessly. Which damping should be used, and why not heavier?
点开看答案Show answer
接近临界阻尼：能最快稳定且不来回弹。Close to critical damping: settles fastest without bouncing. 若用过阻尼，悬挂变"死板"、回位太慢，下一次颠簸还没复原就来了。With heavy damping the suspension feels dead and returns too slowly to cope with the next bump.
— 第 6 站完成 —— Stop 6 done —
7 受迫振动与共振Forced oscillations and resonance Resonance共振
学完你会：解释共振什么时候发生、为什么会有害、以及怎么避开它。By the end: you can explain when resonance occurs, why it can be destructive, and how to avoid it.
说人话：每个物体都有自己的固有频率（由它本身的 k 和 m，或 L 和 g 决定）。如果你推它的节奏正好等于这个频率，每一次小小的推力都踩在点上，能量越积越多，振幅炸上天——这叫共振。用处很多（乐器共鸣箱、微波炉、MRI），害处也多（塔科马海峡大桥在风里扭断、军队齐步过桥会踏塌桥）。避开的办法只有两个：改固有频率（调质量或刚度），或加阻尼把峰压平。 In plain words: every object has a natural frequency, set by its own k and m (or L and g). If you push it at exactly that frequency, every nudge lands in step and the energy piles up until the amplitude goes wild — resonance. It is wonderfully useful (instrument bodies, microwave ovens, MRI) and wonderfully destructive (Tacoma Narrows Bridge twisting itself apart in the wind, soldiers marching in step bringing a bridge down). There are only two cures: shift the natural frequency (change mass or stiffness) or add damping to flatten the peak.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • 驱动频率 f = f₀（固有频率）时发生共振，振幅最大。Resonance at driving f = f₀ (natural frequency), where amplitude peaks.
  • 此时系统从驱动源吸收能量的效率最高。Energy transfer from the driver is then most efficient.
  • 阻尼越轻，共振峰越高越尖；加阻尼会把峰压低。Lighter damping gives a taller, sharper peak; damping lowers it.
  • 避免共振：改质量 / 刚度，或加阻尼。Avoid it by changing mass/stiffness or adding damping.

例题Example

洗衣机脱水时在某个转速整机震动特别厉害，转速再高反而安静。解释原因。A washing machine shakes violently at one spin speed and becomes quieter at higher speeds. Explain.
机身有自身固有频率 f₀；滚筒转速提供挑动频率。The machine body has its own natural frequency f₀; the drum rotation sets the driving frequency. 当转速使驱动频率 ≈ f₀，发生共振，振幅剧增。At the speed where the driving frequency ≈ f₀, resonance occurs and the amplitude shoots up. 转速继续升高，驱动频率远离 f₀，落到曲线两侧，振幅变小，于是又安静了。Spin faster and the driving frequency moves past resonance onto the flanks, so the amplitude falls and it quiets down.

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：想"能量每次都被及时补上"这个画面。Hint: picture the energy top-up arriving in step every single time.

为什么轻轻地、有节奏地推秋千能荡得很高，而用同样力气随便推却推不高？Why does gentle rhythmic pushing make a swing go high, while pushing just as hard at random times does not?
点开看答案Show answer
有节奏地推时，驱动频率 ≈ 秋千固有频率，每次推力都做正功，能量累加。Rhythmic pushing matches the swing's natural frequency: every push does positive work and the energy accumulates. 乱推的节奏对不上，有时推力与运动方向相反而做负功，能量互相抵消。Random pushes fall out of step; some act against the motion and do negative work, cancelling the gains.
— 第 7 站完成 —— Stop 7 done —
