点标题可以直接跳到那一站。每站 = 一张图 + 一道例题 + 一道练习题（答案点开才看）。Click a title to jump there. Each stop = one diagram + one worked example + one practice question.

1 温度、定标点与开尔文Temperature, fixed points and kelvin Temperature温度
学完你会：说出摄氏温标怎么定出来，并能在摄氏与开尔文之间换算。By the end: you can describe how the Celsius scale is defined, and convert between °C and K.
说人话：温度计量的其实是别的量：水银柱的长度、金属丝的电阻、两种金属接头的电压……这些叫测温属性。要先给这个属性定两条刻度线（下定点冰水混合、上定点沸水），然后假设"属性随温度均匀变化"，两点之间等分 100 格，摄氏温标就造出来了。再往前一步是开尔文：从"最低可能温度"开始算起，0 K 就是那个理论最低点。两者每一格一样大，只是零点不同：T(K) = θ(°C) + 273。 In plain words: a thermometer really measures something else: the length of a mercury column, the resistance of a wire, the voltage across two joined metals. These are thermometric properties. You first anchor two marks (ice-water mixture below, boiling water at 1 atm above), assume the property varies uniformly with temperature, divide the gap into 100 equal steps, and you have built the Celsius scale. One step further is kelvin, counting from the lowest temperature that is theoretically possible: 0 K is that floor. The step size is identical; only the zero differs: T(K) = θ(°C) + 273.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • 测温属性：随温度单调变化的某个可测量。A thermometric property is any measurable quantity that changes steadily with temperature.
  • 两个定点画一条直线：X = aθ + b。Two fixed points define the line X = aθ + b.
  • T/K = θ/°C + 273；温差 1 K = 温差 1 °C。T/K = θ/°C + 273; a change of 1 K equals a change of 1 °C.
  • "0 K"叫绝对零度，实际到不了。0 K is absolute zero and cannot actually be reached.
  • 所有气体在足够低压强下表现一致，可以用气体温度计做基准。At low enough pressure all gases behave alike, which is why a gas thermometer can serve as a standard.

例题Example

某测温元件的电阻在冰水中为 20 Ω，在沸水中为 60 Ω。当它为 44 Ω 时的摄氏温度是多少？A sensor has resistance 20 Ω in ice-water and 60 Ω in boiling water. What temperature is it showing at 44 Ω?
线性插值：θ = (R − R₀) / (R₁₀₀ − R₀) × 100Linear interpolation: θ = (R − R₀) / (R₁₀₀ − R₀) × 100 θ = (44 − 20) / (60 − 20) × 100 = 24/40 × 100 = 60 °C

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：温差不用换算，只有"温度值"才加 273。Hint: differences need no conversion — only absolute values get + 273.

把 25 °C 换成开尔文；并求从 25 °C 升到 100 °C 的温度变化量（用 K 表示）。Convert 25 °C into kelvin, and state the rise from 25 °C to 100 °C in kelvin.
点开看答案Show answer
T = 25 + 273 = 298 K 变化量 ΔT = 100 − 25 = 75 K（与 75 °C 的差值相同）Change ΔT = 100 − 25 = 75 K (same size as 75 °C)
— 第 1 站完成 —— Stop 1 done —
2 内能：热到底是什么Internal energy: what heat really is Internal energy内能
学完你会：区分"加热""温度""内能"三个词，知道热不是一种"东西"。By the end: you can separate "heating", "temperature" and "internal energy" — and say why heat is not a substance.
说人话："热"不是装在物体里的一包东西，它是能量的转移方式。物体真正拥有的是内能：所有分子的动能（跑得多快）加上它们之间的势能（彼此拉开多远）。温度只看前半部分的平均水平；所以冰融化时温度不变但内能还在涨——那些能量全拿去拆分子间的"黏合"了（势能在涨），而不是让分子跑更快。 In plain words: heat is not a parcel of stuff stored inside an object — it is a way of transferring energy. What an object has is internal energy: the kinetic energy of all its molecules plus the potential energy between them. Temperature tracks only the average kinetic part. That is why ice can melt while its temperature stays put: the energy goes into loosening the molecules from each other (potential rising) rather than making them move faster.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • 内能 = 分子动能 + 分子间势能（总和）。Internal energy = total molecular kinetic + intermolecular potential energy.
  • 理想气体没有分子间作用力 → 它的内能只有动能，只跟温度有关。An ideal gas has no intermolecular forces, so its internal energy is all kinetic and depends only on temperature.
  • 加热会改变内能；做功也能改变内能（搓手、打气筒发热）。Heating changes internal energy, and so does doing work (rubbing hands, a warm bicycle pump).
  • 温度不变不等于内能不变：相变时温度不动、内能照涨。Constant temperature does not mean constant internal energy: during a change of state it rises anyway.

例题Example

一块冰在 0 °C 融化成 0 °C 的水。温度和内能分别怎么变？能量去哪了？Ice at 0 °C melts into water at 0 °C. What happens to temperature and internal energy, and where does the energy go?
温度不变——整个过程停在 0 °C。Temperature stays constant — it sits at 0 °C throughout. 内能增加：能量被用来拆开分子间的结合，转成分子势能。Internal energy rises: the energy is spent breaking the bonds between molecules, becoming potential energy.

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：理想气体的内能只看温度。Hint: for an ideal gas, internal energy depends only on temperature.

一定质量理想气体被压缩（外界对它做功），同时向外放热且温度恰好不变。它的内能如何变化？A fixed mass of ideal gas is compressed (work done on it) while losing heat, ending at its original temperature. How has its internal energy changed?
点开看答案Show answer
内能不变：理想气体的内能只由温度决定，温度回到原值，内能就回到原值。Unchanged: the internal energy of an ideal gas depends only on temperature, which returned to its starting value. 这意味着外界做的功，恰好等于向外放出的热量。So the work done on the gas exactly equals the heat lost from it.
— 第 2 站完成 —— Stop 2 done —
3 比热容 c 与比潜热 LSpecific heat capacity and latent heat c and L比热容与潜热
学完你会：算"升温要多少热"和"熔化/汽化要多少热"，并读懂加热曲线。By the end: you can calculate both kinds of energy — raising temperature and changing state — and read a heating curve.
说人话：两种情况用两个公式。只是在升温时：Q = mcΔT，其中 c 是比热容——"每千克每升高一度要吃多少焦"。水的 c = 4200 J/(kg·K)，非常大，所以水是天然的温度缓冲剂（沿海城市冬暖夏凉、暖气用水）。在改变状态（熔化、汽化）时：Q = mL，L 是比潜热，虽然温度一动不动，但能量照样在进。水的汽化潜热 2.26 MJ/kg 极其巨大，这就是被 100 °C 蒸汽烫伤比被 100 °C 开水烫严重得多的原因。 In plain words: two situations, two formulas. Just getting hotter: Q = mcΔT, where c is the specific heat capacity — "how many joules per kilogram per degree". Water's c is 4200 J/(kg·K), remarkably large, which is why water is nature's temperature buffer (coastal climates are mild; central heating runs on water). Changing state (melting, boiling): Q = mL, where L is the specific latent heat — energy still pouring in even though the temperature refuses to budge. Water's latent heat of vaporisation, 2.26 MJ/kg, is enormous, which is why steam at 100 °C burns far worse than boiling water at 100 °C.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • 升温：Q = mcΔT；改状态：Q = mL。Warming: Q = mcΔT; changing state: Q = mL.
  • 水的 c = 4200 J/(kg·K)；冰的比潜热约 334 kJ/kg，水汽化约 2.26 MJ/kg。Water: c = 4200 J/(kg·K); latent heat of fusion ≈ 334 kJ/kg, vaporisation ≈ 2.26 MJ/kg.
  • 有热损失时，实际需要的时间更长——题目里"不计热损失"是好消息。With heat losses it takes longer; "no heat lost to surroundings" in a question is good news.
  • 电加热：Q = Pt（电功转成热），常常和上面两个公式一起用。With an electric heater Q = Pt; expect it alongside both formulas above.

例题Example

用 2000 W 的电水壶把 1.5 kg 水从 20 °C 加热到 100 °C，不计热损失，需要多久？A 2000 W kettle heats 1.5 kg of water from 20 °C to 100 °C with no losses. How long does it take?
Q = mcΔT = 1.5 × 4200 × 80 = 504 000 J t = Q/P = 504 000 / 2000 = 252 s ≈ 4.2 分钟

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：汽化潜热 L = 2.26 MJ/kg = 2.26×10⁶ J/kg。Hint: latent heat of vaporisation L = 2.26 MJ/kg = 2.26×10⁶ J/kg.

水已在沸腾。要把 0.20 kg 的 100 °C 水全部变成蒸汽，还需要多少能量？Water is already boiling. How much energy is still needed to turn 0.20 kg of water at 100 °C completely into steam?
点开看答案Show answer
Q = mL = 0.20 × 2.26×10⁶ = 4.52 × 10⁵ J 对比：把同样 0.20 kg 水从 0 °C 烧到 100 °C 只要 8.4×10⁴ J，汽化贵了 5 倍多。Compare: warming the same 0.20 kg from 0 °C to 100 °C takes only 8.4×10⁴ J — vaporisation costs over five times more.
— 第 3 站完成 —— Stop 3 done —
4 理想气体状态方程 pV = nRTThe ideal gas equation pV = nRT Ideal gas理想气体
学完你会：用 pV = nRT 或它的比例形式处理任意状态变化。By the end: you can use pV = nRT, or its ratio form, for any change of state.
说人话：气体的压强、体积、温度这三个脾气互相制约，一个方程全管：pV = nRT。日常用法其实更省事——只要气体的量不变，pV/T 是常数，写 p₁V₁/T₁ = p₂V₂/T₂ 就可以在两个状态间跳来跳去。唯一的陷阱：温度必须换成开尔文。忘加 273 是这门课最常见的丢分点。 In plain words: a gas has three interlocking moods — pressure, volume and temperature — and one equation rules them: pV = nRT. In practice the ratio form is quicker: for a fixed amount of gas pV/T is constant, so p₁V₁/T₁ = p₂V₂/T₂ lets you hop between any two states. One trap: temperature must be in kelvin. Forgetting the +273 is the single most common way to lose marks here.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • pV = nRT；R = 8.31 J/(mol·K)；T 用 K。pV = nRT; R = 8.31 J/(mol·K); T in K.
  • 比例式：p₁V₁/T₁ = p₂V₂/T₂（同一份气体）。Ratio form: p₁V₁/T₁ = p₂V₂/T₂ for the same gas.
  • 也可用分子个数 N：pV = NkT（k 是玻尔兹曼常数）。Using the number of molecules N: pV = NkT with k the Boltzmann constant.
  • 单位：p 用 Pa，V 用 m³（1 cm³ = 10⁻⁶ m³，别忘乘）。Units: p in Pa, V in m³ (1 cm³ = 10⁻⁶ m³ — don't forget the conversion).

例题Example

27 °C 时某气体占 2.0×10⁻³ m³，压强 100 kPa。加热到 127 °C 且体积不变，求新压强。A gas occupies 2.0×10⁻³ m³ at 100 kPa and 27 °C. Heated to 127 °C at constant volume, what is the new pressure?
先换开尔文：T₁ = 300 K，T₂ = 400 K。Convert to kelvin first: T₁ = 300 K, T₂ = 400 K. 体积不变 → p/T = 常数：Volume fixed → p/T constant: p₂ = p₁ × T₂/T₁ = 100 × 400/300 = 133 kPa

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：先算物质的量 n = m / M。Hint: find the amount of substance first: n = m / M.

0.032 kg 的氧气（摩尔质量 32 g/mol）在 300 K 时占 2.0×10⁻³ m³。求压强。0.032 kg of oxygen (molar mass 32 g/mol) occupies 2.0×10⁻³ m³ at 300 K. Find the pressure.
点开看答案Show answer
n = 32 g / 32 g/mol = 1.0 mol p = nRT / V = 1.0 × 8.31 × 300 / 2.0×10⁻³ = 2493 / 0.002 = 1.25 × 10⁶ Pa
— 第 4 站完成 —— Stop 4 done —
5 分子动理论与压强的来源Kinetic theory: where pressure comes from Kinetic theory分子动理论
学完你会：把"压强"翻译成分子行为，并用 pV = ⅓Nm⟨c²⟩ 做简单计算。By the end: you can translate pressure into molecular behaviour and use pV = ⅓Nm⟨c²⟩ for simple calculations.
说人话：气体为什么会顶着容器壁？因为里面的分子在不停撞墙。一个分子撞上去弹回来，动量变化 2mc，按牛二定律就是给墙一个小小的力；一秒钟内这样的撞击多得数不清，平均下来就是稳定的压强。推导的结果是一条很有味道的式子：pV = ⅓ N m ⟨c²⟩。左边是我们能测的三个宏观量，右边全是分子的微观量——它把两个世界焊在了一起。 In plain words: why does a gas push on its container? Because molecules inside are constantly hitting the walls. One molecule bounces off and its momentum changes by 2mc — Newton's second law turns that into a small force. Countless such impacts each second average out into a steady pressure. The derivation gives a beautiful result: pV = ⅓ N m ⟨c²⟩. Macroscopic measurables on the left, microscopic molecular quantities on the right — it welds the two worlds together.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • pV = ⅓ N m ⟨c²⟩，N 是分子个数，⟨c²⟩ 是方均速率。pV = ⅓ N m ⟨c²⟩, where N is the number of molecules and ⟨c²⟩ the mean square speed.
  • 方均根速率 c(rms) = √⟨c²⟩，注意它不是平均速率。Root-mean-square speed c(rms) = √⟨c²⟩ — not the same as the average speed.
  • 分子本身的重力可忽略、分子自身体积可忽略、碰撞是弹性的——这就是理想气体假设。Ideal gas assumptions: molecular weight negligible, molecular volume negligible, collisions elastic.
  • ⟨c²⟩ 与温度成正比：热 = 分子跑得快。⟨c²⟩ ∝ temperature: hotter means molecules moving faster.

例题Example

容器中有 2.0×10²³ 个分子，每个质量 5.0×10⁻²⁶ kg，方均根速率 500 m/s，容器体积 0.010 m³。求压强。A 0.010 m³ vessel holds 2.0×10²³ molecules, each of mass 5.0×10⁻²⁶ kg, with rms speed 500 m/s. Find the pressure.
⟨c²⟩ = 500² = 2.5×10⁵ m²/s² p = ⅓Nm⟨c²⟩ / V = ⅓ × 2.0×10²³ × 5.0×10⁻²⁶ × 2.5×10⁵ / 0.010 分子：⅓ × 2500 = 833Numerator: ⅓ × 2500 = 833 p = 833 / 0.010 = 8.3 × 10⁴ Pa

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：先由 p、V、N、m 反解 ⟨c²⟩，再开方。Hint: solve pV = ⅓ Nm⟨c²⟩ for ⟨c²⟩, then take the square root.

1.0×10²³ 个氮分子（单个质量 4.7×10⁻²⁶ kg）装在 0.0050 m³ 的容器里，压强为 1.2×10⁵ Pa。求方均根速率。1.0×10²³ nitrogen molecules (each 4.7×10⁻²⁶ kg) fill a 0.0050 m³ vessel at 1.2×10⁵ Pa. Find the rms speed.
点开看答案Show answer
pV = 1.2×10⁵ × 0.0050 = 600 JpV = 1.2×10⁵ × 0.0050 = 600 J ⟨c²⟩ = 3pV / (Nm) = 3 × 600 / (1.0×10²³ × 4.7×10⁻²⁶) = 1800 / 4.7×10⁻³ = 3.83×10⁵ c(rms) = √(3.83×10⁵) = 619 m/s
— 第 5 站完成 —— Stop 5 done —
6 玻尔兹曼常数与温度的本质Boltzmann constant and the meaning of temperature k = R/N_A玻尔兹曼常数
学完你会：用 ½m⟨c²⟩ = 3/2 kT 把温度和分子速度连起来。By the end: you can use ½m⟨c²⟩ = 3/2 kT to link temperature and molecular speed.
说人话：这一站给"温度"一个干脆的定义：温度就是分子平均动能的标尺。½m⟨c²⟩ = (3/2)kT，其中 k 是玻尔兹曼常数，它其实就是"每个分子的气体常数"：k = R/N_A（把 8.31 平摊到一摩尔里的 6.02×10²³ 个分子头上）。于是绝对零度为什么存在也好理解了：分子动能为零就没法再低了。另外注意：不同气体在同一温度下，平均动能相同，但轻的跑得更快（因为 KE 一样，质量小的速度大）。 In plain words: this stop gives temperature a clean definition: temperature is the ruler for mean molecular kinetic energy. ½m⟨c²⟩ = (3/2)kT, where k is the Boltzmann constant, which is simply "the gas constant per molecule": k = R/N_A — 8.31 shared out among the 6.02×10²³ molecules in a mole. That explains why there is an absolute zero: kinetic energy cannot go below nothing. Note too that different gases at the same temperature have the same mean kinetic energy, but lighter molecules move faster (same KE, smaller mass means higher speed).
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • ½ m⟨c²⟩ = (3/2) k T；k = 1.38×10⁻²³ J/K。½ m⟨c²⟩ = (3/2) k T; k = 1.38×10⁻²³ J/K.
  • k = R / N_A；R = 8.31，N_A = 6.02×10²³ mol⁻¹。k = R / N_A; R = 8.31 and N_A = 6.02×10²³ mol⁻¹.
  • 同温度下，轻分子方均根速率更大（c(rms) ∝ 1/√m）。At the same temperature, lighter molecules have a larger rms speed (c(rms) ∝ 1/√m).
  • 平均动能＝0 对应 T = 0 K，这就是绝对零度的来源。Zero mean kinetic energy corresponds to T = 0 K — the origin of absolute zero.

例题Example

求 300 K 时分子的平均平动动能（用 J 表示）。Find the mean translational kinetic energy of a molecule at 300 K, in joules.
KE = (3/2)kT = 1.5 × 1.38×10⁻²³ × 300 = 6.2 × 10⁻²¹ J 顺手换 eV：6.2×10⁻²¹ / 1.6×10⁻¹⁹ ≈ 0.039 eV（室温下分子能量约零点零几 eV，是个很好记的量级）In eV: 6.2×10⁻²¹ / 1.6×10⁻¹⁹ ≈ 0.039 eV — a handy order of magnitude for room temperature

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：平均动能 ∝ T；温度翻倍，动能也翻倍，方均根速率只变 √2 倍。Hint: mean KE ∝ T; double T doubles KE, but the rms speed only grows by √2.

氦原子质量 6.6×10⁻²⁷ kg。求它在 300 K 时的方均根速率。A helium atom has mass 6.6×10⁻²⁷ kg. Find its rms speed at 300 K.
点开看答案Show answer
½m⟨c²⟩ = (3/2)kT → ⟨c²⟩ = 3kT/m = 3 × 1.38×10⁻²³ × 300 / 6.6×10⁻²⁷ ⟨c²⟩ = 1.242×10⁻²⁰ / 6.6×10⁻²⁷ = 1.88×10⁶ c(rms) = √(1.88×10⁶) = 1370 m/s
— 第 6 站完成 —— Stop 6 done —
7 热力学第一定律The first law of thermodynamics First law第一定律
学完你会：给任意过程写 ΔU = Q + W，并判断三个量的正负。By the end: you can write ΔU = Q + W for any process and get all three signs right.
说人话：第一定律说白了就是能量守恒的表白：气体内能的变化量 ΔU，等于吸进来的热 Q 加上 外界对它做的功 W。两个都当作"从外面进来为正"。于是：压一下气体（外界做功 W 为正）又不让它散热，它的内能一定上升、温度升高（打气筒发热正是如此）；气体膨胀顶开活塞（对外做功，W 为负），若不吸热，它会自己降温（喷雾罐用久了变凉）。 In plain words: the first law is simply conservation of energy spelled out: the change in internal energy ΔU equals the heat absorbed Q plus the work done on the gas W, with anything entering counted positive. So: squeeze a gas (W positive) and stop it losing heat, and its internal energy must rise, along with its temperature — which is exactly why a bicycle pump warms up. Let the gas expand and push a piston (W negative) without supplying heat, and it cools itself down — why an aerosol can chills after use.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • ΔU = Q + W，其中 Q 为吸收的热，W 为外界对气体做的功。ΔU = Q + W, with Q the heat absorbed and W the work done on the gas.
  • 气体膨胀 → 它对外做功 → W 为负。压缩 → W 为正。The gas expands → it does work → W is negative. Compress it → W positive.
  • 等容过程：体积不变 → W = 0 → ΔU = Q。Constant volume: W = 0, so ΔU = Q.
  • 等温（理想气体）：ΔU = 0 → Q + W = 0。Isothermal (ideal gas): ΔU = 0, so Q + W = 0.
  • 做功的计算常用 W = −pΔV（压强恒定时），ΔV > 0 膨胀就会得到负值。Work often uses W = −pΔV at constant pressure; ΔV > 0 for expansion gives a negative value.

例题Example

一定质量气体吸收 500 J 热量，同时被压缩，外界对它做功 200 J。求内能变化。A fixed mass of gas absorbs 500 J of heat while being compressed, with 200 J of work done on it. Find the change in internal energy.
ΔU = Q + W = 500 + 200 = 700 J 两条进水管都开着，内能涨得最多。Both inlets open, so the internal energy gains the most.

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：膨胀时气体对外做功，所以"外界对气体做的功"是负的。Hint: during expansion the gas does work on the surroundings, so W (on the gas) is negative.

气体在恒定压强 1.0×10⁵ Pa 下从 2.0×10⁻³ m³ 膨胀到 5.0×10⁻³ m³，同时吸热 200 J。求 ΔU。A gas expands from 2.0×10⁻³ m³ to 5.0×10⁻³ m³ at a constant 1.0×10⁵ Pa while absorbing 200 J. Find ΔU.
点开看答案Show answer
ΔV = 3.0×10⁻³ m³；W = −pΔV = −1.0×10⁵ × 3.0×10⁻³ = −300 J ΔU = Q + W = 200 − 300 = −100 J 内能反而减少了：膨胀"花掉"的比吸进来的还多。Internal energy actually falls: expanding cost more than the heat supplied.
— 第 7 站完成 —— Stop 7 done —
— 单元 9 完成 · 主线：热是分子的运动，pV = NkT 与 ΔU = Q + W 把宏观和微观缝在一起 —— Unit 9 done · one thread: heat is molecular motion; pV = NkT and ΔU = Q + W stitch macro to micro —

单元 10 · 电场与电容Unit 10 · Electric Fields & CapacitanceA2

A2 · 和引力几乎一模一样的一套公式，只是多了正负号；电容则是"存电的水箱"A2 · almost the same maths as gravity, plus a plus-or-minus sign; capacitors are water tanks for charge

本单元包括内容What this unit covers 今天要走 7 站7 stops today

点标题可以直接跳到那一站。每站 = 一张图 + 一道例题 + 一道练习题（答案点开才看）。Click a title to jump there. Each stop = one diagram + one worked example + one practice question.

1 库仑定律 F = Qq/(4πε₀r²)Coulomb's law Coulomb库仑定律
学完你会：算两个点电荷之间的力，并判断是吸引还是排斥。By the end: you can calculate the force between point charges and say whether it attracts or repels.
说人话：电学和引力的公式几乎可以套印：F = Qq/(4πε₀r²) 对 F = GMm/r²，都是"两个东西相乘、除以距离平方"。唯一多出来的是正负号：同号相斥、异号相吸，而引力永远相吸。那一长串常数 1/(4πε₀) ≈ 9.0×10⁹，比 G 大得离谱——所以一点点静电就能吊起纸屑，而整个地球才把你按在椅子上。 In plain words: electrostatics and gravity could have shared the printing plate: F = Qq/(4πε₀r²) against F = GMm/r² — two things multiplied, divided by distance squared. The only extra is the sign: like charges repel, unlike attract, whereas gravity only attracts. That constant 1/(4πε₀) ≈ 9.0×10⁹ dwarfs G, which is why a rubbed balloon can lift paper scraps while a whole planet merely holds you in your chair.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • F = Qq/(4πε₀r²)；1/(4πε₀) = 9.0×10⁹ N m² C⁻²。F = Qq/(4πε₀r²); 1/(4πε₀) = 9.0×10⁹ N m² C⁻².
  • 算大小用绝对值，方向另判：同斥异吸。Use magnitudes in the formula and settle the direction separately: like repel, unlike attract.
  • 这是一对作用力与反作用力，两个电荷受力大小相等。Always an action–reaction pair: both feel the same force.
  • 距离翻倍 → 力变 1/4（还是平方反比）。Double the separation → quarter the force (inverse square again).
  • 默认介质是真空；换成别的介质，ε₀ 要换成相应的 ε。This assumes vacuum; in another medium ε₀ is replaced by the appropriate ε.

例题Example

相距 3.0 cm 的两个小球，各带 +2.0 μC 和 −3.0 μC。求它们之间的力。Two small spheres 3.0 cm apart carry +2.0 μC and −3.0 μC. Find the force between them.
F = (9.0×10⁹ × 2.0×10⁻⁶ × 3.0×10⁻⁶) / (0.030)² 分子：9.0×10⁹ × 6.0×10⁻¹² = 5.4×10⁻²Numerator: 9.0×10⁹ × 6.0×10⁻¹² = 5.4×10⁻² F = 5.4×10⁻² / 9.0×10⁻⁴ = 60 N 异号 → 相互吸引。Unlike signs → the force is attractive.

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：距离变成 4 倍时用比例最快。Hint: ratios are quickest when the separation goes up by a factor.

两个点电荷相距 2.0 cm 时作用力为 36 N。距离改成 8.0 cm 后力是多少？Two point charges feel 36 N at 2.0 cm apart. What force at 8.0 cm?
点开看答案Show answer
距离 ×4 → 力 ×1/16Distance ×4 → force ×1/16 F = 36 / 16 = 2.25 N
— 第 1 站完成 —— Stop 1 done —
2 电场强度 E = F/qElectric field strength E = F/q E-field电场强度
学完你会：区分"电场强度"和"受力"，并算点电荷在某处产生的 E。By the end: you can separate field strength from the force felt, and find E due to a point charge.
说人话：电场强度描述的是位置本身：某个点有多"厉害"，只要本领用一个小试探电荷去量——把它放进去看受力多大，再除以它自己的电量：E = F/q。因为是"除法"，E 与试探电荷无关（就像 gravitational field strength g 与物体质量无关）。把库仑定律代进来，点电荷在 r 处的场强就是 E = Q/(4πε₀r²)。单位是 N/C（等价 V/m）。 In plain words: field strength describes the place, not the probe. To measure it you drop in a small test charge, see how hard it is pushed, and divide by its charge: E = F/q. Because it is a division, E does not depend on the test charge (exactly as g does not depend on the object's mass). Substituting Coulomb's law gives the field of a point charge at distance r: E = Q/(4πε₀r²), in N/C (equivalently V/m).
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • E = F/q（定义）；点电荷 E = Q/(4πε₀r²)。E = F/q (definition); for a point charge E = Q/(4πε₀r²).
  • 单位是 N/C，也可以写 V/m，两者等价。Units N/C or equivalently V/m.
  • E 是矢量：方向规定为正电荷受力方向（所以指向负电荷）。E is a vector: its direction is where a positive charge would be pushed (so it points towards a negative charge).
  • 多个电荷的情况用矢量叠加：先画方向再加减。With several charges, add as vectors — sketch the directions first.

例题Example

距 +5.0 μC 点电荷 0.20 m 处的电场强度多大？在那里放一个 +2.0 nC 电荷受力多少？What is the field strength 0.20 m from a +5.0 μC point charge, and what force acts on a +2.0 nC charge placed there?
E = 9.0×10⁹ × 5.0×10⁻⁶ / 0.20² = 4.5×10⁴ / 0.040 = 1.13×10⁶ N/C F = qE = 2.0×10⁻⁹ × 1.13×10⁶ = 2.3×10⁻³ N 正电荷受力方向与 E 相同（向外，远离 +Q）。A positive charge is pushed along E, i.e. away from +Q.

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：先把 μC 换成 C。Hint: convert μC into coulombs first.

某处放 −1.5 nC 电荷时受到 3.0×10⁻⁴ N 向东的力。求该处电场强度的大小与方向。A charge of −1.5 nC experiences 3.0×10⁻⁴ N towards the east. Find the field strength and direction there.
点开看答案Show answer
E = F/|q| = 3.0×10⁻⁴ / 1.5×10⁻⁹ = 2.0×10⁵ N/C 负电荷受力与 E 反向，故 E 指向向西。A negative charge is pushed against E, so E points west.
— 第 2 站完成 —— Stop 2 done —
3 电场线与匀强电场 E = V/dField lines and uniform fields: E = V/d Uniform field匀强电场
学完你会：读懂电场线图，并在平行板之间用 E = V/d 换算。By the end: you can read a field-line picture and use E = V/d between parallel plates.
说人话：电场线是给看不见的电场画的一张地图：线从正电荷出发、到负电荷结束；越密代表场强越大；箭头方向就是正电荷会被推的方向。两块平行带电金属板之间，这张地图特别简单——平行且疏密均匀，说明场强处处相同，叫匀强电场。此时一条极其好用的公式出现了：E = V/d，电压除以板间距就是场强。 In plain words: field lines are simply a map of an invisible field: they start on positive and end on negative charge; closer lines mean a stronger field; the arrow shows which way a positive charge would be shoved. Between two parallel charged plates the map is particularly simple — parallel and evenly spaced, meaning the field strength is the same everywhere: a uniform field. Out pops a wonderfully handy formula: E = V/d, the voltage divided by the plate separation.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • 匀强电场：E = V/d；由此 V = Ed。Uniform field: E = V/d, hence V = Ed.
  • 电场线疏密代表强弱，箭头代表方向。Spacing shows strength, arrows show direction.
  • 电场线与等势面垂直，且由高电势指向低电势。Lines meet equipotentials at right angles, running from high to low potential.
  • 板外几乎为零（理想情况下）；边缘会有些"散开"，实际考题忽略。Outside the plates the field is essentially zero; exam questions ignore fringing at the edges.

例题Example

两板相距 2.0 cm，加上 500 V 电压。求板间场强和一个电子受到的电力的大小（e = 1.6×10⁻¹⁹ C）。Plates 2.0 cm apart have 500 V across them. Find the field strength and the force on an electron (e = 1.6×10⁻¹⁹ C).
E = V/d = 500 / 0.020 = 2.5×10⁴ V/m F = qE = 1.6×10⁻¹⁹ × 2.5×10⁴ = 4.0×10⁻¹⁵ N 电子带负电，受力方向与 E 相反。The electron is negative, so the force is opposite to E.

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：mm 要换成 m。Hint: convert mm into metres.

板间距 5.0 mm，希望板间场强达到 3.0×10⁵ V/m，需要加多大电压？Plates are 5.0 mm apart. What voltage gives a field of 3.0×10⁵ V/m?
点开看答案Show answer
V = Ed = 3.0×10⁵ × 5.0×10⁻³ = 1500 V
— 第 3 站完成 —— Stop 3 done —
4 电势 V 与零点Electric potential V and the zero of potential Potential电势
学完你会：算某点的电势，并理解"电势差 = 每单位电荷搬运的功"。By the end: you can find the potential at a point and read potential difference as work per unit charge.
说人话：如果电场强度是"这地方力有多猛"，那么电势就是"这地方能量有多高"。定义：把一个单位正电荷从无穷远搬到该处需要做的功，就是该点的电势（并规定无穷远处为零）。公式 V = Q/(4πε₀r)——注意是 1/r 而不是 1/r²，因为它量的是能量而不是力。电势随 Q 的正负可以有正负：正电荷周围电势为正，负电荷周围为负。 In plain words: if field strength is "how hard this place pushes", then potential is "how much energy this place holds". Definition: the work done in bringing a unit positive charge from infinity to that point, with infinity defined as zero. The formula is V = Q/(4πε₀r) — note 1/r rather than 1/r², because it measures energy, not force. Potential can be positive or negative: positive around a +Q, negative around a −Q.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • V = Q/(4πε₀r)，零点设在无穷远。V = Q/(4πε₀r), with zero at infinity.
  • 电势差（电压）V = W/q：单位正电荷从一处搬到另一处要做的功。Potential difference V = W/q: work per unit positive charge moved between the points.
  • 电势是标量，相加直接加减 numerically（比矢量叠加省事）。Potential is a scalar — just add numbers, no vector triangle needed.
  • 正电荷顺着电场方向走，电势降低。A positive charge moving along the field goes to lower potential.

例题Example

求距 +6.0 μC 点电荷 0.30 m 处的电势；把 +2.0 μC 电荷放在那里，它具有多少电势能？Find the potential 0.30 m from a +6.0 μC charge, and the potential energy of a +2.0 μC charge placed there.
V = 9.0×10⁹ × 6.0×10⁻⁶ / 0.30 = 5.4×10⁴ / 0.30 = 1.8×10⁵ V Ep = qV = 2.0×10⁻⁶ × 1.8×10⁵ = 0.36 J

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：电场与电势差的关系 V = Ed 也在这里能用。Hint: V = Ed still applies, linking field and potential difference.

匀强电场 E = 4.0×10³ V/m，沿电场方向相距 5.0 cm 的两点间电势差是多少？哪点电势高？In a uniform field of 4.0×10³ V/m, what is the potential difference between points 5.0 cm apart along the field, and which is at higher potential?
点开看答案Show answer
V = Ed = 4.0×10³ × 0.050 = 200 V 逆着电场方向的起点更高：电场总从高电势指向低电势。The point upstream is higher: fields always point from high to low potential.
— 第 4 站完成 —— Stop 4 done —
5 等势面与移动电荷做功Equipotentials and moving charge Work做功
学完你会：判断搬电荷要不要做功，几条微焦级别的功夫就够。By the end: you can tell whether moving a charge takes work, with just a few microjoules of effort.
说人话：等势面就是电势相同的一张面（或线），完全对应地图上的等高线：沿着等高线走不费力，跨过它才算"爬坡"。所以在同一个等势面上移动电荷，不做功（W = 0）；跨越电势差才要做功：W = qΔV。另外记住，电场线永远垂直穿过等势面——这是画图题的评分点。 In plain words: an equipotential is a surface (or line) of constant potential, exactly like a contour line on a map: walking along a contour costs no effort, crossing one is climbing. So moving a charge along an equipotential takes no work (W = 0), whereas a potential difference does: W = qΔV. Also note that field lines always cross equipotentials at right angles — a standard mark in drawing questions.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • W = qΔV；沿等势面走，ΔV = 0，W = 0。W = qΔV; along an equipotential ΔV = 0 so W = 0.
  • 电场线垂直于等势面。Field lines are perpendicular to equipotentials.
  • 移动路径不重要，只看起点和终点的电势差（保守场）。The route does not matter — only the start and end potentials (a conservative field).
  • 负电荷的电势能随电势升高而降低（注意 q 是负的）。A negative charge loses potential energy as potential rises (q is negative).

例题Example

把 +3.0 μC 电荷从电势 200 V 处搬到 500 V 处，需要做多少功？换成电子呢？How much work to move +3.0 μC from a point at 200 V to one at 500 V? And for an electron?
ΔV = 300 V；W = qΔV = 3.0×10⁻⁶ × 300 = 9.0×10⁻⁴ J 电子 q = −1.6×10⁻¹⁹ C：W = −1.6×10⁻¹⁹ × 300 = −4.8×10⁻¹⁷ J（负值说明电场自己推着它走）For an electron, q = −1.6×10⁻¹⁹ C: W = −1.6×10⁻¹⁹ × 300 = −4.8×10⁻¹⁷ J — negative, meaning the field does the pushing

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：路径只是绕了个圈，看起点终点电势差即可。Hint: the route wanders, but only the endpoints matter.

在 +Q 的电场中，把 +2.0 μC 电荷绕着该电荷沿同一等势面走一圈，做功多少？A +2.0 μC charge is carried right round a closed equipotential around +Q. How much work is done?
点开看答案Show answer
始终在同一等势面上 → ΔV = 0 → W = 0Always on the same equipotential → ΔV = 0 → W = 0
— 第 5 站完成 —— Stop 5 done —
6 电容 C 与平行板电容器Capacitance and the parallel-plate capacitor Capacitance电容
学完你会：用 C = Q/V 与 C = ε₀A/d 处理电容器，知道怎么把电容做大。By the end: you can handle capacitors with C = Q/V and C = ε₀A/d, and know what makes capacitance larger.
说人话：电容器就是存电的水箱。它的标称容量叫电容：C = Q/V，也就是"每加 1 伏特电压能存下多少库仑电荷"。1 法拉大得吓人，实际用的都是 μF、nF、pF。结构上想把容量做大，只有三条路：极板面积更大、板间距更小、中间垫上介质（增大 ε）。手机里那些指甲盖大小的元件就是靠卷起来加特殊介质，塞进好几法拉。 In plain words: a capacitor is a water tank for charge. Its capacity is its capacitance: C = Q/V, how many coulombs it stores per volt applied. One farad is enormous in practice, so real parts come in μF, nF and pF. To build more capacity you have exactly three levers: more plate area, smaller separation, and a dielectric between them (raising ε). The thumbnail-sized parts in a phone achieve whole farads by rolling the plates up with exotic dielectrics.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • C = Q/V；平行板 C = ε₀A/d（真空/空气时）。C = Q/V; parallel plates C = ε₀A/d (vacuum or air).
  • 单位：1 F = 10⁶ μF = 10⁹ nF = 10¹² pF。Units: 1 F = 10⁶ μF = 10⁹ nF = 10¹² pF.
  • 串联↓、并联↑ 电容与电阻正好相反。Capacitors combine the opposite way to resistors: series gives less, parallel gives more.
  • 插介质会增大电容；这与"介质削弱场强"是一致的。Adding a dielectric raises capacitance, consistent with its weakening of the field.

例题Example

平行板面积 0.020 m²，间距 0.50 mm，空气介质。求电容与加上 12 V 时储存的电荷。Parallel plates of area 0.020 m² separated by 0.50 mm in air. Find the capacitance and the charge stored at 12 V.
C = ε₀A/d = 8.85×10⁻¹² × 0.020 / 5.0×10⁻⁴ = 1.77×10⁻¹³ / 5.0×10⁻⁴ C = 3.5×10⁻¹⁰ F = 350 pF Q = CV = 3.5×10⁻¹⁰ × 12 = 4.2×10⁻⁹ C

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：先由 Q 和 V 求 C，再用比值看板间距的影响。Hint: find C from Q and V, then use ratios for the separation effect.

100 μF 电容加 6.0 V 时带多少电荷？若把板间距加倍而介质不变，电容变为多少？A 100 μF capacitor is charged to 6.0 V. What charge is stored, and what is the capacitance if the plate separation is doubled?
点开看答案Show answer
Q = CV = 100×10⁻⁶ × 6.0 = 6.0×10⁻⁴ C C ∝ 1/d，间距加倍 → C 减半 = 50 μFC ∝ 1/d, so doubling d halves it to 50 μF
— 第 6 站完成 —— Stop 6 done —
7 电容器储能与充放电Energy stored, charging and discharging Energy储能
学完你会：算电容器存了多少能量，并读懂指数型的充放电曲线。By the end: you can work out energy stored and read exponential charge/discharge curves.
说人话：给电容器充电时，电压不是一下子到位的：一开始轻松，越充越费劲，所以平均电压只有最终的一半，能量就是 W = ½QV = ½CV²——那个 ½ 正是 Q–V 图里三角形的来历。放电时，电压按指数曲线一路滑落，速度取决于 RC 乘积（叫时间常数 τ = RC）：电阻越大放得越慢。相机闪光灯就是典型的"慢慢充、瞬间放"。 In plain words: charging does not happen all at once: it starts easy and gets harder, so the average voltage is only half the final value, giving W = ½QV = ½CV² — that ½ is literally the triangle under the Q–V graph. On discharge, the voltage slides down an exponential curve at a rate set by the RC product, called the time constant τ = RC: bigger resistance, slower drain. A camera flash is the classic slow-charge, instant-discharge device.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • W = ½QV = ½CV² = ½Q²/C。W = ½QV = ½CV² = ½Q²/C.
  • ½ 的来源：充电全过程中平均电压 = V/2。The ½ comes from the average voltage being V/2 over the whole charge.
  • 放电：Q = Q₀ e^(−t/RC)，时间常数 τ = RC。Discharge: Q = Q₀ e^(−t/RC) with time constant τ = RC.
  • 每过一个 τ，电压剩 37%；约 5τ 后可认为放完。After each τ about 37% remains; after roughly 5τ it is effectively empty.

例题Example

470 μF 电容充电到 12 V。求储存的电荷和能量。A 470 μF capacitor is charged to 12 V. Find the charge and energy stored.
Q = CV = 470×10⁻⁶ × 12 = 5.6×10⁻³ C W = ½CV² = ½ × 470×10⁻⁶ × 144 = 3.4×10⁻² J

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：τ = RC；一个 τ 后剩 37%。Hint: τ = RC; 37% remains after one τ.

1000 μF 电容经 10 kΩ 电阻放电。求时间常数 τ，以及 10 s 后剩余电荷的比例。A 1000 μF capacitor discharges through a 10 kΩ resistor. Find τ and the fraction of charge left after 10 s.
点开看答案Show answer
τ = RC = 10 000 × 1000×10⁻⁶ = 10 s Q/Q₀ = e^(−10/10) = e⁻¹ = 0.37 （正好剩下 37%）(exactly 37% remains)
— 第 7 站完成 —— Stop 7 done —

单元 11 · 磁与感应Unit 11 · Magnetism and InductionA2

1 磁场图景：条形磁铁、导线、线圈Field patterns: bar magnet, wire, coil Field patterns磁感线
学完你会：画出三种磁场图景，并能判断导线周围磁场的方向。By the end: you can sketch all three patterns and judge the direction of the field around a wire.
说人话：磁场也有"地图"，规则和电场很像：磁场线永不相交、越密越强。三个必须记牢的画面：条形磁铁外部由 N 指向 S；长直导线周围是一圈圈同心圆，方向用右手握拳定则——拇指指电流，四指弯曲的方向就是磁场绕的方向；长线圈（螺线管）内部几乎是一片匀强磁场，强度可以靠加铁芯、加电流、加密匝数大幅提升，这就是电磁铁。 In plain words: magnetic fields have maps too, following much the same rules: lines never cross and closer means stronger. Three pictures you must know: a bar magnet with lines going N to S outside; a straight wire circled by concentric rings, direction set by the right-hand grip rule — thumb along the current, fingers curl along the field; and a long solenoid, whose interior is almost uniform and can be strengthened enormously with an iron core, more current, or more turns per metre. That is an electromagnet.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • 磁感线不相交；外部由 N 指向 S。Lines never cross; outside they run N to S.
  • 右手握拳定则判导线周围方向：拇指 = 电流，四指 = B。Right-hand grip rule: thumb = current, curled fingers = B.
  • 螺线管内部≈匀强；插入铁芯显著增强。Solenoid interior ≈ uniform; an iron core boosts it sharply.
  • 电流越大、离导线越近 → 磁场越强。More current or a closer position → stronger field.

例题Example

一根竖直导线中电流向上。在它的正东方向放一枚小磁针，磁针 N 极指向哪里？A vertical wire carries current upwards. A plotting compass is placed due east of it — which way does its N pole point?
右手握拳：拇指向上，四指在正东一侧指向北。Grip rule: thumb up, so on the east side the curled fingers point north. 磁针 N 极顺着磁场方向 → 指向北。The N pole follows the field, so it points north.

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：电流方向反过来，环绕方向也反过来。Hint: reverse the current and the whole circulation reverses.

若上述导线电流改为向下，导线正东侧的磁场方向变为哪个方向？If the current runs downwards instead, which way does the field point east of the wire?
点开看答案Show answer
拇指改为向下，四指在该侧指向南。Thumb now points down, so the fingers there point south.
— 第 1 站完成 —— Stop 1 done —
2 通电导线受力 F = BILForce on a current-carrying wire: F = BIL Motor effect电动机效应
学完你会：用 F = BIL 算力的大小，用左手定则判方向。By the end: F = BIL for the size, the left-hand rule for the direction.
说人话：一根通了电的导线放在磁场里，会被推。力的大小 F = BIL sinθ（θ 是导线与磁场的夹角；垂直时最大，平行时为零），方向用弗莱明左手定则：食指磁场、中指电流、拇指推力，三者互相垂直。考纲里要求能说出导线为什么会被推：导线里的电流是定向漂移的电荷，每个电荷都受到第 3 站要讲的洛伦兹力，加起来就是整根导线的合力。 In plain words: put a current-carrying wire in a magnetic field and it gets pushed. The size is F = BIL sinθ (θ between wire and field; maximum when perpendicular, zero when parallel), and the direction comes from Fleming's left-hand rule: index = field, middle = current, thumb = force, all mutually perpendicular. The syllabus also wants the reason: the current is drifting charge, and every carrier feels the Lorentz force (next stop) — add them up and you have the force on the wire.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • F = BIL sinθ；只有垂直于磁场的那段 L 才有效。F = BIL sinθ; only the length perpendicular to the field counts.
  • 弗莱明左手定则：食指 B、中指 I、拇指 F。Fleming's left-hand rule: index B, middle I, thumb F.
  • 这是电动机效应：电能 → 机械能。This is the motor effect: electrical energy into mechanical.
  • 线圈两侧受力方向相反，形成转矩（转起来）。Opposite forces on the two sides of a coil make a torque — and it spins.

例题Example

一段 0.15 m 的导线垂直放在 0.40 T 磁场中，通有 3.0 A 电流。求它受到的力。A 0.15 m wire sits perpendicular to a 0.40 T field carrying 3.0 A. Find the force on it.
垂直 → sinθ = 1：Perpendicular → sinθ = 1: F = BIL = 0.40 × 3.0 × 0.15 = 0.18 N

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：导线与磁场夹 30°，取 sin30°。Hint: the wire is at 30° to the field, so use sin 30°.

0.20 m 导线通 5.0 A，与 0.25 T 匀强磁场成 30°。求受力。A 0.20 m wire carrying 5.0 A lies at 30° to a uniform 0.25 T field. Find the force.
点开看答案Show answer
F = BIL sinθ = 0.25 × 5.0 × 0.20 × sin30° = 0.25 × 0.50 = 0.125 N
— 第 2 站完成 —— Stop 2 done —
3 运动电荷受力 F = BQvForce on a moving charge: F = BQv Lorentz force洛伦兹力
学完你会：算单个电荷在磁场中的受力，并知道为什么会走圆。By the end: you can find the force on a single charge and explain why its path is a circle.
说人话：把第 2 站的公式拆解到单个电荷头上，就是 F = BQv sinθ。关键性质是这个力永远垂直于速度——和第 7 单元的向心力一模一样——所以它只改变方向，不改变快慢，粒子在匀强磁场里画出一个标准的圆（或螺旋）。把 BQv 等于 mv²/r 还能得到 r = mv/(BQ)：速度越大圆越大，磁场越强圆越小。质谱仪、回旋加速器、老式电视的偏转线圈，全靠这条。 In plain words: shrink the previous formula down to one charge and you get F = BQv sinθ. Its crucial property is that it is always perpendicular to the velocity — exactly like the centripetal force of Unit 7 — so it changes direction, never speed, and the particle traces a perfect circle (or a helix) in a uniform field. Setting BQv = mv²/r even gives r = mv/(BQ): faster particles loop wider, stronger fields squeeze them tighter. Mass spectrometers, cyclotrons and the deflection coils of old televisions all rest on this.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • F = BQv sinθ；速度平行于磁场时不受力。F = BQv sinθ; moving parallel to the field gives no force.
  • 力⊥速度 → 不做功，速率不变。Force ⊥ velocity → no work, so speed is unchanged.
  • 圆周：BQv = mv²/r → r = mv/(BQ)。Circular path: BQv = mv²/r → r = mv/(BQ).
  • 正负电荷偏转方向相反（这也是弗莱明左手定则：中指是电流方向）。Opposite signs curve opposite ways (the left-hand rule uses conventional current).

例题Example

电子以 3.0×10⁷ m/s 垂直射入 0.50 T 匀强磁场。求受力大小与轨道半径（e = 1.6×10⁻¹⁹ C，m = 9.1×10⁻³¹ kg）。An electron enters a 0.50 T uniform field perpendicularly at 3.0×10⁷ m/s. Find the force and its orbital radius (e = 1.6×10⁻¹⁹ C, m = 9.1×10⁻³¹ kg).
F = BQv = 0.50 × 1.6×10⁻¹⁹ × 3.0×10⁷ = 2.4×10⁻¹² N r = mv/(BQ) = (9.1×10⁻³¹ × 3.0×10⁷) / (0.50 × 1.6×10⁻¹⁹) = 2.73×10⁻²³ / 8.0×10⁻²⁰ r = 3.4×10⁻⁴ m

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：平行穿行时 sinθ = 0。Hint: parallel motion means sinθ = 0.

质子以 5.0×10⁶ m/s 沿磁场方向射入。它受到的磁力多大？轨道会弯吗？A proton enters at 5.0×10⁶ m/s travelling along the field direction. What magnetic force acts, and does its path bend?
点开看答案Show answer
速度与磁场平行 → F = 0，沿直线前进，不偏转。Velocity parallel to the field → F = 0; it goes straight on, undeflected.
— 第 3 站完成 —— Stop 3 done —
4 B 的定义与霍尔效应Defining flux density and the Hall effect Hall effect霍尔效应
学完你会：说出磁感应强度 B 的定义，并解释霍尔探头怎么测磁场。By the end: you can define flux density B and explain how a Hall probe measures it.
说人话：既然通电导线在磁场里会受力，那反过来就有了 B 的定义：把一段通 1 安培、长 1 米的导线垂直放进磁场，它受多少牛顿的力，B 就是多少特斯拉，即 B = F/(IL)。实际操作里没法时时称"受力"，于是用霍尔探头：一块通电薄片，磁场把漂流电荷推向一侧，两侧积出微小电压——电压与 B 成正比，量电压就知道磁场。手机里的电子罗盘、汽车里的轮速传感器都是这个原理。 In plain words: since a current-carrying wire feels a force, we can turn that round into a definition of B: put a 1-metre wire carrying 1 amp perpendicular to the field, and however many newtons of force it feels is B in tesla — that is B = F/(IL). Weighing forces is impractical in the lab, so we use a Hall probe instead: a current-carrying slice whose drifting charges get pushed to one side, building a tiny voltage proportional to B. Measure the voltage, know the field. Your phone's compass and your car's wheel-speed sensors both work this way.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • B = F/(IL)，要求导线与磁场垂直；单位 T。B = F/(IL) with the wire perpendicular to the field; unit tesla.
  • 1 T = 1 N/(A·m)；地球磁场才约 50 μT。1 T = 1 N/(A·m); the Earth's field is only about 50 μT.
  • 霍尔效应：一侧堆积电荷 → 横向霍尔电压，∝ B。Hall effect: charge piles up on one side → a transverse Hall voltage ∝ B.
  • 霍尔电压很小，实际探头要接放大器。The Hall voltage is small, so practical probes include amplification.

例题Example

垂直放置的 0.25 m 导线通 4.0 A 时受力 0.60 N。求该处磁感应强度。A 0.25 m wire carrying 4.0 A perpendicular to a field feels 0.60 N. Find the flux density.
B = F/(IL) = 0.60 / (4.0 × 0.25) = 0.60 / 1.0 = 0.60 T

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：探头读数 ∝ B；读数翻倍意味着 B 也翻倍。Hint: the reading is proportional to B, so double the reading means double the field.

霍尔探头在某处读数为 30 mV，移到另一处变为 45 mV。若第一处 B = 0.20 T，第二处磁场是多少？A Hall probe reads 30 mV at one spot and 45 mV at another. If the first field is 0.20 T, what is the second?
点开看答案Show answer
B₂ = 0.20 × (45 / 30) = 0.30 T
— 第 4 站完成 —— Stop 4 done —
5 磁通量 Φ = BAMagnetic flux Φ = BA Flux磁通量
学完你会：用 Φ = BA cosθ 算穿过某个面的磁通量，注意角度定义。By the end: you can compute flux with Φ = BA cosθ and get the angle convention right.
说人话：磁通量可以粗暴地理解成"穿过这个框的磁感线条数"：Φ = BA cosθ。这里最容易错的是 θ——它不是框面与磁场的夹角，而是面的法线（垂直于面的假想线）与磁场的夹角。所以磁场正对着框面穿过时 θ = 0，磁通最大；磁场平行于框面（擦着过去）时磁通为零。线圈有 N 匝就再乘 N，叫磁通匝链数 NΦ。 In plain words: think of flux crudely as how many field lines thread the loop: Φ = BA cosθ. The trap is θ — it is not the angle between the field and the surface, but between the field and the normal to the surface. So when the field passes straight through, θ = 0 and flux is maximum; when the field skims along the plane, flux is zero. With N turns, multiply by N to get the flux linkage NΦ.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • Φ = BA cosθ，θ 是 B 与法线的夹角，单位 Wb。Φ = BA cosθ, θ between B and the normal; unit weber (Wb).
  • 面正对磁场时 Φ = BA；面平行磁场时 Φ = 0。Face square-on: Φ = BA. Face edge-on: Φ = 0.
  • N 匝线圈：磁通匝链数 NΦ。N turns: flux linkage NΦ.
  • 1 Wb = 1 T·m²。1 Wb = 1 T·m².

例题Example

面积 0.020 m² 的线圈置于 0.50 T 匀强磁场中，线圈面垂直于磁场。求磁通量；若线圈有 200 匝，磁通匝链数是多少？A coil of area 0.020 m² sits in a uniform 0.50 T field with its plane perpendicular to the field. Find the flux, and the flux linkage if it has 200 turns.
面垂直于磁场 → 磁场沿法线 → θ = 0：Plane perpendicular to B means B along the normal, so θ = 0: Φ = BA = 0.50 × 0.020 = 0.010 Wb NΦ = 200 × 0.010 = 2.0 Wb

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：线圈面与磁场平行，意味着法线与磁场成 90°。Hint: the plane parallel to B means the normal is at 90° to it.

面积 0.050 m² 的线圈面平行于 0.80 T 匀强磁场。此时穿过它的磁通量是多少？A coil of area 0.050 m² lies with its plane parallel to a 0.80 T field. What flux threads it?
点开看答案Show answer
Φ = BA cos90° = 0
— 第 5 站完成 —— Stop 5 done —
6 法拉第定律与楞次定律Faraday's and Lenz's laws Induction电磁感应
学完你会：用法拉第定律算感应电动势大小，用楞次定律定方向。By the end: Faraday for the size, Lenz for the direction.
说人话：发电机的原理一句话：磁通量一变，就有电动势。法拉第定律给大小：ε = −NΔΦ/Δt（磁通匝链数变化得越快，电动势越大）。那个负号是楞次定律：感应出来的东西总在反对引起它的变化——你往里推磁铁它就推开你，你拉走它就拽住你。这不是大自然任性，而是能量守恒的必然：要是它顺着推，能量就凭空翻倍了。 In plain words: the whole principle of the generator in one line: change the flux and you get an e.m.f. Faraday's law gives its size, ε = −NΔΦ/Δt — the faster the flux linkage changes, the bigger the e.m.f. The minus sign is Lenz's law: whatever appears always opposes the change that caused it. Push a magnet in and it shoves back; pull it out and it clings on. That is not nature being perverse — it is conservation of energy insisting. If it helped you along, energy would multiply from nowhere.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • ε = −N ΔΦ/Δt；磁通变化率才是关键，不是磁通本身多大。ε = −N ΔΦ/Δt; it is the rate of change that matters, not how big the flux is.
  • 楞次定律：感应效应总阻碍引起它的变化（不是阻碍运动本身）。Lenz's law: the induced effect opposes the change — not the motion as such.
  • 导体切割磁感线时也可写 ε = BLv（每根导体棒）。A single rod cutting lines gives ε = BLv.
  • 增大感应：更强的 B、更大的面积、更快的运动、更多匝数。To induce more: stronger B, bigger area, faster movement, more turns.

例题Example

500 匝线圈的磁通匝链数在 0.20 s 内从 1.5 Wb 均匀降到 0.30 Wb。求平均感应电动势。The flux linkage of a 500-turn coil falls uniformly from 1.5 Wb to 0.30 Wb in 0.20 s. Find the mean induced e.m.f.
Δ(NΦ) = 0.30 − 1.5 = −1.2 Wb ε = −Δ(NΦ)/Δt = −(−1.2) / 0.20 = 6.0 V

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：面积也是线圈的一部分，可以直接算 NΦ 的变化。Hint: area is part of the linkage, so find the change in NΦ directly.

100 匝、面积 0.010 m² 的线圈在 0.50 s 内从「正对磁场」转到「面平行磁场」，B = 0.40 T。求平均电动势。A 100-turn coil of area 0.010 m² turns from face-on to edge-on in 0.50 s in a 0.40 T field. Find the mean e.m.f.
点开看答案Show answer
初始 NΦ = 100 × 0.40 × 0.010 = 0.40 Wb；末了 Φ = 0Initially NΦ = 100 × 0.40 × 0.010 = 0.40 Wb; finally Φ = 0 ε = 0.40 / 0.50 = 0.80 V
— 第 6 站完成 —— Stop 6 done —
7 交流电与变压器Alternating current and transformers AC交流电
学完你会：区分峰值与有效值，并用变压器公式处理升降压。By the end: you can separate peak from rms values and handle step-up and step-down transformers.
说人话：发电厂的发电机输出的是正弦交流：电压忽正忽负、来回摆。说它的"大小"要说清是哪一种——峰值 V₀ 是最高点到中线的距离，有效值 V(rms) 是"跟多大直流电发热效果一样"的等效值，两者关系 V(rms) = V₀/√2。市电说的 230 V 就是有效值，它的峰值其实有 325 V。变压器则靠两组匝数不同的线圈共享同一份变化的磁通：Vs/Vp = Ns/Np；理想情况下功率守恒，所以电压升几倍、电流就降几倍，这正是远距离输电用高压的原因。 In plain words: the generator in a power station produces sinusoidal AC: voltage swinging positive, then negative, then back. Saying "how big" it is needs care — the peak V₀ is how far it reaches from the middle, while the rms value is the equivalent d.c. that would heat things equally, with V(rms) = V₀/√2. Mains quoted as 230 V is the rms value; it actually peaks near 325 V. A transformer simply has two coils sharing the same changing flux: Vs/Vp = Ns/Np. With an ideal transformer power is conserved, so raising the voltage lowers the current by the same factor — which is exactly why we transmit electricity at high voltage.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • V(rms) = V₀/√2，I(rms) = I₀/√2。V(rms) = V₀/√2 and I(rms) = I₀/√2.
  • 功率用有效值算：P = V(rms) I(rms)。Power uses rms: P = V(rms) I(rms).
  • 变压器：Vs/Vp = Ns/Np；理想 VpIp = VsIs。Transformer: Vs/Vp = Ns/Np; ideal: VpIp = VsIs.
  • 必须用交流电：恒定直流不产生变化的磁通，变压器不工作。It needs AC: steady d.c. gives no changing flux, so the transformer does nothing.
  • 实际变压器有损耗：线圈发热、铁芯涡流、漏磁。Real transformers lose power: coil heating, core eddy currents, flux leakage.

例题Example

市电有效值 230 V。求峰值；若接 60 W 的灯泡，电流有效值多大？Mains rms voltage is 230 V. Find the peak value and the rms current for a 60 W lamp.
V₀ = 230 × √2 = 325 V I(rms) = P / V(rms) = 60 / 230 = 0.26 A

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：理想变压器功率不变。Hint: for an ideal transformer power is unchanged.

变压器初级 400 匝接 230 V 市电，次级 100 匝。求次级电压；若次级电流为 2.0 A，初级电流多大？A transformer has 400 primary turns on 230 V mains and 100 secondary turns. Find the output voltage, and the primary current if the secondary draws 2.0 A.
点开看答案Show answer
Vs = Vp × Ns/Np = 230 × 100/400 = 57.5 V Ip = VsIs / Vp = 57.5 × 2.0 / 230 = 0.50 A

1 光电效应：经典物理的翻车现场The photoelectric effect: where classical physics fails Photoelectric光电效应
学完你会：说出三个经典波动理论没法解释的实验事实。By the end: you can list three observations the classical wave picture cannot explain.
说人话：用光照射金属，会有电子被打出来，这就是光电效应。麻烦在于实验结果和"光的波动说"正面冲突三件事：一、低于某个频率（截止频率）的光，再亮也打不出一个电子——按波动说，光够亮就攒够能量，应该能出来；二、只要频率超一点点，哪怕是极微弱的光，电子立刻就出来，不需要"攒"的时间；三、更强的光只会多打出一些电子，而不是让每个电子更有劲。这三条把整个 19 世纪的物理学逼到了墙角。 In plain words: shine light on a metal and electrons can be knocked out — the photoelectric effect. The trouble is that three findings flatly contradict the wave picture. First: below a certain frequency (the threshold) no electrons emerge, however bright the light — although if light were a wave, brightness should eventually supply enough energy. Second: a shade above the threshold, electrons leave instantly, even with the feeblest light — no accumulating time needed. Third: brighter light simply releases more electrons, not faster ones. Between them, these three findings backed nineteenth-century physics into a corner.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • 截止频率：低于它，多亮都白搭。A threshold frequency exists: below it, brightness is irrelevant.
  • 高于截止频率 → 瞬时发射，无延迟。Above threshold, emission is instant, with no time delay.
  • 光强决定电子个数（光电流），频率决定每个电子的动能。Intensity decides how many electrons (photocurrent); frequency decides the kinetic energy of each.
  • 这套解释要求把光想成一份份的能量包——这就是光子。Explaining all this needs light delivered as discrete packets — photons.

例题Example

某金属的截止频率是 5.0×10¹⁴ Hz。用 4.0×10¹⁴ Hz 的强红光照射会怎样？换成 7.0×10¹⁴ Hz 的弱紫光呢？A metal has threshold frequency 5.0×10¹⁴ Hz. What happens with intense red light at 4.0×10¹⁴ Hz? With faint violet light at 7.0×10¹⁴ Hz?
红光 4.0 < 5.0 → 低于截止频率：一个电子也打不出来，再强也不行。Red at 4.0 < 5.0 → below threshold: no electrons at all, however strong the light. 紫光 7.0 > 5.0 → 立刻有电子逸出；只是光弱，出来的数量少。Violet at 7.0 > 5.0 → electrons emerge immediately; merely fewer of them because the light is faint.

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：把"变亮"翻译成"单位时间到达的光子数变多"。Hint: translate "brighter" as more photons arriving per second.

用高于截止频率的光照射时，把光源调亮一倍，逸出电子的最大动能如何变化？光电流如何变化？With light above the threshold, the source intensity is doubled. How do the maximum electron energy and the photocurrent change?
点开看答案Show answer
最大动能不变（频率没变，每个光子的能量没变）。Maximum kinetic energy is unchanged (same frequency, same energy per photon). 光电流变大（光子多了，打出的电子多了）。Photocurrent increases (more photons, more electrons released).
— 第 1 站完成 —— Stop 1 done —
2 光子 E = hf 与逸出功Photons: E = hf and the work function Photons光子
学完你会：由频率或波长算光子能量，并理解逸出功与截止频率的关系。By the end: you can find photon energy from frequency or wavelength, and link work function to threshold frequency.
说人话：爱因斯坦的解法极其干脆：光就是以 E = hf 为一份的能量包（光子），一个光子把能量整个交给一个电子。电子想逃出金属要先缴一笔过路费，叫逸出功 Φ；光的频率越高，每份能量越大，交完过路费剩下的就越多。截止频率正好是"刚刚够交费"的那一档：hf₀ = Φ。这解释了为什么只看颜色（=频率）而不看亮度（=光子数目）。 In plain words: Einstein's fix was brutally simple: light comes as packets (photons) of energy E = hf, and one photon hands its energy over to one electron in full. To escape the metal, an electron must first pay an exit fee — the work function Φ. Higher frequency means bigger packets, so more is left over after paying the fee. The threshold frequency is precisely the "just enough to pay" case: hf₀ = Φ. That is why colour (frequency) rules and brightness (number of photons) does not.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • E = hf = hc/λ；h = 6.63×10⁻³⁴ J·s。E = hf = hc/λ; h = 6.63×10⁻³⁴ J·s.
  • 频率越高（波长越短）→ 单光子能量越大。Higher frequency (shorter wavelength) → more energy per photon.
  • 逸出功 Φ 是金属的属性；hf₀ = Φ。The work function Φ is a property of the metal; hf₀ = Φ.
  • 光强 = 每秒的光子数；它不改变每个光子的能量。Intensity = photons per second; it does not change the energy per photon.

例题Example

波长 500 nm 的绿光光子能量是多少（用 J 和 eV 各表示一次）？What is the energy of a 500 nm green-light photon, in joules and in eV?
E = hc/λ = (6.63×10⁻³⁴ × 3.0×10⁸) / 5.0×10⁻⁷ = 1.989×10⁻²⁵ / 5.0×10⁻⁷ E = 3.98×10⁻¹⁹ J 换 eV：除 1.6×10⁻¹⁹ → 2.49 eVIn eV: divide by 1.6×10⁻¹⁹ → 2.49 eV

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：截止频率处 hf₀ = Φ，先由 f₀ 求 Φ（单位 J 或 eV）。Hint: at threshold hf₀ = Φ, so find Φ from f₀ first.

某金属逸出功为 2.0 eV。求截止频率（1 eV = 1.6×10⁻¹⁹ J）。A metal has work function 2.0 eV. Find its threshold frequency (1 eV = 1.6×10⁻¹⁹ J).
点开看答案Show answer
Φ = 2.0 × 1.6×10⁻¹⁹ = 3.2×10⁻¹⁹ J f₀ = Φ/h = 3.2×10⁻¹⁹ / 6.63×10⁻³⁴ = 4.8×10¹⁴ Hz
— 第 2 站完成 —— Stop 2 done —
3 爱因斯坦光电方程与截止电压Einstein's equation and stopping voltage Equation光电方程
学完你会：由光子能量减去逸出功得到最大动能，并会用截止电压 eVs 表示。By the end: subtract the work function from the photon energy, and quote the result via the stopping voltage.
说人话：完整的账本是这样的：进来的钱是 hf，先付掉过路费 Φ，剩下的变成电子的动能——½mv最大² = hf − Φ。实验上怎么量"最大动能"？加一个反向电压，把最快的电子都挡回去，那个刚好够挡住的电压叫截止电压 Vs，满足 eVs = ½mv最大²。把这条式子画成图，横轴截距是截止频率，斜率是普朗克常数 h，纵轴截距是负的逸出功——三种不同金属只是三条平行线。 In plain words: the full bookkeeping is: money in is hf, pay the exit fee Φ first, and whatever remains becomes kinetic energy — ½mv max² = hf − Φ. How do you measure that maximum in the lab? Apply a reverse voltage just strong enough to stop even the fastest electrons: the stopping voltage Vs, where eVs = ½mv max². Plot it and the horizontal intercept gives the threshold frequency, the gradient gives Planck's constant h, and the vertical intercept minus the work function. Three different metals are simply three parallel lines.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • hf = Φ + ½mv最大²（能量守恒的写法）。hf = Φ + ½mv max² — conservation of energy written out.
  • 截止电压：eVs = ½mv最大²，与光强无关。Stopping voltage: eVs = ½mv max², independent of intensity.
  • K-f 图：斜率 = h，横截距 = f₀，纵截距 = −Φ。K–f graph: gradient = h, x-intercept = f₀, y-intercept = −Φ.
  • 同样一道光照在不同金属上，截止电压不同（因为 Φ 不同）。The same light gives different stopping voltages for different metals (different Φ).

例题Example

逸出功 2.0 eV 的金属，受波长 300 nm 的紫外光照射。求逸出电子的最大动能（eV）与截止电压。A metal with work function 2.0 eV is illuminated by 300 nm ultraviolet light. Find the maximum kinetic energy (in eV) and the stopping voltage.
E = hc/λ = (6.63×10⁻³⁴ × 3.0×10⁸) / 3.0×10⁻⁷ = 6.63×10⁻¹⁹ J = 4.14 eV K最大 = hf − Φ = 4.14 − 2.0 = 2.14 eV eVs = 2.14 eV → Vs = 2.14 V（电子伏特的定义让这一步几乎白送）eVs = 2.14 eV → Vs = 2.14 V — the electronvolt makes this step free

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：截止电压 1.5 V 意味着最大动能就是 1.5 eV。Hint: a stopping voltage of 1.5 V means the top kinetic energy is 1.5 eV.

某金属截止电压为 1.5 V 时，入射光子能量 3.8 eV。求逸出功（eV）。With a stopping voltage of 1.5 V, the incident photons carry 3.8 eV. Find the work function in eV.
点开看答案Show answer
Φ = hf − K最大 = 3.8 − 1.5 = 2.3 eV
— 第 3 站完成 —— Stop 3 done —
4 能级与光谱Energy levels and spectra Energy levels能级
学完你会：由能级差求光子频率，并解释为什么原子光谱是一条条线。By the end: you can turn an energy-level difference into a photon frequency, and say why spectra are lines rather than rainbows.
说人话：原子里的电子不能随便停在任意高度，只能待在固定的台阶（能级）上。从高能级掉到低能级，多余的能量不多不少正好放出一个光子：hf = E₂ − E₁。因为台阶是离散的，能放出的能量也只有几种，于是光谱不是连续的彩虹，而是一根根分立的亮线——每种元素有自己的"台阶高度"，所以谱线就成了元素的指纹（氦元素就是这样先在太阳光谱里被发现的）。反过来，用连续光照射气体，某些波长被吸收，就在连续谱上留下黑线。 In plain words: electrons in an atom cannot sit at just any height; only certain fixed steps (energy levels) are allowed. Drop from a higher level to a lower one and the surplus energy leaves as exactly one photon: hf = E₂ − E₁. Because the steps are discrete, only certain energies can be emitted, so the spectrum is not a continuous rainbow but a set of separate bright lines. Every element has its own step heights, making these lines a fingerprint — helium was discovered in the Sun's spectrum before anyone found it on Earth. Run it backwards: shine continuous light through a gas and certain wavelengths are absorbed, leaving dark lines in an otherwise continuous spectrum.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • 跃迁：hf = E₂ − E₁（往下放光子，往上吸光子）。Transitions: hf = E₂ − E₁ (emitted downwards, absorbed upwards).
  • 能级圖上最低处（基态）常取负值，电离就是升到 0。The ground state is drawn with negative energy; reaching 0 means ionisation.
  • 发光（发射）谱是亮线；吸收谱是连续彩虹上的暗线。Emission spectra are bright lines; absorption spectra are dark lines on a continuous background.
  • 不同跃迁可以组合出多条谱线，每对能级对应一条。Many transitions are possible; each pair of levels gives its own line.

例题Example

某原子两能级相差 3.0 eV。求放出光子的频率与波长。Two levels in an atom differ by 3.0 eV. Find the frequency and wavelength of the emitted photon.
E = 3.0 × 1.6×10⁻¹⁹ = 4.8×10⁻¹⁹ J f = E/h = 4.8×10⁻¹⁹ / 6.63×10⁻³⁴ = 7.2×10¹⁴ Hz λ = c/f = 3.0×10⁸ / 7.2×10¹⁴ = 4.2×10⁻⁷ m = 420 nm （紫光）(violet light)

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：先把 eV 换成 J，再算频率。Hint: convert eV to joules, then find the frequency.

某原子基态为 −13.6 eV，第一激发态为 −3.4 eV。求两者之间跃迁产生的光子频率。An atom has ground state −13.6 eV and first excited state −3.4 eV. Find the frequency of the photon for a transition between them.
点开看答案Show answer
ΔE = −3.4 − (−13.6) = 10.2 eV = 10.2 × 1.6×10⁻¹⁹ = 1.63×10⁻¹⁸ J f = ΔE/h = 1.63×10⁻¹⁸ / 6.63×10⁻³⁴ = 2.46×10¹⁵ Hz
— 第 4 站完成 —— Stop 4 done —
5 波粒二象性与德布罗意波长Wave–particle duality and de Broglie wavelength de Broglie德布罗意
学完你会：由动量求德布罗意波长，并明白为什么电子会衍射而人不会。By the end: you can find a de Broglie wavelength from momentum, and see why electrons diffract while people do not.
说人话：前四站确立了"光有时像粒子"。德布罗意反过来说：粒子有时也像波——任何动量为 p 的东西都有一条波长 λ = h/p。为什么不觉得自己的身体会衍射？因为 h 太小而你的质量太大：一个 60 kg、以 1 m/s 走路的人，波长只有 10⁻³⁵ 米量级，比什么都小得多。而电子质量极小，波长和 X 射线的数量级相当，所以它真的会在晶体上衍射出漂亮的圆环——电子显微镜就靠这个。 In plain words: the previous stops established that light sometimes behaves like a particle. de Broglie turned it around: particles sometimes behave like waves — anything with momentum p has a wavelength λ = h/p. Why don't you diffract through doorways? Because h is tiny and your mass is huge: a 60 kg person strolling at 1 m/s has a wavelength around 10⁻³⁵ m. An electron, though, is so light that its wavelength rivals that of X-rays, so it genuinely diffracts off crystals into elegant rings — the basis of the electron microscope.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • λ = h/p = h/(mv)（非相对论情形）。λ = h/p = h/(mv) in non-relativistic cases.
  • 动量越大（速度越快、质量越大）→ 波长越短。More momentum → shorter wavelength.
  • 电子经加速电压 V 后：½mv² = eV ⇒ λ = h/√(2meV)。After an accelerating voltage V: ½mv² = eV, so λ = h/√(2meV).
  • 这是电子衍射和电子显微镜成立的前提。This underpins electron diffraction and the electron microscope.

例题Example

电子以 2.0×10⁷ m/s 运动（m = 9.1×10⁻³¹ kg）。求它的德布罗意波长。An electron moves at 2.0×10⁷ m/s (m = 9.1×10⁻³¹ kg). Find its de Broglie wavelength.
p = mv = 9.1×10⁻³¹ × 2.0×10⁷ = 1.82×10⁻²³ kg·m/s λ = h/p = 6.63×10⁻³⁴ / 1.82×10⁻²³ = 3.6×10⁻¹¹ m 约 0.036 nm，正好和原子间距同量级——所以它能被晶体衍射。About 0.036 nm, right on the scale of atomic spacings — which is exactly why crystals diffract it.

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：先用 eV 求动能，再由 ½mv² 解出 v。Hint: get the kinetic energy in joules from eV, then solve ½mv² for v.

电子经 100 V 加速后，求它的德布罗意波长（e = 1.6×10⁻¹⁹ C，m = 9.1×10⁻³¹ kg）。An electron is accelerated through 100 V. Find its de Broglie wavelength (e = 1.6×10⁻¹⁹ C, m = 9.1×10⁻³¹ kg).
点开看答案Show answer
动能 eV = 100 eV = 1.6×10⁻¹⁷ JKE = eV = 100 eV = 1.6×10⁻¹⁷ J v = √(2K/m) = √(3.2×10⁻¹⁷ / 9.1×10⁻³¹) = √(3.52×10¹³) = 5.93×10⁶ m/s λ = h/(mv) = 6.63×10⁻³⁴ / (9.1×10⁻³¹ × 5.93×10⁶) = 1.23×10⁻¹⁰ m
— 第 5 站完成 —— Stop 5 done —
6 X 射线的产生Producing X-rays X-raysX 射线
学完你会：区分轫致辐射（连续谱）与特征 X 射线（线状），并算最短波长。By the end: you can separate bremsstrahlung from characteristic X-rays, and find the minimum wavelength.
说人话：X 射线管做的事很朴素：把电子加速到很高速度，再让它猛地撞停在重金属靶上。撞停的过程叫轫致辐射（刹车辐射），给出连续的 X 射线谱：因为电子每次损失的能量都不一样。极端情况下电子一次就把全部动能变成单个光子，这就给谱定了最短波长：eV = hc/λ最小。谱上还叠着几根尖峰，那是电子把靶原子的内层电子撞飞后，外层电子补位时放出的固定能量——叫特征 X 射线，和上个单元的原子谱完全同理。 In plain words: an X-ray tube does something quite plain: accelerate electrons to high speed and slam them into a heavy metal target. The sudden stop is bremsstrahlung — braking radiation — and produces a continuous spectrum, because electrons lose varying amounts of energy each time. In the extreme case all the kinetic energy becomes a single photon, setting the shortest possible wavelength: eV = hc/λ min. Superposed on that are a few sharp spikes: electrons knocked out of the target atoms' inner shells, followed by outer electrons dropping in and releasing fixed energies — characteristic X-rays, exactly the same physics as last stop's atomic spectra.
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • 轫致辐射：减速 → 连续谱，有最短波长。Bremsstrahlung: deceleration → continuous spectrum with a minimum wavelength.
  • 最短波长：eV = hc/λ最小（全部动能给一个光子）。Minimum wavelength: eV = hc/λ min (all the KE into one photon).
  • 特征 X 射线：线状，取决于靶材料（原子能级）。Characteristic X-rays: discrete lines determined by the target's atomic levels.
  • 提高管电压 → 光子能量更高、最短波长更短（穿透力更强）。Raising the tube voltage → higher-energy photons and shorter minimum wavelength (more penetrating).
  • 绝大部分输入能量变成了热，所以靶要强制冷却。Most input energy becomes heat, which is why targets need active cooling.

例题Example

X 射线管电压为 30 kV。求所产生光子的最大能量（eV）与最短波长。An X-ray tube runs at 30 kV. Find the maximum photon energy in eV and the shortest wavelength.
一个电子获得的动能 = 30 kV 加速 = 30 keV（这就是最大光子能量）Each electron gains KE = 30 keV from the 30 kV acceleration — this is the maximum photon energy 换成焦耳：30 000 × 1.6×10⁻¹⁹ = 4.8×10⁻¹⁵ JIn joules: 30 000 × 1.6×10⁻¹⁹ = 4.8×10⁻¹⁵ J λ最小 = hc/E = (6.63×10⁻³⁴ × 3.0×10⁸) / 4.8×10⁻¹⁵ = 4.1×10⁻¹¹ m

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：λ最小 ∝ 1/V，电压翻倍则最短波长减半。Hint: λ min ∝ 1/V, so doubling the voltage halves it.

上题的管子把电压提到 60 kV，最短波长变为多少？The same tube is raised to 60 kV. What is the new minimum wavelength?
点开看答案Show answer
λ最小 = 4.1×10⁻¹¹ × (30/60) = 2.1×10⁻¹¹ m
— 第 6 站完成 —— Stop 6 done —
7 电子衍射与 eV 换算Electron diffraction and eV conversions Diffraction电子衍射
学完你会：熟练使用 eV 单位，并解释电子衍射实验说明了什么。By the end: you are fluent in eV, and can say what electron diffraction proves.
说人话：把电子束射向薄的多晶石墨，屏上出现的不是杂乱的点，而是一圈圈同心圆环——那是只有波才会画的图案，说明电子确实在像一个波那样衍射。加速电压越大，电子动量越大、波长越短，圆环就缩得越小。这一站也就到了 A-Level 物理的终点：微观世界的规则不是"要么粒子要么波"，而是"看你怎么问它"。 In plain words: fire electrons at a thin sheet of polycrystalline graphite and the screen shows not scattered dots but concentric rings — a pattern only a wave can draw, proving that electrons really do diffract. Raising the accelerating voltage gives more momentum, a shorter wavelength, and therefore smaller rings. And with that we reach the end of A-Level physics: the microscopic world is ruled not by "either particle or wave" but by "it depends what question you ask".
记住这几点：Remember these:
  • 1 eV = 1.6×10⁻¹⁹ J；这是必须肌肉记忆的换算。1 eV = 1.6×10⁻¹⁹ J — memorise this conversion cold.
  • 经过 V 伏特加速的电子：K = eV，λ = h/√(2meV)。Accelerated through V volts: K = eV and λ = h/√(2meV).
  • 衍射环的大小 ∝ λ，所以加快电压环变小。Ring size ∝ λ, so more voltage means smaller rings.
  • 这是波粒二象性的直接证据：粒子表现为波。Direct evidence for wave–particle duality: particles behaving as waves.

例题Example

把电子的加速电压提高到原来的 4 倍，德布罗意波长与衍射环半径各如何变化？The accelerating voltage is increased by a factor of 4. How do the de Broglie wavelength and the diffraction ring radius change?
λ = h/√(2meV) ∝ 1/√V → 电压 ×4 则 λ 减半λ = h/√(2meV) ∝ 1/√V, so ×4 voltage halves λ 环半径 ∝ λ → 也减半。Ring radius ∝ λ, so it too halves.

✏️ 你来试一题Your turn

提示：α 粒子带两个电子电荷，走过同样的电压要乘 2。Hint: an alpha particle carries two elementary charges, so double it for the same voltage.

一个质子经 1000 V 加速，获得的动能是多少 eV 与多少焦耳？A proton is accelerated through 1000 V. What kinetic energy does it gain, in eV and in joules?
点开看答案Show answer
K = 1000 eV = 1.0×10³ × 1.6×10⁻¹⁹ = 1.6×10⁻¹⁶ J
— 第 7 站完成 —— Stop 7 done —
— 单元 12 完成 · 也意味着 12 个单元、84 站全部走完：从运动学到光量子，你已经有了一整套看世界的工具 —— Unit 12 done · and with it all 12 units, 84 stops: from kinematics to light quanta, you now have a full toolkit for looking at the world —

单元 1 · 力学公式Unit 1 · Mechanics formulas

公式Formula含义与用法Meaning and use
v = u + at末速度 = 初速度 + 加速度 × 时间（匀加速）final velocity from initial velocity, acceleration and time
s = ut + ½at²位移公式，最常用displacement — the most-used of the four
v² = u² + 2as不含时间的那条，题目没给 t 就用它the one without time — use it when t is not given
s = ½(u + v)t平均速度 × 时间mean velocity × time
F = ma牛顿第二定律；F 是合力，不是某一个力Newton's second law; F is the resultant force
W = mg重量 = 质量 × 重力加速度weight = mass × g
W = F s cos θ功 = 力 × 沿力方向的位移work = force × displacement along the force
Eₖ = ½mv²动能kinetic energy
ΔEₚ = mgh重力势能变化，h 是竖直高度差change in G.P.E.; h is the vertical height
P = W / t功率 = 做功 ÷ 时间power = work done ÷ time
P = F v恒定速度下的功率（牵引力 × 速度）power at constant speed (driving force × velocity)
p = m v动量，单位 kg·m/s（或 N·s）momentum, in kg m s⁻¹ (or N s)
F t = Δp冲量 = 动量变化量impulse equals change in momentum
m₁u₁ + m₂u₂ = m₁v₁ + m₂v₂动量守恒（无外力时，碰撞前后总动量相等）conservation of momentum in a collision
ρ = m / V密度density
p = F / A压强 = 压力 ÷ 受力面积pressure = force ÷ area
p = ρ g h液体压强（h 为液面下深度）pressure in a liquid at depth h
moment = F × d力矩 = 力 × 到转轴的垂直距离moment = force × perpendicular distance to the pivot
Σ顺时针 = Σ逆时针Σclockwise = Σanticlockwise力矩原理（平衡条件）principle of moments (equilibrium)
η = 有用输出useful output / 总输入total input × 100%效率，永远小于 100%efficiency, always less than 100%

比例极限limit of proportionality弹性极限elastic limit
弹性形变elastic deformation塑性形变plastic deformation
弹性势能 / 应变能elastic potential / strain energy卸载unloading
应力stress应变strain
杨氏模量Young modulus横截面积cross-sectional area
极限抗拉强度ultimate tensile strength屈服点yield point

单元 2 · 材料公式Unit 2 · Materials formulas

公式Formula含义与用法Meaning and use
F = k x胡克定律；x 是伸长量，不是总长度Hooke's law; x is the extension, not the total length
E = ½ F x = ½ k x²弹性势能 = F–x 图线下的三角形面积strain energy = area of the triangle under the F–x graph
stress = F / A应力，单位 Pa（N/m²）stress, in pascals (N m⁻²)
strain = x / L应变，没有单位（两个长度相除）strain — no unit, it is a ratio
E = stress / strain = F L / (A x)杨氏模量，材料本身的性质，与形状无关Young modulus — a property of the material, not the sample
k（串联）(series): 1/k = 1/k₁ + 1/k₂弹簧串联，越串越软springs in series: the combination is less stiff
k（并联）(parallel): k = k₁ + k₂弹簧并联，越并越硬springs in parallel: the combination is stiffer

同相in phase反相in antiphase / out of phase

单元 3 · 波公式Unit 3 · Waves formulas

公式Formula含义与用法Meaning and use
v = f λ波速 = 频率 × 波长，整个 A-Level 最通用的公式之一wave speed = frequency × wavelength — one of the most useful in A-Level
f = 1 / T频率与周期互为倒数，单位 Hzfrequency is the reciprocal of period, in hertz
相长：constructive: path difference = nλ路径差为整数倍波长 → 亮纹 / 变强whole number of wavelengths → bright fringe
相消：destructive: path difference = (n + ½)λ路径差为半波长的奇数倍 → 暗纹 / 抵消odd number of half-wavelengths → dark fringe
λ = 2 L / n弦上第 n 谐波：L 为弦长，n = 1 是基频nth harmonic on a string of length L; n = 1 is the fundamental
I = P / A强度 = 功率 ÷ 面积，单位 W/m²intensity = power ÷ area, in W m⁻²
I ∝ A² （振幅）(amplitude)强度正比于振幅的平方（不是正比于振幅）intensity goes as the square of the amplitude
I ∝ 1 / r²点光源的平方反比定律inverse square law for a point source
I = I₀ cos²θ偏振片旋转 θ 后的强度（马吕斯定律，A-Level 只作了解）intensity after a polariser rotated by θ
相邻波节间距node spacing = λ / 2驻波上相邻两个波节（或波腹）相距半个波长adjacent nodes (or antinodes) are half a wavelength apart

单元 4 · 电学公式Unit 4 · Electricity formulas

公式Formula含义与用法Meaning and use
I = Q / t电流 = 电荷 ÷ 时间；1 A = 1 C/scurrent = charge ÷ time; 1 A = 1 C s⁻¹
Q = n e总电荷 = 电子个数 × 元电荷（e = 1.60 × 10⁻¹⁹ C）total charge = number of electrons × e (1.60 × 10⁻¹⁹ C)
V = W / Q电势差 = 能量 ÷ 电荷；1 V = 1 J/Cp.d. = energy ÷ charge; 1 V = 1 J C⁻¹
V = I R欧姆定律；只对欧姆导体成立Ohm's law — only for ohmic conductors
P = V I = I²R = V² / R电功率，三个写法按已知条件挑electrical power — pick whichever form matches your data
W = V I t = I² R t电能 / 电功electrical energy
R = ρ L / A电阻 = 电阻率 × 长度 ÷ 横截面积；A 要换成 m²resistance from resistivity; convert A to m²
R（串联）(series) = R₁ + R₂ + …串联电阻直接相加，总电阻大于任何一个series: just add; the total exceeds every resistor
1/R（并联）(parallel) = 1/R₁ + 1/R₂ + …并联总电阻小于最小的那一支路parallel: the total is less than the smallest branch
E = I (R + r)全电路欧姆定律：电动势分摊在内阻与外电阻上whole-circuit equation: e.m.f. shared between r and R
V = E − I r端电压 = 电动势 − 电流 × 内阻；画 V–I 图时截距 = E，梯度 = −rterminal p.d.; on a V–I graph the intercept is E and the gradient is −r
V₁ = V × R₁ / (R₁ + R₂)分压器：电阻越大分到的电压越多potential divider: the larger resistor takes the larger share
I = n A v q电流的微观式（载流子数密度 × 面积 × 漂移速度 × 电荷）microscopic current equation
1 kWh = 3.6 × 10⁶ J电费单单位换算the electricity-bill unit
G = 1 / R电导，单位是西门子 Sconductance, in siemens

单元 5 · 核物理公式Unit 5 · Nuclear formulas

公式Formula含义与用法Meaning and use
A = Z + N核子数 = 质子数 + 中子数nucleon number = protons + neutrons
E = Δm c²质量亏损换成能量；Δm 必须用 kgmass defect into energy; Δm must be in kg
1 u = 1.66 × 10⁻²⁷ kg原子质量单位the atomic mass unit
1 u ↔ 931.5 MeV最省事的一条：Δm（u）直接乘 931.5 得 MeVthe short cut: Δm in u × 931.5 gives MeV
1 eV = 1.60 × 10⁻¹⁹ J电子伏特 → 焦耳；1 MeV = 1.60 × 10⁻¹³ Jelectronvolt → joule; 1 MeV = 1.60 × 10⁻¹³ J
N = N₀ e⁻λt未衰变核数随时间衰减；活度、计数率同样适用number of undecayed nuclei; also activity and count rate
λ = ln 2 / t½ ≈ 0.693 / t½衰变常数与半衰期的关系linking decay constant and half-life
A（活度）(activity) = λ N活度，单位 Bq（1 Bq = 1 s⁻¹）activity in becquerel (1 Bq = 1 s⁻¹)
剩余比例 = (½)ⁿ，n = t / t½fraction left = (½)ⁿ, n = t / t½快速法：数经历了几个半衰期the quick way: count the half-lives
α: A − 4, Z − 2α 衰变：放出一个氦核 ⁴₂Heα decay: a ⁴₂He nucleus is emitted
β⁻: A 不变unchanged, Z + 1β⁻ 衰变：核内 n → p + e⁻ + ν̄β⁻ decay: n → p + e⁻ + ν̄ in the nucleus
γ: A, Z 都不变unchangedγ 只释放能量，不改变核的组成γ releases energy without changing the nucleus
结合能 = Δm c²；每核子 = 结合能 / AB.E. = Δm c²; per nucleon = B.E. / A越大越稳定，铁-56 附近最高（≈ 8.8 MeV）bigger means more stable; the peak is near iron-56 (≈ 8.8 MeV)

单元 6 · 实验技能公式与规则Unit 6 · Practical rules

规则 / 公式Rule / formula含义与用法Meaning and use
%Δx = (Δx / x) × 100%百分比不确定度percentage uncertainty
加减：add/subtract: Δx = Δa + Δb绝对不确定度直接相加add the absolute uncertainties
乘除：×/÷: %Δy = %Δa + %Δb百分比不确定度相加add the percentage uncertainties
次方：power: %Δy = n × %Δa （y = aⁿ）(for y = aⁿ)指数乘到百分比上multiply the percentage by the index
Δx = (x最大max − x最小min) / 2多次读数时取一半极差；若小于仪器精度就用仪器精度half the range for repeats; use the instrument precision if that is larger
结果写法 x ± Δxwrite x ± Δx两者的小数位数要对齐，如 1.23 ± 0.04 mmatch the decimal places, e.g. 1.23 ± 0.04 m
答案位数significant figures = 数据中最少的the fewest given一般 2～3 位，中间过程多留一位usually 2 or 3, keeping an extra figure part-way
直线化：y = m x + clinearise: y = mx + c把公式摆成直线，梯度 m 与截距 c 就会给出待求量rearrange to a straight line; m and c give the quantity you want
gradient = Δy / Δx （取线上相距远的两点）(two far-apart points on the line)不要用原始数据点求梯度，这是最常见的扣分点never take the gradient from raw data points — a classic lost mark

万有引力gravitational force引力场强度gravitational field strength

单元 7 · 圆运动与引力公式Unit 7 · Circular motion & gravitation formulas

公式Formula含义与用法Meaning and use
θ = s / r （θ 单位 rad）(θ in rad)弧度定义：弧长除以半径；一圈 = 2π radradian = arc length ÷ radius; one turn = 2π rad
v = r ω线速度与角速度的桥梁，最常用links linear and angular speed — used constantly
ω = 2π / T = 2π f转一圈用 T 秒，转过的角永远是 2πone turn takes T seconds and covers 2π radians
a = v² / r = r ω²向心加速度，方向始终指向圆心centripetal acceleration, always towards the centre
F = m v² / r = m r ω²向心力不是新力，是合力扮演的角色centripetal force is not a new force — it is the role played by the resultant
圆锥摆：conical pendulum: tan θ = v² / (r g)摆线与竖直方向的夹角 θ，r 是水平圆周半径θ is the angle to the vertical; r is the radius of the horizontal circle
倾斜路面：banked track: tan θ = v² / (r g)这个速度下不打滑也不需要摩擦力at this speed no friction is needed at all
F = G M m / r²牛顿万有引力定律，r 是中心到中心的距离Newton's law of gravitation; r is centre to centre
g = G M / r²引力场强度，与高度有关，与卫星质量无关field strength depends on height, not on the satellite's mass
轨道：orbit: G M m / r² = m v² / r引力当向心力，这一句是所有卫星题的起点gravity provides the centripetal force — the start of every satellite question
v = √(G M / r)轨道越高，速度越慢the higher the orbit, the slower the speed
T² = 4π² r³ / (G M) ，即 T² ∝ r³, i.e. T² ∝ r³开普勒第三定律；同步卫星 T = 24 hKepler's third law; a geostationary satellite has T = 24 h
G = 6.67 × 10⁻¹¹ N m² kg⁻²引力常数，通常题目会给出the gravitational constant, usually given in the question

位移displacement回复力restoring force
周期period频率frequency
相位phase相位差phase difference
同相in phase反相out of phase / antiphase
阻尼damping临界阻尼critical damping
受迫振动forced oscillation驱动力driving force
固有频率natural frequency共振resonance

劲度系数spring constant摆长length of pendulum

单元 8 · 简谐振动公式Unit 8 · Oscillations formulas

公式Formula含义与用法Meaning and use
a = − ω² x简谐运动的定义式：负号表示加速度总指向平衡位置the defining equation; the minus sign points back to equilibrium
x = x₀ sin(ω t) 或 x₀ cos(ω t)or x₀ cos(ω t)x₀ 是振幅；从平衡位置计时用 sin，从端点计时用 cosx₀ is the amplitude; use sin from the centre, cos from an end
ω = 2π / T = 2π f角频率，与圆运动里的 ω 是同一个东西angular frequency — the same ω as in circular motion
v = ± ω √(x₀² − x²)某一位移处的速度；x = 0 处最大，x = ±x₀ 处为零speed at displacement x; maximum at x = 0, zero at the ends
v最大max = ω x₀平衡位置处的最大速度the maximum speed, at the equilibrium position
a最大max = ω² x₀端点处的最大加速度the maximum acceleration, at the ends
T = 2π √(m / k)弹簧振子：质量越大越慢，弹簧越硬越快mass–spring: heavier means slower, stiffer means faster
T = 2π √(L / g)单摆：与摆球质量无关，只与摆长有关pendulum: independent of the bob's mass, depends only on length
E总total = ½ m ω² x₀² （不变）(constant)理想无阻尼时总能量守恒，在动能与势能间来回换constant when undamped, swapping between kinetic and potential
E弹p.e. = ½ k x²弹簧的弹性势能elastic potential energy stored in the spring
阻尼：振幅逐渐减小，周期基本不变damping: amplitude falls, period stays almost the same考试高频判断句a favourite exam point
共振：resonance: f驱动driving = f₀驱动力频率等于固有频率时振幅最大；阻尼越小峰越尖amplitude peaks when driving frequency = natural frequency; less damping gives a sharper peak

中文中文English中文中文English
温度temperature热力学温度thermodynamic temperature
内能internal energy热量heat / thermal energy transferred
比热容specific heat capacity比潜热specific latent heat
熔解fusion / melting汽化vaporisation

热力学温标thermodynamic scale热平衡thermal equilibrium

单元 9 · 热物理公式Unit 9 · Thermal physics formulas

公式Formula含义与用法Meaning and use
T (K) = θ (°C) + 273摄氏转开尔文；所有气体公式必须用 K°C to K; every gas formula needs kelvin
Q = m c Δθ升温吸热；Δθ 用 ℃ 或 K 都可以（因为是差值）heating; Δθ may be in °C or K since it is a difference
Q = m L相变吸热，温度不变：L_f 熔解，L_v 汽化change of state at constant temperature; L_f fusion, L_v vaporisation
p V = n R T理想气体状态方程，n 是摩尔数，T 必须是 Kideal gas equation; n in mol, T in K
p V = N k T分子数版本，N 是分子个数the molecular version, N being the number of molecules
p V = ⅓ N m ⟨c²⟩分子动理论基本式，m 是单个分子质量the kinetic-model equation; m is the mass of one molecule
c方均根r.m.s. = √⟨c²⟩方均根速率，不是平均速率root-mean-square speed — not the mean speed
½ m ⟨c²⟩ = (3/2) k T温度决定平均动能，这是温度的微观含义temperature sets the mean kinetic energy — its microscopic meaning
k = R / N_A = 1.38 × 10⁻²³ J/K玻尔兹曼常数 = 气体常数 ÷ 阿伏伽德罗常数Boltzmann constant = gas constant ÷ Avogadro constant
R = 8.31 J/(mol·K)摩尔气体常数the molar gas constant
N_A = 6.02 × 10²³ mol⁻¹阿伏伽德罗常数Avogadro constant
ΔU = Q + W热力学第一定律：W 是外界对气体做的功（压缩为正）first law; W is work done on the gas, so compression is positive
W = p ΔV （恒压时）(at constant pressure)气体被压缩 ΔV < 0，外界对气体做功compression gives ΔV < 0, meaning work is done on the gas
理想气体内能只由温度决定for an ideal gas, internal energy depends only on temperature等温过程 ΔU = 0，于是 Q = −Wisothermal means ΔU = 0, so Q = −W

单元 10 · 电场与电容术语Unit 10 · Electric fields & capacitance terms

中文中文English中文中文English
点电荷point charge电荷量charge
库仑定律Coulomb's law电场强度electric field strength
电场线electric field line匀强电场uniform electric field
电势electric potential电势差 / 电压potential difference
等势面equipotential surface零点zero of potential
电容capacitance电容器capacitor
极板plate电介质dielectric
真空介电常数permittivity of free space相对介电常数relative permittivity
法拉farad充电 / 放电charging / discharging
储能energy stored时间常数time constant
验电器electroscope库仑coulomb
无穷远处为零zero at infinity密立根油滴实验Millikan's oil-drop experiment

单元 10 · 电场与电容公式Unit 10 · Electric fields & capacitance formulas

公式Formula含义与用法Meaning and use
F = Q₁ Q₂ / (4π ε₀ r²)库仑定律，r 两点电荷间的距离Coulomb's law; r is the separation of the charges
E = F / q电场强度定义：单位正电荷受力，单位 N/Cfield strength = force per unit positive charge, in N/C
E = Q / (4π ε₀ r²)点电荷的电场，与距离平方成反比field of a point charge, inverse-square
E = V / d只适用于匀强电场，单位也常写成 V/muniform fields only; V/m is the same unit
E = − ΔV / Δx （梯度形式）(gradient form)电场方向是电势降低最快的方向the field points down the steepest potential slope
V = Q / (4π ε₀ r)点电荷的电势，取无穷远为零；注意不是平方potential of a point charge (not squared!) with zero at infinity
W = q ΔV电荷在两点间移动时电场做的功work done moving a charge between two points
沿等势面移动：W = 0moving along an equipotential: W = 0同一等势面上电势差为零，不做功no potential difference means no work done
C = Q / V电容定义，单位 F（1 F = 1 C/V）definition of capacitance in farads
C = ε₀ A / d ；; C = ε A / d平行板电容：面积越大、间距越小、介电常数越大，电容越大parallel plates: bigger area, smaller gap and larger ε all raise C
并联：parallel: C = C₁ + C₂ + …相当于把极板面积拼起来like joining the plate areas together
串联：series: 1/C = 1/C₁ + 1/C₂ + …相当于把间距加厚；每个电容分到的电压不同like thickening the gap; the voltage splits
W = ½ Q V = ½ C V² = Q² / (2C)电容器储能；等于 V–Q 图线下的三角形面积energy stored; the area under a V–Q graph
ε₀ = 8.85 × 10⁻¹² F/m真空介电常数permittivity of free space
τ = R C放电时间常数，单位 s；约 5τ 后基本放完the RC time constant in seconds; roughly 5τ to empty
Q = Q₀ e^(−t / RC)放电曲线（也可写 V、I），指数衰减exponential decay for V or I as well

单元 11 · 磁与感应术语Unit 11 · Magnetism & induction terms

中文中文English中文中文English
磁场magnetic field磁感应强度magnetic flux density
磁通量magnetic flux磁感线magnetic field line
北极 / 南极north pole / south pole特斯拉tesla
载流导线current-carrying wire线圈 / 螺线管coil / solenoid
弗莱明左手定则Fleming's left-hand rule右手握拳定则right-hand grip rule
霍尔效应Hall effect霍尔电压Hall voltage
电磁感应electromagnetic induction感应电动势induced e.m.f.
法拉第定律Faraday's law楞次定律Lenz's law
磁通匝链数flux linkage发电机generator
变压器transformer初级 / 次级线圈primary / secondary coil
交流电alternating current有效值root-mean-square value
峰值peak value整流rectification

单元 11 · 磁与感应公式Unit 11 · Magnetism & induction formulas

公式Formula含义与用法Meaning and use
F = B I L载流直导线受力，要求 B 与 I 垂直force on a straight wire; B and I must be perpendicular
F = B I L sin θθ 是 B 与导线的夹角；平行时不受力θ between B and the wire; no force when parallel
F = B Q v单个运动电荷受力，方向也用左手定则force on a moving charge, again by the left-hand rule
B = F / (I L)磁感应强度的定义式，单位 Tdefinition of flux density, in tesla
r = m v / (B Q)垂直入射磁场时做圆周运动的半径（向心力 = 洛伦兹力）radius of the circular path when entering at 90°
V_H = B I / (n t q)霍尔电压，n 是载流子数密度，t 是薄片厚度Hall voltage; n is carrier density, t is thickness
Φ = B A磁通量，B 垂直于面时才成立，单位 Wbflux when B is normal to the area, in webers
Φ = B A cos θθ 是 B 与面的法线的夹角θ between B and the normal to the surface
N Φ （磁通匝链数）(flux linkage)N 匝线圈总的磁通，法拉第定律用它total flux through N turns — what Faraday's law uses
ε = − N ΔΦ / Δt法拉第定律；负号就是楞次定律Faraday's law; the minus sign is Lenz's law
楞次定律：感应电流总反抗引起它的变化Lenz's law: the induced current opposes the change causing it先判断"反抗什么"，再定方向decide what is being opposed, then fix the direction
导体切割磁感线：rod cutting flux: ε = B L v直导棒横切匀强磁场a straight rod moving across a uniform field
V有效r.m.s. = V₀ / √2正弦交流电的有效值，注意先用有效值算功率r.m.s. of a sine wave; use r.m.s. for power
P = V有效r.m.s.² / R不必写 ½，有效值本身就包含了平均效果no extra ½ — the r.m.s. value already handles the averaging
N_s / N_p = V_s / V_p理想变压器：匝数比 = 电压比ideal transformer: turns ratio = voltage ratio
V_p I_p = V_s I_s理想变压器输入输出功率相等（升压则降流）power in = power out; stepping up voltage steps down current

单元 12 · 量子物理术语Unit 12 · Quantum physics terms

中文中文English中文中文English
光电效应photoelectric effect光子photon
逸出功work function阈值频率threshold frequency
截止电压stopping potential光电子photoelectron
普朗克常数Planck constant量子化quantisation
能级energy level基态ground state
激发态excited state电离ionisation
发射谱 / 吸收谱emission / absorption spectrum谱线spectral line

单元 12 · 量子物理公式Unit 12 · Quantum physics formulas

公式Formula含义与用法Meaning and use
E = h f = h c / λ光子能量，频率越高能量越大photon energy rises with frequency
h = 6.63 × 10⁻³⁴ J s普朗克常数，整章的核心数字Planck constant — the key number of the whole unit
Φ = h f₀逸出功 = 阈值频率对应的光子能量work function at the threshold frequency
h f = Φ + ½ m v最大max²爱因斯坦光电方程，考试必写Einstein's photoelectric equation — learn it by heart
e V_s = h f − Φ截止电压版本，常用来画直线求 hthe stopping-potential form, used to find h from a straight-line graph
h f = E₂ − E₁跃迁放出一个光子，能量正好是两个能级之差a jump emits one photon of exactly the level difference
h f = 电离能（从该能级到无穷远）ionisation energy from that level基态电离能最大largest from the ground state
λ = h / p = h / (m v)德布罗意波长；动量越大波长越短de Broglie wavelength; more momentum means shorter λ
p = √(2 m E_k) ，故 λ = h / √(2 m E_k), so λ = h / √(2 m E_k)给的是动能不是速度时用它use this when kinetic energy is given rather than speed
e V = h c / λ最短min加速电压全部变成一个光子，得到最短波长all the accelerating voltage into one photon gives the minimum wavelength
1 eV = 1.60 × 10⁻¹⁹ J电子伏特换算，几乎每题都要用the electronvolt conversion, used in almost every question
E_k = e V （一个电子经 V 加速）(one electron accelerated through V)先换成焦耳再代公式convert to joules before substituting
强度只改变光电子数目，不改变最大动能intensity changes only the number of electrons, not their maximum energy经典波动说解释不了的现象，也是量子说的立足点the fact the wave model cannot explain — and where quantum ideas begin
