速查表收尾 · 三条使用建议Closing the cheat sheet · three tips

① 术语表用来"认脸"：看到中文能反应出英文即可，不必默写拼写。1. Use the glossary for recognition, not spelling — you need to know the English word when you meet the Chinese one.

② 公式表只背加粗的：其余的在考场上可以从加粗的那几条推出来。2. Learn only the bold formulas; the rest can be derived from them.

③ 单位表每天扫一眼：漏写单位和漏换算（mA、mm²）是最不划算的失分方式。3. Glance at the units table daily — a missing unit or a missed conversion (mA, mm²) is the cheapest way to lose marks.

外部学习资源 · 含实验课程（全部免费）External resources · including practical work (all free)

物理是实验科学，光看公式学不会 · 这一页按「实验 → 大纲真题 → 讲解练习 → 工具」分组，第一组最重要Physics is an experimental science — formulas alone will not do. Grouped as practical work → syllabus & papers → explanations → tools; the first group matters most

怎么搭配着用：第一组是实验，A-Level 物理有专门的实验/实操考查（做实验、处理数据、设计实验），本页「单元 6 · 实验技能」讲的就是它——配合下面的实验网站一起用，效果最好。平时：跟本预习站学一个单元 → 去第一组找一个同主题的虚拟实验动手调一调 → 学完去「二、官方大纲与真题」刷一组真题。先看画面，再记公式。 How to combine them: the first group is practical work. A-level physics examines practical skills separately (carrying out experiments, handling data, designing investigations) — that is what Unit 6 of this site covers, and it works best together with the experiment sites below. Routine: study one unit here → find a matching virtual experiment in group 1 and play with it → do a set of real questions from group 2. Picture first, formula second.
访问提示：下面大多是境外网站。PhET、oPhysics、PhysicsUK、Desmos、GeoGebra 一般能直接打开；IOPSpark、Physics & Maths Tutor 偶尔转圈，换浏览器或用手机热点再试。先问学校用的是哪个考试局（CIE 9702 / AQA / OCR / Edexcel）——本页链接以剑桥 CIE 9702 为主，其他考试局的实验要求大同小异。 Access note: most sites are hosted overseas. PhET, oPhysics, PhysicsUK, Desmos and GeoGebra usually open directly; IOPSpark and Physics & Maths Tutor sometimes hang — try another browser or a phone hotspot. Ask which exam board you are on (CIE 9702 / AQA / OCR / Edexcel). Links here lean towards Cambridge CIE 9702, but practical requirements are broadly similar across boards.

一、实验课程（这一组最重要 🧪）1. Practical / experiment resources (most important)

真做不了实验也别怕：下面既有「照着手册一步一步做」的真实实验，也有「在电脑上动手调」的虚拟实验No lab? No problem: below you will find both real experiments with full instructions and virtual ones you run in the browser

IOPSpark — Practical Physics （英国物理学会官方实验库）(Institute of Physics experiment collection) ↗
首选。英国物理学会（IoP）与 Nuffield 基金会的合作项目，几百个经过课堂验证的实验，每个都有：实验目的、器材清单、步骤、安全提示、给实验员的技术说明。按主题分好了：力与运动、电路与电场、波、光与光学、原子与核、能量、电磁学……还有专门的「Working scientifically」（设计实验、收集数据、处理数据）。老的 practicalphysics.org 已整体搬到这里。Start here. A collaboration between the Institute of Physics and the Nuffield Foundation: hundreds of classroom-tested experiments, each with aim, apparatus list, procedure, safety notes and technician guidance. Grouped by topic — forces and motion, electric circuits and fields, waves, light and optics, atoms and nuclei, energy, electromagnetism — plus a "Working scientifically" strand on designing experiments and handling data. The old practicalphysics.org has migrated here.
spark.iop.org/practical-physics
IOPSpark 总资源库（3000+ 免费资源）main resource library (3000+ free resources) ↗
除了实验，还有课堂活动、教学指导，以及专门面向 16–19 岁（就是 A-Level 阶段）的 Teaching Advanced Physics 板块。想找某个考点的教学材料，在这里搜关键词。Beyond experiments: classroom activities, teaching guidance, and the Teaching Advanced Physics strand aimed at 16–19 (i.e. A-level). Search a topic keyword to find materials.
spark.iop.org
PhysicsUK 互动实验与练习工具interactive tools & practice ↗
一套按 A-Level 考点做的虚拟实验：涟漪水槽（波的反射/折射/衍射/干涉）、电容充放电、简谐振动与阻尼共振、电场与电势、磁场中的带电粒子、电磁感应、变压器、光电效应、布朗运动……还有按专题的选择题训练。做完本页单元 3「波」和单元 4「电学」，来这里动手调一调最有感觉。A set of virtual experiments mapped to A-level topics: ripple tank (reflection, refraction, diffraction, interference), capacitor charge/discharge, SHM and resonance, electric fields and potential, charged particles in magnetic fields, electromagnetic induction, transformers, photoelectric effect, Brownian motion — plus topic-by-topic multiple-choice practice. Especially good after Unit 3 (waves) and Unit 4 (electricity) here.
physicsuk.co.uk/tools
PhET 互动仿真（科罗拉多大学）interactive simulations (Univ. of Colorado) ↗
免费开源，打开就玩，不用注册。重点推荐：Circuit Construction Kit（电路虚拟实验台，搭电路、测电流电压）、Wave Interference（波的干涉）、 Forces and Motion、Projectile Motion、Radioactive Dating。本页单元 4 电学用它特别合适。Free, open, no sign-up. Highlights: Circuit Construction Kit (build circuits, measure current and p.d.), Wave Interference, Forces and Motion, Projectile Motion, Radioactive Dating. Perfect alongside Unit 4 (electricity).
phet.colorado.edu
oPhysics 互动仿真（力学/波/电磁）interactive simulations (mechanics / waves / EM) ↗
页面朴素但都是能改参数的仿真：抛体、斜面、动量碰撞、简谐振动、驻波、电路。适合「把公式里的一个数改掉，看看图像怎么变」。Plain but fully parameterised simulations: projectiles, inclined planes, momentum collisions, SHM, standing waves, circuits. Ideal for "change one number in the formula and watch the graph move".
ophysics.com
Maths with Matt A-Level 物理仿真A-level physics simulations ↗
按 A-Level 主题做的一批仿真：轨道与卫星、单摆阻尼与共振、衍射光栅、光纤全反射、双缝干涉、波的叠加、驻波、串联并联电路。每页都有「试一试 / 观察什么」的提示。A-level themed simulations: orbits and satellites, pendulum damping and resonance, diffraction grating, optical fibres, double-slit interference, superposition, standing waves, series and parallel circuits. Each page suggests what to try and what to look for.
mathswithmatt.co.uk/physics-simulations
MarkScheme CIE 9702 按大纲点的讲解 + 仿真CIE 9702 notes + simulations by syllabus point ↗
按剑桥 9702 大纲的每一个点（如 10.1 实际电路）列出讲解，并配上可交互的仿真和「为什么值得看 / 试什么 / 观察什么」的引导。考 CIE 的话，这个站和自己学的进度能对得最准。Notes organised by each CIE 9702 syllabus point (e.g. 10.1 practical circuits), each paired with an interactive simulation plus guidance on what to try and what to look for. If you sit CIE, this maps most directly onto what you study.

三、讲解与练习3. Explanations & practice

看不懂就换个人讲一遍，很正常If it does not click, hear it from someone else

isaacscience.org/pages/physics_a_level.html
A Level Physics Online ↗
专门讲 A-Level 物理的视频站，按专题排好，讲得慢、写得很清楚，适合英文还没完全适应的时候听。Videos dedicated to A-level physics, arranged by topic, slow and clearly written — good while your English is still catching up.
alevelphysicsonline.com
Khan Academy — 物理Physics ↗
基础补课最好用：力学、波、电路、磁场都有短视频 + 即时练习，做错给提示。Best for filling gaps: mechanics, waves, circuits and magnetism with short videos and instant practice with hints.
khanacademy.org/science/physics
The Physics Classroom ↗
老牌物理教学站，文字讲解 + 动画 + 互动练习，讲得很细，适合把概念彻底弄懂。A long-running teaching site with text, animations and interactive practice. Very thorough — good for genuinely understanding a concept.
physicsclassroom.com

四、计算与作图工具4. Calculators & graphing tools

物理里画图和算直线斜率是家常便饭Graphs and straight-line gradients are everyday work in physics

Desmos 图形计算器Graphing calculator ↗
实验数据打进去能直接拟合直线、算斜率和截距——本页「单元 6 · 直线化求物理量」那站就用得上。Enter your experimental data and fit a straight line to get gradient and intercept — exactly what Unit 6's "linearising a relationship" stop needs.
desmos.com/calculator
GeoGebra 图形与几何Graphing & geometry ↗
能画函数图像、做几何作图、画向量和光路图。Graphs, geometry, vectors and ray diagrams.

五、A2（高二）· 六个单元的仿真5. A2 (Year 13) · simulations for the six units

A2 的概念更抽象，先看「动起来的画面」会省力很多 · 每个链接都对应本站的单元编号A2 ideas are more abstract — seeing them move first saves a lot of effort. Each link carries its unit number here

PhET — Gravity and Orbits （引力与轨道）(orbits) ↗
拖着太阳和地球改质量、改距离，看轨道怎么变、什么时候会被甩出去。配合本页单元 7（圆运动与引力）：把卫星调到不同高度，看它的速度变慢、周期变长。Drag the Sun and Earth to change their masses and separation and watch the orbit change, or break. Use with Unit 7: raise the satellite and watch the speed fall while the period grows.
phet.colorado.edu · gravity-and-orbits
PhET — My Solar System （我的太阳系）(build a system) ↗
自己搭一个双星或行星系统，调初始速度看轨道变成圆、椭圆还是发散。理解为什么同一高度只有一个速度能做圆周轨道。Build your own binary star or planetary system and vary the launch speed to see circles, ellipses or escape — why only one speed gives a circular orbit at a given radius.
phet.colorado.edu · my-solar-system
PhET — Pendulum Lab （单摆实验室）(pendulums) ↗
改摆长、质量、重力场（月球/木星），还能加摩擦看阻尼衰减。配合本页单元 8（简谐振动）：验证 T = 2π√(L/g)——摆球质量怎么改，周期都不动。Change length, mass and the planet it swings on, or add friction to watch damping. Use with Unit 8 to test T = 2π√(L/g) — the bob's mass changes nothing.
phet.colorado.edu · pendulum-lab
PhET — Wave on a String （绳上的波）(driven oscillations) ↗
选「手动/振荡/脉动」，调驱动频率和阻尼。配合本页单元 8 最后两站：把驱动频率慢慢往上拧，振幅会在某个频率突然炸开——那就是共振。Choose manual, oscillate or pulse and vary driving frequency and damping. Use with the last two stops of Unit 8: sweep the frequency and the amplitude suddenly explodes — resonance.
phet.colorado.edu · wave-on-a-string
PhET — Gas Properties （气体性质）(kinetic model) ↗
往容器里打气体分子，加热、压缩，看压强和温度计怎么动。配合本页单元 9（热物理）：看清温度 = 平均动能这件事——加热后分子变快，压强自然变大。Pump molecules into a box, heat it, compress it and watch pressure and temperature respond. Use with Unit 9 to see that temperature is mean kinetic energy: heating speeds the molecules up, so pressure rises.
phet.colorado.edu · gas-properties
PhET — Charges and Fields （电荷与电场）(electric field) ↗
摆几个电荷，实时显示电场方向、等势面和某点的电势值。配合本页单元 10（电场与电容）：直观看到等势面密集处电场强，以及沿等势面移动不做功。Place charges and see field directions, equipotentials and the potential at any point. Use with Unit 10 to see that crowded equipotentials mean a strong field, and that moving along one costs no work.
phet.colorado.edu · charges-and-fields
PhET — Capacitor Lab （电容器实验台）(capacitance) ↗
拖动极板间距、面积，插入电介质，读电容值随三个量怎么变。配合本页单元 10 后半（C = εA/d）。Drag plate separation and area, slide in a dielectric and read off how C responds to each. Use with the second half of Unit 10 (C = εA/d).
phet.colorado.edu · capacitor-lab
PhET — Faraday's Law （法拉第定律）(induction) ↗
推磁铁进线圈，看灯泡亮起来、电压表指针动。配合本页单元 11（磁与感应）：慢慢推不亮、快速推才亮，这就是磁通变化率；还能看楞次定律的反抗方向。Push a magnet into a coil and watch the bulb and the voltmeter needle. Use with Unit 11: slow pushes do nothing, fast ones light the bulb — that is the rate of change of flux, with Lenz's law shown by the direction of opposition.
phet.colorado.edu · faradays-law
PhET — Magnets and Electromagnets （磁铁与电磁铁）(electromagnets) ↗
看铁屑显示磁感线、通电线圈产生磁场，还能把线圈变成电磁铁。配合本页单元 11 第一站建立磁场图景。See field lines in iron filings, the field of a current loop, and turn a coil into an electromagnet. Use with the first stop of Unit 11 to build the picture of a magnetic field.
phet.colorado.edu · magnets-and-electromagnets
PhET — Photoelectric Effect （光电效应）(photons) ↗
换金属靶、调光的频率和强度，看电子出不出得来、出来多快。配合本页单元 12（量子物理）：把强度拧到最大也打不出电子时的那种挫败感，正是量子说的出发点。Change the target metal, and the frequency and intensity of the light, to see whether electrons emerge and how fast. Use with Unit 12: the frustration of cranking intensity with no electrons at all is exactly where quantum ideas start.
phet.colorado.edu · legacy/photoelectric-effect

六、A2（高二）· 讲不清楚就换个地方看6. A2 (Year 13) · other explanations

同一件事听三种讲法很正常，哪个让你懂了就用哪个Hearing a thing three ways is normal — keep whichever one clicks

Khan Academy — 圆周运动与引力（单元 7）Centripetal force & gravitation (Unit 7) ↗
短视频 + 即时练习，讲得慢、英文简单，适合单元 7 卡住时补基础。同站还有：振荡与波（单元 8）、热力学（单元 9）、磁场（单元 11）、量子物理（单元 12）。Short videos with instant practice, slow and plainly worded — good when Unit 7 stalls. The same site covers oscillations (Unit 8), thermodynamics (Unit 9), magnetic fields (Unit 11) and quantum physics (Unit 12).
khanacademy.org · centripetal-force-and-gravitation
Save My Exams — CIE A-Level 物理复习笔记CIE A-level Physics revision notes ↗
按剑桥 9702 大纲条整理的复习笔记，A2 六个专题（圆运动引力、振动、热物理、电场、电磁、量子）都有，每节后面配真题式小题。免费，不用注册。Notes matched to the Cambridge 9702 syllabus points, covering all six A2 topics — gravitation, oscillations, thermal, fields, electromagnetism and quantum — with exam-style questions each. Free, no sign-up.
savemyexams.com · a-level/physics/cie
A Level Physics Online — A2 专题视频A2 topic videos ↗
A2 的六个单元都有对应视频，老师在黑板上边写边讲，语速适中，把每一个公式的推导过一遍。听不懂讲得太快的版本时首选。Every A2 unit has its own video: the teacher writes each derivation on the board at a gentle pace — the first stop when a fast-paced explanation has lost you.
alevelphysicsonline.com
★ 没有实验室？在家也能做的 10 个实验No lab? 10 experiments you can do at home Home practicals家庭实验

器材都是家里能找到的。每个实验都按 A-Level 实验题的套路来做：改一个自变量 → 测因变量 → 重复取平均 → 画图 → 求斜率。做完顺手把数据、表格、图、结论写下来，就是一份完整的实验报告练习。All apparatus is stuff you have at home. Follow the A-level practical pattern each time: change one independent variable → measure the dependent one → repeat and average → plot a graph → find the gradient. Write up the data, table, graph and conclusion and you have a full report-writing exercise.

  1. 单摆测 g：绳子 + 小重物，改摆长 L，测 20 个周期求 T，画 T² 对 L 的图，斜率 = 4π²/g。（对应单元 1、6）Measuring g with a pendulum: string plus a small mass, vary length L, time 20 oscillations for T, plot T² against L — gradient = 4π²/g. (Units 1, 6)
  2. 弹簧的胡克定律：弹簧 + 尺子 + 一堆硬币，加砝码测伸长，画 F 对 x 的图，斜率 = k。（对应单元 2）Hooke's law with a spring: spring, ruler and coins; add load and measure extension; plot F against x — gradient = k. (Unit 2)
  3. 橡皮筋的滞回：加载和卸载各测一次，看看两条曲线不重合（弹性滞后）。Rubber-band hysteresis: measure loading and unloading; the two curves do not coincide (elastic hysteresis).
  4. 纸的承重：把纸折成不同形状（平板/瓦楞/圆筒），测能承受多少本书——材料与结构。（对应单元 2）How much load can paper take? Fold it flat, corrugated, or into cylinders and load with books — materials and structure. (Unit 2)
  5. 水面波：洗脸盆装水，用尺子敲水面制造波，观察反射、衍射（障碍物）、干涉（两个手指同时敲）。（对应单元 3）Waves on water: a basin of water; tap the surface with a ruler to see reflection, diffraction around an obstacle, and interference from two fingers. (Unit 3)
  6. 玻璃杯与筷子：看折射与「折断」的筷子；用手电筒看全反射。（对应单元 3 之后的几何光学）Glass and chopsticks: refraction and the "broken" chopstick; total internal reflection with a torch. (Geometrical optics after Unit 3)
  7. 电阻丝的长度：电池 + 灯泡 + 铅笔芯或电阻丝，改长度看亮度变化——电阻与长度成正比。（对应单元 4）Resistance and length: a cell, a bulb and a pencil lead or resistance wire — change the length and watch the brightness; R ∝ length. (Unit 4)
  8. 水果电池：柠檬 + 铜片 + 锌片，测电压，几个串起来看电压怎么变（内阻与串联）。（对应单元 4）Fruit battery: lemon with copper and zinc electrodes; measure the e.m.f. and connect several in series — internal resistance and series cells. (Unit 4)
  9. 手机测重力加速度：用手机里的加速度传感器 App（如 phyphox）做自由落体或电梯升降的测量。Phone as a sensor: use a sensor app such as phyphox to record free fall or a lift accelerating.
  10. 冷却曲线：一杯热水每分钟记一次温度，画 T 对 t 的图，看降温速率怎么随温差变化。Cooling curve: a cup of hot water, temperature every minute; plot T against t and watch how the rate of cooling depends on the temperature difference.
  11. 弹簧振子验证 T = 2π√(m/k)：弹簧下挂不同数量的硬币，先静止量伸长（求 k），再数 20 个完整振动求 T，画 T² 对 m 的图，斜率 = 4π²/k。（对应单元 8）Spring oscillator, testing T = 2π√(m/k): hang different numbers of coins, measure the static extension first (to get k), then time 20 oscillations; plot T² against m — gradient = 4π²/k. (Unit 8)
  12. 阻尼与共振：同一根绳子挂重物做摆，先让它自然衰减，记录每 10 个周期的振幅（阻尼）；再用节拍器 App 每隔固定时间轻推一下，慢慢改变推送节奏，会找到一个振幅最大的节奏——那就是共振。（对应单元 8）Damping and resonance: let a pendulum decay, recording amplitude every 10 oscillations (damping); then push it gently in time with a metronome app and sweep the tempo — the amplitude peaks at one tempo. That is resonance. (Unit 8)
  13. 测液体的比热容（近似）：保温杯里装已知质量的热水，倒入已知质量的冷水或牛奶，记下混合前后的温度，用 m₁cΔθ₁ = m₂cΔθ₂ 反推。要提醒自己哪些热量漏掉了。（对应单元 9）An approximate specific heat capacity: mix known masses of hot and cold liquids in a flask, record both temperatures, and use m₁cΔθ₁ = m₂cΔθ₂. List where heat escaped. (Unit 9)
  14. 电容器的充放电：一颗大电容（几块钱的电解电容）+ 电阻 + 电池 + 万用表，每 5 秒读一次电压，画 U 对 t 的图，能看到漂亮的指数衰减曲线，还能估出 τ = RC。（对应单元 10）Charging and discharging a capacitor: a large electrolytic capacitor, a resistor, a cell and a multimeter; read the voltage every 5 s and plot V against t to see the exponential decay, estimating τ = RC. (Unit 10)
  15. 线圈 + 磁铁发电：几十圈漆包线绕成线圈，接一个 LED（或耳机/万用表），磁铁快速插入时 LED 闪一下，慢插几乎不亮——直观版本的法拉第定律。（对应单元 11）Make electricity with a coil: wind a few dozen turns of enamelled wire, connect an LED (or earphones or a multimeter); a fast magnet makes it flash, a slow one barely does — Faraday's law made visible. (Unit 11)
  16. LED 里的「量子」：红、黄、绿、蓝 LED 各一颗，用几节电池逐个点亮，比较点亮它们所需的电压高低——颜色越靠蓝紫，需要的电压越高，因为 E = hf。（对应单元 12）The quantum in an LED: light up red, yellow, green and blue LEDs from a battery pack and compare the voltage each needs to start glowing — bluer LEDs need more, because E = hf. (Unit 12)

进阶：phyphox（德国亚琛工大做的免费手机物理实验 App）能把你手机里的加速度、陀螺仪、麦克风变成测量仪器，做声速、单摆、磁场的实验，官网 phyphox.org。Next level: phyphox, a free app from RWTH Aachen that turns your phone's accelerometer, gyroscope and microphone into measuring instruments for sound speed, pendulum and magnetic-field experiments — see phyphox.org.

物理不是背出来的，是做出来的 🎉Physics is not memorised — it is done 🎉

今天就挑一个：打开 IOPSpark Practical Physics 找一个实验读一遍，或者打开 PhET 的电路实验台搭一个电路。动手那几分钟，比多看三页笔记有用。Pick just one thing today: read through an experiment on IOPSpark Practical Physics, or build a circuit in PhET's Circuit Construction Kit. A few minutes of hands-on beats three more pages of notes.

今天不用全记住，看懂了就是赢You don't need to memorise it all today — understanding it is the win

物理和数学不一样：先有画面，再有公式。每一站的配图和「说人话」是主线，公式只是给画面配的数字。Physics is not like maths: the picture comes first, the formula second. The diagrams and the "in plain words" boxes carry the ideas; the formulas just put numbers on them.
